Existing methods either rely on complex cross-modal interactions that lead to low retrieval efficiency, or depend on large-scale vision-language adaptation, which requires massive data and computational resources. To address these issues, we develop a fast-then-fine (FTF) retrieval framework centered on interaction-free multi-granular recall and parameter-free text-guided cross-modal reranking with the Balanced Text-guided Interaction Block (BTIB). Specifically, interaction-free multi-granular recall pre-computes gallery embeddings and matches them with the corresponding query embedding without full-gallery token-level text-image interaction, while text-guided cross-modal reranking with BTIB refines local semantic correspondence within the top-K candidate set. Furthermore, an inter- and intra-modal loss is designed to jointly optimize cross-modal alignment across multi-granular representations. Extensive experiments on public benchmarks demonstrate that the FTF achieves competitive retrieval accuracy while significantly improving retrieval efficiency compared with existing methods.

</details>


### [36] [OmniGlimpse: Asymmetric Identity Memory for Online Multi-View Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36785)
*Rangya Zhang,Jiaping Xiao,Yujing Shang,Yuhang Zhang,Mingsheng Li,Mir Feroskhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出在线多视图身份维护（OMIM）问题，并给出 OmniGlimpse 非对称“提议-验证”框架，在无需离线场景训练与相机标定的条件下实现多视图目标身份关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视图跟踪可扩展视觉覆盖，但如何在不依赖离线场景特定训练和相机标定的情况下，将不同视角的观测匹配到正确身份仍具挑战。现有离线公式依赖部署相关知识，而在线自适应又会放大关联错误。

Method: 将问题形式化为 OMIM：目标在每个视角仅初始化一次，之后不再提供身份标签。OmniGlimpse 采用非对称 propose-verify 结构，分离自适应关联与稳定身份巩固：在表示层，短时局部与跨视图证据映射到目标特定自适应空间以提出关联，同时由冻结编码器定义的标准空间中维护长期身份分布用于验证；在更新层，短时证据仅指导当前关联，bootstrapping 后只有经验证的观测才能更新持久记忆或驱动自适应。

Result: 在统一的“指定目标”评估协议下，OmniGlimpse 在 M3Track 上相较最佳在线基线各项指标分别提升 9.1 IDF1 与 22.1 CVIDF1；在 MvMHAT 上对应提升 20.3 与 26.2。尽管未使用离线场景特定训练，其 IDF1 超过离线训练参照方法，跨视图关联亦具竞争力。

Conclusion: 结果表明，该在线身份维护方法能够在适应外观变化的同时，将可靠证据巩固为稳定身份锚点，为无标定、无场景特定训练的多视图在线跟踪提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Multi-view tracking extends visual coverage beyond a single camera, providing complementary observations when targets are occluded or leave the view. This raises a question: how can observations from different views be matched to the correct identities without offline scene-specific training or camera calibration? Progress toward this goal remains limited because many offline formulations assume such deployment-specific knowledge, while online adaptation can amplify association errors. We study this problem as online multi-view identity maintenance (OMIM): targets are initialized once per view, with no further identity labels provided after each initialization. To this end, we introduce OmniGlimpse, an asymmetric propose-verify framework that separates adaptive association from stable identity consolidation. At the representation level, short-term local and cross-view evidence is mapped into target-specific adaptive spaces to propose associations, whereas long-term identity distributions are maintained for verification in a canonical space defined by a frozen encoder. At the update level, short-term evidence may guide current associations, whereas, after bootstrapping, only verified observations may update persistent memory or drive adaptation. Under a unified target-specified evaluation protocol, OmniGlimpse improves over the best evaluated online baseline for each metric by 9.1 IDF1 and 22.1 CVIDF1 points on M3Track. The corresponding gains on MvMHAT are 20.3 and 26.2 points. Despite using no offline scene-specific training, it outperforms offline-trained references in IDF1 and remains competitive in cross-view association. These results support an approach to online identity maintenance that adapts to changing appearances while consolidating reliable evidence into stable identity anchors.

</details>


### [37] [Visual Autoregressive Generation with Next Token Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36793)
*Ruihang Jiang,Zongyao Zhao,Zhaolin Wang,Yuanwei Liu,Xiatian Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Next Token Imagination (NTI)，让视觉自回归模型在受约束的条件下"想象"超越GT下一token，从而覆盖更广的视觉分布，在ImageNet上显著提升生成保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉内容空间近乎无限，任何训练集只能覆盖极小部分。现有生成模型（如视觉自回归模型）仅用GT前序token预测GT下一token，学习被限制在观测数据内，难以充分刻画视觉分布。

Method: 以视觉自回归生成为概念验证（其固定离散token空间便于引导与约束"想象"），提出NTI：允许模型在GT下一token之外进行探索，但通过精心设计、将探索约束在真实视觉分布内的准则来保证有界性。

Result: 在ImageNet上的大量实验表明，NTI持续且显著提升生成图像保真度，以更小模型和更低训练成本逼近SOTA扩散模型与AR模型。

Conclusion: 让模型在合理约束下"想象"训练数据之外的视觉实现，可突破仅拟合观测数据的局限，是提升视觉生成质量的有效途径。

Abstract: The space of possible visual content is enormous, and arguably unbounded. Any training set, however large, therefore covers only a tiny fraction of it. Yet generative models are typically trained to fit only the images they are given. In visual autoregressive (AR) generation, for example, each token is predicted from the preceding ground-truth (GT) tokens and supervised solely by the GT next token. Confining learning to observed data in this way limits how much of the visual distribution a model can capture. In this work, we hypothesize that letting a model imagine beyond its training data, by exploring plausible visual realizations it has never observed, can ease this limitation. We use visual AR generation as a proof of concept, since its fixed, discrete token space makes imagination easier to steer and constrain. We propose Next Token Imagination (NTI), a method that allows the model to explore beyond the GT next token, subject to a carefully designed criterion that keeps this imagination bounded by the underlying visual distribution. Extensive experiments on ImageNet show that NTI substantially and consistently improves the fidelity of generated images, approaching state-of-the-art diffusion and AR models while using a smaller model and lower training cost.

</details>


### [38] [uTHCD-C: A Corruption-Robustness Benchmark for Tamil Handwritten Character Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36850)
*Preethi S,Meeradevi T,Arun Prakash A*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 uTHCD-C——首个针对孤立泰米尔手写字符识别（HCR）的标准化多重损坏鲁棒性基准，含10种损坏类型（含手写特定笔画细化）与5级严重度，评估9种CNN/Transformer架构并给出鲁棒性与算力权衡的对比基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有泰米尔手写字符识别评测多集中于干净测试图像，缺乏标准化、面向多重损坏的鲁棒性基准，导致不同架构在非理想条件下的可靠性无法横向比较。

Method: 基于 uTHCD 数据集构建基准：定义10种损坏类型（其中一种为手写特有的笔画细化损坏），每类5个严重度等级；采用五折分层交叉验证与书写者无关（writer-independent）测试；评估9种卷积与Transformer架构；以 VGG16-BN 为参照归一化计算平均损坏误差 mCE；并对比参数量、计算复杂度与推理吞吐。

Result: ConvNeXt 与 Swin 的 mCE 最低（0.75、0.83），ShuffleNet 与 MnasNet 最高（1.55、1.85），表明架构间鲁棒性差异显著；效率测量进一步刻画了鲁棒性与计算需求之间的权衡。

Conclusion: uTHCD-C 通过标准化损坏配置、评测协议与对比结果，为鲁棒且计算高效的泰米尔手写字符识别研究提供了可复现的基础。

Abstract: Evaluation of Tamil handwritten character recognition (HCR) has largely focused on clean test images, and a standardized multi-corruption robustness benchmark for isolated Tamil HCR has not been established. We introduce uTHCD-C, a corruption-robustness benchmark based on the unconstrained Tamil Handwritten Character Database (uTHCD). The benchmark defines ten corruption types, including a handwriting-specific stroke-thinning corruption, at five severity levels. We evaluate nine convolutional and transformer-based architectures using five-fold stratified cross-validation and writer-independent testing. Robustness is quantified using mean Corruption Error (mCE), normalized against a VGG16-BN reference. We additionally compare parameter count, computational complexity, and inference throughput to characterize the computational requirements of the evaluated architectures. The results establish comparative robustness baselines for Tamil HCR, revealing substantial differences across architectures: ConvNeXt and Swin achieve the lowest mCE values (0.75 and 0.83, respectively), whereas ShuffleNet and MnasNet exhibit higher values (1.55 and 1.85, respectively). The accompanying efficiency measurements characterize the trade-offs between corruption robustness and computational requirements. By providing standardized corruption configurations, evaluation protocols, and comparative results, uTHCD-C establishes a reproducible basis for future research on robust and computationally efficient Tamil HCR.

</details>


### [39] [LingGS: Decoupling Feed-Forward Geometry Prediction from Camera Tracking for High-Fidelity Gaussian SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36853)
*Da Li,Yihan Zang,Ivan Viola,Zeyu Wang,Dominik Engel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种单目RGB高斯泼溅SLAM在线系统：以冻结的流式3D基础模型充当虚拟RGB-D传感器提供深度，再用显式几何求解器经特征重投影估计相机运动，最后由流式高斯建图器融合观测；在有限优化预算下实现高质量重建与响应式跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目RGB高斯泼溅SLAM在有限优化预算下难以同时兼顾高质量重建与响应灵敏的相机跟踪；且学习式相机预测的跟踪精度不足。

Method: 1）冻结的流式3D基础模型作为虚拟RGB-D传感器，从单目视频预测深度；2）显式几何求解器通过特征重投影估计相机位姿，替代学习式位姿预测；3）流式高斯建图器将观测增量式整合为稠密场景表示。并验证该几何跟踪可在多种代表性流式几何骨干上提升位姿精度。

Result: 在室内与户外数据集上重建质量显著优于现有方法；单张RTX 4090上约7 FPS，吞吐量为所评估的已有SLAM系统的2–3倍。

Conclusion: 将学习到的几何先验与显式几何相机跟踪相结合，是提升单目高斯泼溅SLAM跟踪精度与效率的有效途径，且几何跟踪方案对多种流式几何骨干具有普适性。

Abstract: Achieving high-quality reconstruction and responsive camera tracking remains challenging for monocular RGB Gaussian splatting SLAM under a limited optimization budget. We present an online system that addresses this challenge by combining learned geometric priors with explicit geometric camera tracking, which can substantially outperform learned camera prediction in terms of tracking accuracy. A frozen streaming 3D foundation model serves as a virtual RGB-D sensor, providing depth estimation from monocular video. An explicit geometric solver then estimates camera motion through feature reprojection, and a streaming Gaussian mapper integrates the resulting observations into a dense scene representation. We further show that geometric tracking improves camera pose accuracy across representative streaming geometry backbones, demonstrating the broader applicability of our approach. Extensive experiments on indoor and outdoor datasets show that our method significantly improves reconstruction quality over existing approaches, achieving about 7 FPS on a single RTX 4090 and delivering 2-3 times the throughput of the evaluated prior SLAM systems.

</details>


### [40] [RangeRectQuant: Compressing Scientific Fields for Every Future Rectangular Sum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36873)
*Jiahao Zhang,Xinling Wen,Yu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 RangeRectQuant 算法，通过协调像素误差，在保证任意轴对齐矩形求和（前缀和）误差预算的前提下对科学栅格进行有损量化压缩，实现超出独立像素误差方案的存储节省，且支持数据生产者一次编码、用户无需源数据即可测量任意矩形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学栅格常在待测区域未知时就已存储。若采用独立像素误差界，要让一个绝对误差预算覆盖所有未来矩形，必须做精确整数重建（被迫无损），存储开销大。希望允许每个像素改变，同时保证任意矩形和的精度。

Method: 利用四个前缀和可确定任意矩形总和的性质，让算法在保持像素值范围与指定精确样本的同时控制前缀和误差。在每一行位置上精确刻画使剩余像素满足约束的可行误差，证明其构成单一整数区间（即使存在多种补全）；用反向传播求区间端点，正向传播据此选择量化像素。计算为线性复杂度，空间仅需一行。

Result: 在按地理留出的建成地表与人造水面分布图上实验，相比满足相同要求的替代方案取得显著的存储节省。

Conclusion: 数据生产者只需编码一次，后续用户即可测量任意矩形而无需源数据、重新编码或查询相关校正；算法以线性时间与单行空间精确满足所有轴对齐矩形的误差预算。

Abstract: Scientific rasters are often stored before the regions to be measured are known. Independent pixel-error bounds can force exact integer reconstruction when one absolute error budget covers every future rectangle. Coordinated changes allow every pixel to change while every rectangular sum remains accurate. A prefix sum accumulates values from the top-left corner. Four such sums determine any rectangular total. RangeRectQuant preserves the pixel range and designated exact samples while controlling errors in prefix sums. At each row position, we characterize exactly which errors let the remaining pixels meet these constraints. These errors form a single integer interval, even when many completions exist. A backward pass finds its endpoints; a forward pass uses them to choose quantized pixels. The resulting field meets the prescribed error budget for every axis-aligned rectangle, with linear work and workspace proportional to one row. Experiments on geographically held-out built-surface and water-occurrence maps show substantial storage savings over alternatives meeting the same requirements. Data producers encode once; later users measure arbitrary rectangles without the source, re-encoding, or query-specific correction.

</details>


### [41] [HoloRecon: Incremental Reconstruction of Long-Range Panoramas with Global Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36876)
*Zhuoxiao Li,Xinyi Liu,Hongye Hou,Jing OU,Kang Du,Shuai Zhang,Dongli Wu,Taoyu Wu,Siyuan Feng,Xiaohui Zhu,Wufan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出全景重建框架，将ORB稀疏全景SLAM前端与微调的前馈模型耦合，用预测局部几何对远距离回访的特征匹配做3D验证以生成长程约束，并结合PanoRecon基准取得最低轨迹误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长序列全景重建需要详细场景几何与跨远距离回访的全局一致相机位姿。全景SLAM与增量式SfM依赖特征匹配，但重复相似结构和小部分重叠导致长程可靠连接难建立，漂移无法消除；前馈模型局部几何精细但算力受限、推理窗口小，需额外全局约束。

Method: 框架前端为基于ORB的稀疏全景建图，后端耦合微调的前馈重建模型；用稀疏地标标定预测深度，并利用预测局部几何对跨回访特征匹配进行3D验证，从而在区域间也生成长程相机约束；据验证后的连接优化全局位姿图，再用校正位姿更新稠密几何。

Result: 在自建的PanoRecon七场景基准（含重复建筑结构、大小回环及参考相机位姿与LiDAR几何，用于评估全局一致性）上，方法取得所比较方法中最低的平均绝对轨迹误差(ATE)和较高的F-1 Score。

Conclusion: 将连续特征跟踪与稀疏建图同前馈模型的快速高质量局部重建相结合，可有效解决长序列全景重建的漂移与全局一致性问题，实现精确定位与全局一致的稠密建图。

Abstract: Long-sequence panoramic reconstruction requires detailed scene geometry and globally consistent camera poses across distant revisits. Panoramic SLAM and incremental SfM rely on feature correspondences, but repeated, similar structures and small partial overlap make reliable long-range connections difficult to establish, leaving accumulated drift unresolved. Feed-forward models offer detailed local geometry, but their high computational costs limit inference window size, necessitating additional constraints for global consistency. This motivates combining continuous feature-based tracking and sparse mapping with rapid, high-quality local reconstruction from feed-forward models to achieve accurate localization and globally consistent dense mapping over long sequences. We therefore propose a panoramic reconstruction framework that couples an ORB-based sparse panoramic mapping frontend with a fine-tuned feed-forward model. Our key insight is that predicted local geometry enables 3D verification of feature matches across distant revisits, yielding long-range camera constraints even between regions. We calibrate predicted depths with sparse landmarks and use verified connections to optimize the global pose graph, updating dense geometry with the corrected camera poses. We also introduce PanoRecon, a seven-scene benchmark with repetitive architecture, large and small loops, and reference camera poses and LiDAR geometry for evaluating global consistency. Our method achieves the lowest mean absolute trajectory error and high F-1 Score among the evaluated methods. Project Page:https://zhuoxiaoli.github.io/HoloRecon_page/

</details>


### [42] [Explicit Regional Representations For Video Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36978)
*Fuyi Yang,Lei Yang,Andrew Liu,Chenyu Wei,Xiaoyi Guo,Ming Kai Hsu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出像素级区域表示——质量相关性热图（QRH），结合局部退化证据与视语言模型引导下的感知先验，无需像素级标注；通过分布保持采样与质量感知采样两种确定性片段采样策略将QRH接入现有VQA模型，实验表明后者在多个基准上提升多数指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端VQA方法对其预测所依据的区域性证据基本是隐式的，缺乏可解释、可复用的区域级表征接口。

Method: 构建显式像素级区域表示QRH，融合局部退化证据与感知先验，由视觉-语言模型引导生成且无需像素级真值标注；将其应用于已有VQA模型，采用两种确定性片段采样策略：分布保持采样（无需重训练即近似原采样器的QRH分数分布）与质量感知采样（优先高QRH分数区域并结合微调）。

Result: 在DOVER与多个基准上的实验显示：分布保持采样能紧密近似随机采样的平均预测；质量感知采样在主流VQA基准上提升大多数评价指标。

Conclusion: 显式区域表示可作为下游VQA的有效接口，兼顾预测保真与性能提升。

Abstract: End-to-end video quality assessment (VQA) methods leave the regional evidence underlying their predictions largely implicit. We introduce an explicit pixel-level regional representation for VQA, instantiated as a Quality-Relevance Heatmap (QRH) that combines localized degradation evidence with perceptual priors under vision-language model guidance, without requiring pixel-level annotations. We apply QRH to existing VQA models through two deterministic fragment-sampling strategies: distribution-preserving sampling approximates the original sampler's QRH-score distribution without retraining, while quality-aware sampling prioritizes regions with high QRH scores and is paired with fine-tuning. Experiments with DOVER on multiple benchmarks show that the former closely approximates predictions averaged over random samples, while the latter improves most evaluated metrics across popular VQA benchmarks. These results support explicit regional representations as a useful interface for downstream VQA.

</details>


### [43] [LearnPrunerV2: Learnable Progressive Token Pruning for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36986)
*Rinyoichi Takezoe,Yaqian Li,Weiren Wang,Zihao Bo,Huihong Shi,Anzhou Hou,Mo Guang,Jiale Yan,Kaiwen Long*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LearnPrunerV2 将两阶段启发式视觉 token 剪枝框架升级为全可学习架构：在每层插入轻量可学习模块，融合内在视觉显著性与通过 shadow token 捕获的查询相关性来估计 token 重要性，并提出预算确定的逐层剪枝调度（BDP），在 LLaVA-v1.5 上剪掉 77.8% 视觉 token 仍保留 99.8% 原始性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）因视觉 token 数量庞大而计算开销过高，token 剪枝是有效方向，但现有方法存在两点缺陷：1）剪枝准则不可靠——注意力内在偏置或与查询无关的设计导致难以准确定位任务相关区域；2）剪枝策略次优——采用刚性、非自适应的调度，忽略 token 冗余随模型深度动态演变的事实。

Method: 将此前两阶段启发式框架 LearnPruner 扩展为完全可学习架构：在 VLM 主干每一层集成轻量可学习模块，通过融合内在视觉显著性（intrinsic visual saliency）与借助 shadow token 捕获的外在查询相关性（extrinsic query relevance）来估计 token 重要性；同时发现跨层一致的"阶梯式冗余模式"（staircase redundancy pattern），据此提出预算确定的剪枝（Budget-Deterministic Pruning, BDP），为每层设定固定预算，形成硬件友好的剪枝调度。

Result: 在多种基准与多种 VLM 上，LearnPrunerV2 一致优于先前最先进方法；在 LLaVA-v1.5 上剪除 77.8% 视觉 token 时仍保持 99.8% 的原始性能，实现更优的精度—效率权衡。

Conclusion: 可学习的 token 重要性估计结合按层固定预算的调度，能同时解决剪枝准则不可靠与调度不自适应的问题，在保持近乎无损精度的前提下大幅压缩视觉 token，为 VLM 推理加速提供了有效的精度—效率折中方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated remarkable multimodal capabilities but suffer from prohibitive computational overhead due to the massive number of visual tokens. While token pruning is a promising solution, existing methods still suffer from two major drawbacks: 1) unreliable pruning criteria that fail to accurately localize task-specific regions due to inherent attention biases or query-agnostic designs, and 2) suboptimal pruning strategies that employ rigid, non-adaptive schedules, overlooking the dynamic evolution of token redundancy across different model depths. To address these challenges, we propose LearnPrunerV2, which extends the previous two-stage heuristic framework (LearnPruner) into a fully learnable architecture. Specifically, we integrate lightweight learnable modules into every layer of the VLM backbone, which estimate token importance by fusing intrinsic visual saliency with extrinsic query relevance captured via a shadow token. By uncovering a consistent staircase redundancy pattern across VLM layers, we further introduce Budget-Deterministic Pruning (BDP) to establish a hardware-friendly schedule with fixed per-layer budgets. Extensive experimental results across various benchmarks and VLMs demonstrate that LearnPrunerV2 consistently outperform previous state-of-the-art methods. Notably, LearnPrunerV2 preserves 99.8% of the original performance when pruning 77.8% of visual tokens on LLaVA-v1.5, achieving a superior accuracy-efficiency trade-off.

</details>


### [44] [SplatScout: Query-Driven Part-Level Referring Segmentation in Gaussian Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37098)
*Yihan Zang,Da Li,Shinkyu Park,Ivan Viola,Dominik Engel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SplatScout——一种查询驱动、闭环的高斯场景指代分割方法，让VLM主动选视角、指导分割并检视渲染结果以迭代修正；同时构建部件级基准PartRef-ScanNet++，取得0.538 mIoU并优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高斯场景指代分割主要针对整体物体，依赖分割器生成的固定候选集，再由VLM从中选择；这些预定义候选可能遗漏作为目标或判别线索所需的细粒度部件。

Method: 将感知置于VLM控制之下的闭环框架：模型主动选择观察视角、直接指导分割、并检视渲染后的3D结果来精化预测；同时构建PartRef-ScanNet++部件级基准（12个ScanNet++场景、261条人工校验查询、由网格导出真值）。

Result: 在PartRef-ScanNet++上达到0.538 mIoU，显著优于现有方法；消融实验证明迭代精化的收益，不同VLM骨干的对比凸显工具反馈有效利用的重要性；在四个现有指代基准上也取得更高分数。

Conclusion: 以查询驱动、闭环的VLM控制式感知替代固定候选选择，可有效支持细粒度部件级指代分割，并具备跨基准的泛化优势。

Abstract: Existing referring segmentation methods on Gaussian scenes primarily target whole objects, relying on segmenter-generated candidates from which a vision-language model (VLM) selects. Such predetermined fixed candidates may omit fine-grained parts needed as targets or cues for identifying objects. We introduce SplatScout, a query-driven, closed-loop approach that places perception under VLM control. The model actively selects views, directs segmentation, and inspects rendered 3D results to refine its predictions. For part-level evaluation, we introduce PartRef-ScanNet++, comprising 261 manually verified queries across 12 ScanNet++ scenes with mesh-derived ground truth. On PartRef-ScanNet++, SplatScout achieves 0.538 mIoU, substantially outperforming existing methods. Ablations demonstrate the benefits of iterative refinement, while comparisons across VLM backbones highlight the importance of effective tool-feedback utilization. SplatScout also achieves higher scores on four existing referring benchmarks. Code and the PartRef-ScanNet++ benchmark are available at https://github.com/kaust-violab/SplatScout.

</details>


### [45] [MRI-to-CT Translation of Spinal Images via Anatomy-Guided Pseudo-Pairing and Adversarial Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37134)
*Eduardo Nieto,Ibai Genua,Davide Scorza,Karen López-Linares*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出三阶段框架（解剖引导伪配对+对抗扩散+3D精修），实现脊柱MRI→CT合成，3D椎体Dice从0.666提升至0.739。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MRI常用于脊柱疾病诊断，但手术规划需CT以更清晰显示椎体骨解剖；由MRI合成CT可避免额外辐射暴露并简化术前评估。

Method: 三阶段框架：①解剖引导的椎体匹配系统，利用脊柱分割识别相似的矢状位MRI与CT切片，经刚性配准对齐，从未配对扫描生成伪配对数据；②用这些配对训练对抗扩散模型完成MRI→CT转换；③将合成切片拼装为3D体数据，并用网络精修以改善层间连续性与结构一致性。训练使用逾10,000张未配对矢状位MRI与CT切片。

Result: 在来自83个MRI体数据的1,911张切片（覆盖全部脊柱区域）上评估，结合分布度量与椎体分割指标；相比单纯扩散，二值3D椎体Dice由0.666提升至0.739；体数据后处理减少了堆叠切片间可见的不连续，并改善轴位与冠状位椎体一致性。

Conclusion: 解剖引导伪配对、对抗扩散与3D精修相结合可提升脊柱MRI→CT合成质量：椎体匹配主要增强解剖对齐与保真度，体数据精修改善3D一致性；支持进一步发展MRI衍生类CT可视化用于术前规划，尤其需保留细粒度强度细节并维持体数据一致性。

Abstract: Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) is routinely used to diagnose spinal disorders, but surgical planning often requires Computed Tomography (CT) for better visualization of vertebral bone anatomy. Synthesizing CT from MRI could avoid additional radiation exposure and streamline preoperative assessment.
  We propose a three-stage framework for spine MRI-to-CT synthesis. First, an anatomy-guided vertebral matching system identifies similar sagittal MRI and CT slices using spine segmentations and aligns them through rigid registration, creating pseudo-paired data from unpaired scans. Second, these pairs train an adversarial diffusion model for MRI-to-CT translation. Third, synthesized slices are assembled into 3D volumes and refined by a network that improves inter-slice continuity and structural coherence.
  The framework was trained on over 10,000 unpaired sagittal MRI and CT slices and evaluated on 1,911 slices from 83 MRI volumes spanning all spinal regions. Assessment combined distribution-based measures with vertebral segmentation metrics. Compared with diffusion alone, the proposed approach increased binary 3D vertebral Dice from 0.666 to 0.739. Volumetric post-processing reduced visible discontinuities between stacked slices and improved vertebral coherence in axial and coronal views.
  Combining anatomy-guided pseudo-pairing, adversarial diffusion and 3D refinement improves spine MRI-to-CT synthesis. Vertebral matching primarily enhanced anatomical alignment and fidelity, while volumetric refinement improved 3D consistency. These findings support further development of MRI-derived CT-like visualization for preoperative planning, particularly to preserve fine intensity details while maintaining volumetric coherence.

</details>


### [46] [Physics Guided Diffusion Model Based Exposure Interpolation for High Dynamic Range Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37135)
*Zhengguo Li,Chaobing Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种物理引导的扩散模型曝光插值算法，用于解决双大曝光比（LER）单帧HDR成像中的亮度顺序反转（BOR）伪影问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多帧HDR存在鬼影问题；用单次拍摄同时获取两幅大曝光比SDR图像可避免鬼影，但直接融合会产生亮度顺序反转（BOR）伪影，因此需要在两幅LER图像的曝光时间之间插值出一幅中间曝光图像。相比CNN类神经增强插值方法，需更好地生成高频信息。

Method: 构建物理引导的扩散模型曝光插值框架：由物理驱动分量插值出初始图像，再由新的扩散模型分量生成残余高频细节；该扩散模型基于生成模型的相关性分析设计，采用新的预测目标以降低噪声数据与目标之间皮尔逊相关性的波动，并抑制拟合误差的放大因子。

Result: 实验结果表明该曝光插值算法有效；相较于基于卷积神经网络的现有神经增强曝光插值算法，在生成更多高频信息方面具有固有优势。

Conclusion: 物理驱动初值+扩散模型高频残差的组合可有效缓解BOR伪影，为单帧LER HDR成像提供可行的曝光插值方案。

Abstract: Ghosting artifacts are an open problem for popular multi-shot based high dynamic range (HDR) imaging. An effective solution is to capture two large-exposure-ratio (LER) standard dynamic range (SDR) images simultaneously via a single-shot. Brightness order reversal (BOR) artifacts are generated when the two LER images are directly fused together. It is crucial to interpolate an image with an exposure time in between the exposure times of two LER images. In this paper, a novel physics-guided diffusion model based exposure interpolation algorithm is proposed to address the BOR artifacts. An initial image is interpolated by a physics-driven component, and the residual high-frequency details are generated by a new diffusion model based component. The new diffusion model is based on the correlation analysis of generative models and includes a new predicted target to reduce the variation of the Pearson correlation between the noisy data and the target, as well as the amplification factor of the fitting error. Compared with existing neural augmented exposure interpolation algorithms on top of convolution neural networks, the proposed exposure interpolation algorithm has an inherent advantage to generate more high frequency information. Experiment results validate the proposed exposure interpolation algorithm.

</details>


### [47] [GradCO: Behavior-Aware Gradient-Conflict Offloading for Text-Based Person Anomaly Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37267)
*Zhiheng Fu,Zixu Li,Zhiwei Chen,Jiale Huang,Fangxu Liu,Jinhe Bi,Yupeng Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 GradCO（行为感知梯度冲突卸载）框架用于文本行人异常检索（TPAS），通过 B-MoE、COL、HIA 三个组件在保留身份线索的同时区分细粒度行为，在五个 TPAS/TIPR 基准上显著超越 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TPAS 需在保留身份线索的同时区分细粒度行为，存在两大挑战：同身份不同行为的困难负样本在细粒度对齐时引发梯度冲突；强外观线索在聚合阶段会掩盖弱行为证据。

Method: GradCO 包含三部分：(1) Behavior-Aware Mixture-of-Experts (B-MoE)，构建外观、正常行为、异常行为三路专家流，其角色由检索目标端到端学习，无需专家级语义标签；(2) Conflict Offload Learning (COL)，在相似度计算中引入对条件化的卸载锚点，减轻主文本表示上的冲突梯度负担；(3) Hierarchical Intent Alignment (HIA)，以查询条件化权重聚合专家相似度与特征。

Result: 在五个 TPAS/TIPR 基准上，GradCO 显著优于现有 SOTA 方法，验证了各设计组件的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将外观与行为表征分流、卸载冲突梯度并分层聚合，GradCO 有效缓解了 TPAS 中的梯度冲突与行为证据被掩盖问题，为该任务提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Text-based Person Anomaly Search (TPAS) retrieves a target pedestrian using natural-language descriptions of appearance and normal or anomalous behavior. Unlike conventional text-based person retrieval, TPAS must preserve identity cues while distinguishing fine-grained behaviors. It presents two challenges: same-identity, different-behavior hard negatives cause gradient conflict during granular alignment, while strong appearance cues can obscure weak behavioral evidence during aggregation. We propose GradCO (behavior-aware Gradient-Conflict Offloading), comprising three complementary components. Behavior-Aware Mixture-of-Experts (B-MoE) forms appearance, normal-behavior, and anomalous-behavior streams whose roles are learned from retrieval objectives without expert-specific semantic labels. Conflict Offload Learning (COL) uses pair-conditioned offload anchors in similarity computation to reduce the conflicting-gradient burden on the main text representations. Hierarchical Intent Alignment (HIA) aggregates expert similarities and features using query-conditioned weights. Experiments on five TPAS/TIPR benchmarks show GradCO significantly surpasses SOTA methods and validate our design.

</details>


### [48] [TRUCO: A Scalable Lock-Free Algorithm for Parallel Contour Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37297)
*Rafael Munoz-Salinas,Francisco J. Romero-Ramirez,Manuel J. Marin-Jimenez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TRUCO：一种无锁并行轮廓提取算法，基于起点归属规则与投机追踪模型，将二值掩膜转为几何轮廓，在高分辨率场景下较工业标准库获得一个数量级以上加速并保持良好可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统轮廓算法（如 Suzuki-Abe）本质串行，且需构建拓扑层级带来额外开销；高分辨率成像下需利用多核 CPU，同时避免分块拼接与重同步的复杂性。

Method: 提出 TRUCO（Threaded Raster Unrestricted Contour Ownership）无锁并行算法：引入"起点归属规则"确定轮廓归属线程，配合投机追踪模型——线程向下追踪轮廓至相邻区域，并以向上终止逻辑保证每条轮廓恰好被处理一次。

Result: 在合成与真实数据集、消费级与服务器级 CPU 上均显著优于现有基线；相较工业标准实现加速超过一个数量级，并在多样硬件架构的高分辨率场景中保持稳健可扩展性。

Conclusion: TRUCO 以无锁、无需分块拼接与重同步的设计实现了高效并行的几何边界提取，适合作为高分辨率分割输出的后处理方案。

Abstract: Transforming binary masks into geometric contours remains a critical step in many computer vision pipelines, particularly for post-processing high-resolution segmentation outputs. Yet, standard algorithms such as the Suzuki-Abe method are limited by their sequential nature and the overhead of building topological hierarchies. As modern applications transition to high-resolution imaging, there is a critical need for contour extraction that leverages multi-core CPU architectures without the complexity of tile stitching or heavy synchronization.
  This paper presents TRUCO (Threaded Raster Unrestricted Contour Ownership), a lock-free parallel algorithm designed for high-performance geometric boundary extraction. TRUCO introduces a start-point ownership rule, combined with a speculative tracing model, in which threads trace contours downward into adjacent regions and use an upward-termination logic to ensure each contour is processed exactly once.
  Experimental results on synthetic and real-world datasets, conducted on both consumer and server-grade CPUs, demonstrate that TRUCO significantly outperforms existing baselines. The method achieves speedups of over an order of magnitude compared to the industry standard while maintaining robust scalability on high-resolution scenes across diverse hardware architectures.

</details>


### [49] [CodeCap-Bench: Can Code Serve as a Good Video Caption?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37341)
*Jiafu Tang,Yifan Xu,Eric Tang,Junjie Zhao,Shipeng Luo,Te Sun,Haoxuan Hu,Yiyan Ji,Xinlong Chen,Zihao Wang,Yang Shi,Yuanxing Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Jiaheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"代码作为视频描述"（Code as Video Caption）新范式，用可执行程序表达视频的图形基元、布局规则与时间函数，并构建CodeCap-Bench基准评估MLLM生成忠实代码描述的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言在精确表达细粒度空间结构与时间动态时往往冗长且存在歧义，难以充分保留视觉信息，因此需要一种更紧凑、无歧义的互补表示形式。

Method: 将代码描述与自然语言描述在下游问答与视频重建任务上对比；构建CodeCap-Bench，包含5类结构化2D内容共323个视频、细粒度标注与多层级评测框架，系统评估主流MLLM的代码描述生成能力。

Result: 代码描述在推理准确率与重建保真度上优于自然语言描述；领先的闭源MLLM展现出较好的代码描述能力，但在捕捉复杂时间动态上仍有困难，开源模型明显落后。

Conclusion: 代码可作为视频内容的有效互补表示，但其潜力受限于MLLM生成忠实代码描述的能力，尤其时间动态建模仍是主要瓶颈。

Abstract: Video captioning aims to preserve visual information through compact descriptions, predominantly using natural language. However, precisely expressing fine-grained spatial structures and temporal dynamics in natural language often leads to lengthy and potentially ambiguous descriptions. In this work, we explore Code as Video Caption, a paradigm where executable programs serve as a complementary representation of visual content through graphical primitives, layout rules, and functions of time. To examine whether code captions effectively preserve video information, we compare them with natural language captions on downstream question answering and video reconstruction tasks. Our results show that code captions achieve superior reasoning accuracy and reconstruction fidelity. Despite these advantages, realizing the potential of code captioning depends on whether current multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can generate faithful code captions. To systematically assess this capability, we introduce CodeCap-Bench, which comprises 323 videos spanning five categories of structured 2D content, along with fine-grained annotations and a multi-level evaluation framework. Extensive experiments reveal that while leading proprietary MLLMs demonstrate promising code captioning capabilities, they still struggle to faithfully capture complex temporal dynamics, with open-source models lagging substantially behind.

</details>


### [50] [ReferStream: Interactive Subject Injection for Streaming Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37566)
*Shuolin Xu,Guanyuan Li,Boyu Li,Kelu Yao,Xiaogang Xu,Hongchuan Yu,Shuicheng Yan,Xiaobin Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReferStream：免训练的流式视频生成新主体注入框架，结合Joint Latent场景内参考构建与瞬态缓存注入，并配套新基准ReferStream Bench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频生成中需自然注入新主体，但独立于当前场景编码参考缺少整合引导，而将参考特征常驻内存又会干扰已建立上下文。

Method: 免训练框架：Joint Latent将参考潜变量置于最新场景潜变量的目标区域，使参考特征在上下文中被提取；Cache Restoration用这些特征调节注入chunk后恢复被覆盖的全局缓存条目，同时保留新生成历史，无需重训生成器或修改已输出帧。

Result: 提出ReferStream Bench基准（计划性参考到达、演化指令、主体存在显式标注，覆盖InShot与MultiShot）；实验中文本遵循度、主体相似度和计数准确率优于基线，包括预先给定全部参考的离线方法。

Conclusion: 以场景内参考构建加瞬态缓存注入实现无需训练的流式新主体注入，在不破坏既有主体的前提下提升注入保真度，并提供相应评测基准。

Abstract: Injecting new subjects naturally during streaming video generation requires integrating newly supplied visual references into the existing video while preserving established subjects and the scene. Encoding references independently of the current scene provides little guidance for this integration, while keeping their features in persistent memory can interfere with established context. We propose ReferStream, a framework that requires no additional training and combines reference construction using the current scene with transient cache injection. Joint Latent places reference latents in target regions of the latest scene latent, allowing reference features to be extracted in context. Cache Restoration uses these features to condition the injection chunk, then restores the overwritten global cache entries while retaining the newly generated history. This procedure incorporates new visual conditions without retraining the generator or revising emitted frames. We further introduce ReferStream Bench, a new benchmark with scheduled reference arrivals, evolving instructions, and explicit annotations of subject presence for InShot and MultiShot generation. Evaluation follows the event schedule and measures both the fidelity of new subjects and the preservation of established subjects, alongside video quality, text adherence, and count accuracy. Experiments on ReferStream Bench show higher scores for text adherence, subject similarity, and count accuracy than the compared baselines, including offline methods given all references in advance. Project page: https://xushuolin.github.io/ReferStream/.

</details>


### [51] [The Noise Plane: A Diffusion Bridge Across Exponent and Dispersion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37576)
*Xuran Hu,Yuru Jia,Tengxi Wang,Miloš Daković,Ljubiša Stanković,Wufan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 p-Bridge：以噪声指数 p 与累积测量 ν 构成的"噪声平面"为统一框架的 Tweedie 噪声扩散去噪方法，用共享网络实现跨指数鲁棒的去噪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式去噪通常假设高斯扰动，而传感器噪声多服从随信号变化的非高斯统计规律（Poisson、Gamma 等），固定高斯假设在真实成像场景中不匹配，且对每个噪声模型单独训练成本高。

Method: 将噪声建模为 Tweedie 分布族：p 刻画方差—强度关系，ν（累积测量）控制噪声水平，二者构成包含高斯、Poisson、Gamma 的噪声平面；用独立增量的随机桥连接不同测量分布，使去噪等价于渐进测量累积，起止指数均可指定，支持从观测水平恢复到指定更高水平；测量叠加得到共享的反向均值更新，并配以 deviance 目标学习条件均值；提出潜在复合 Poisson–Gamma 构造以支持中间指数的采样；采用单一以 p 为条件的网络适配不同噪声模型。

Result: 指数条件化提升了跨指数鲁棒性，优于固定指数模型；使用估计的 p 时，共享网络性能与 oracle 挑选的五个专家模型集合相当；在合成图像、FMD 荧光显微与多传感器 SAR 图像上验证有效。

Conclusion: 以噪声平面与随机桥为统一视角的 p-Bridge 框架，将去噪解释为测量累积过程，用一个指数条件化网络覆盖多种信号相关噪声，兼顾跨噪声体制的泛化与实际应用效果。

Abstract: Diffusion-based denoising commonly uses Gaussian perturbations, whereas sensor noise follows diverse signal-dependent statistical laws. In this paper, we propose $p$-Bridge, a unified measurement-indexed diffusion framework for Tweedie noise. The noise exponent $p$ describes the variance--intensity relation, while accumulated measurement $ν$ controls the noise level. Together, they define a noise plane that includes Gaussian, Poisson, and Gamma models. The stochastic bridge connects measurement distributions through independent increments, giving denoising a physical interpretation as progressive measurement accumulation. Both the starting and stopping indices can be specified, allowing restoration from an observed measurement level to a prescribed higher level. Measurement superposition yields a shared reverse-mean update, paired with a deviance-based objective for learning the required conditional mean. A latent compound Poisson--Gamma construction enables sampling at intermediate exponents. A single exponent-conditioned network adapts to different Tweedie noise models without separate training for each exponent. Experiments show that exponent conditioning improves cross-exponent robustness over fixed-exponent models. Using estimated exponents, the shared network achieves robustness comparable to an oracle-selected set of five specialist models. Experiments on synthetic images, FMD fluorescence microscopy, and multi-sensor SAR images further demonstrate its effectiveness across different signal-dependent noise regimes.

</details>


### [52] [As the Story Unfolds: Watching a Film and Identifying Characters as a Human Does](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37579)
*Zhongrui Gui,Junyu Xie,Tengda Han,Weidi Xie,Andrew Zisserman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"角色命名"任务：给定对话中出现的姓名及对应视频上下文，判定画面中哪个角色拥有该姓名，无需外部演员表即可从影片本身实现在线角色身份推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有角色识别方法依赖外部资源（演员表、演员照片、预构建角色库），不符合真实观影中观众随剧情推进逐步建立角色身份的认知过程，论文希望直接从影片本身推断角色身份。

Method: 构建角色命名与首次出场的数据集及标注；提出面向该任务的视频-对话联合模型，利用镜头正反打、自我介绍、直接称呼、第三方指称、视线、时间连续性等电影语言线索；并提出随影片推进逐步构建角色库的渐进式框架。

Result: 提供了角色命名与首次角色出场的评测基准，并将所提视频-对话模型与商用多模态大语言模型进行对比。

Conclusion: 通过数据集、模型与渐进式角色库框架，支撑在线、类人的电影理解，使角色身份从影片内容中被自主推断。

Abstract: Films introduce characters incrementally as they progress. We study the problem of identifying principal characters and assigning their names directly from the movie itself, without using external cast lists, actor photographs, or pre-built character banks. We introduce the character naming task: given a name mentioned in dialogue and the associated video context, the model must determine which visible character bears that name. This task is challenging because name resolution often depends on cinematic cues such as shot-reverse-shot structure, self-introduction, direct address, third-party reference, gaze, and temporal continuity. We provide a dataset and annotations for evaluating character naming and first character appearances, develop a video-and-dialogue model for the task, and compare it with proprietary multimodal large language models. We further introduce a progressive framework for building a character bank as the film unfolds. Together, these contributions support online, human-like movie understanding in which character identities are inferred from the film itself.

</details>


### [53] [Zero-Shot Multimodal Event Spotting in Soccer Broadcasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37583)
*Sadia Kamal,Mahmudul Hasan,Tony Braskich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出零样本足球事件检测框架：以重叠片段的故事板+解说文本由通用多模态模型联合处理，实现事件检测、分类与时序定位，无需足球专用训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有足球事件检测方法依赖大规模标注数据与足球领域专用训练，成本高且跨数据集泛化差。

Method: 将整场比赛切分为重叠片段，用紧凑视觉故事板与对齐的解说转录文本作为输入，交由通用多模态模型联合处理，实现零样本检测、分类与时序定位；并引入证据支撑的精彩度评分阶段，用自然语言解释对检测事件排序。

Result: 在3场人工标注、共130个事件的比赛上，最佳配置达72.0% F1@5s；跨数据集设定下优于经SoccerNet训练的CALF与NetVLAD++；故事板表示相较逐帧推理降低72%推断开销。

Conclusion: 零样本多模态框架可在无任务专用训练下有效完成足球事件发现，故事板表示兼顾精度与效率，并支持可解释的精彩片段排序。

Abstract: Automatic soccer event spotting supports highlight generation, video indexing, and sports analytics, but existing methods depend on large annotated datasets and soccer-specific training. We present a zero-shot framework that detects, classifies, and temporally localizes events in full-length soccer broadcasts without task-specific parameter training. Each match is segmented into overlapping clips represented by a compact visual storyboard and the aligned commentary transcript, which a general-purpose multimodal model processes jointly. On three manually annotated matches containing 130 events, the best configuration achieves 72.0% F1@5s and outperforms SoccerNet-trained CALF and NetVLAD++ in a cross-dataset setting, while the storyboard representation reduces inference cost by 72% compared with individual frames. We further analyze the effects of model family, input representation, and prompt optimization, and introduce an evidence-grounded excitement-scoring stage that ranks detected events with natural-language explanations for highlight selection.

</details>


### [54] [Perceiving and Predicting Dynamic Sports: Spatiotemporal Intelligence in Vision Models for Egocentric Tennis Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37619)
*Tudor Braicu,Joseph DelPreto,Jared Boyer,Mónika Farsang,Maximilian Stölzle,Tim Niklas Seyde,Daniela Rus*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文构建含自我中心视频与五个异中心视频同步的网球数据集，用11类基准设定评估7个时空预测任务，最佳模型实现自我球员0.14 m、球0.54 m定位误差，并据此提出条件扩散模型实时生成全身网球运动学动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 敏捷机器人需要时空智能来理解场景、推理交互并预判未来事件，但基础视觉模型（尤其是移动自我中心视角下）支撑这些能力的效果尚不明确，故需系统性基准评估。

Method: 采集带标注的网球数据集（自我中心视频与5路异中心视频同步，含全局位置、接触时刻与击球类型标签）；设计7个自我中心预测任务（2D自我球员与3D球定位、未来球员/球轨迹、正反手击球预判、下次接触剩余时间、接触时刻3D球位置）；对比图像编码器、视频编码器与视觉语言模型，均接联合训练预测头；并另提出条件扩散模型，由视觉编码预测驱动实时生成全身运动学动作。

Result: 最佳模型当前帧定位平均误差为自我球员0.14 m、球0.54 m；1.1 s未来预测误差为球员0.34 m、球0.91 m；接触时间预测平均误差0.14 s。辅助深度信息显著提升3D球定位，说明度量深度推断仍是难点；更长图像历史未提升球预测，表明表征更多利用场景上下文而非仅依赖帧间运动。

Conclusion: 结果为理解基础视觉模型如何借自我中心视频支撑实时动态机器人提供了实证基准与初步方法路径。

Abstract: Agile robots require spatiotemporal intelligence to interpret scenes, reason about interactions, and anticipate future events. Yet it remains unclear how well foundation vision models support these capabilities, particularly from a moving egocentric camera, which is a natural view for robots. To investigate this, we collect an annotated tennis dataset and train a suite of vision models on spatiotemporal tasks. Our dataset contains egocentric video synchronized with five allocentric videos, estimated global positions, labeled contact times, and annotated stroke types. We then evaluate seven prediction tasks from egocentric images: 2D ego player and 3D ball localization, future player and ball trajectories, forehand or backhand stroke anticipation, time to the next ego player ball contact, and 3D ball position at that contact. We compare image encoders, video encoders, and vision-language models with jointly trained prediction heads. The best performing models achieve mean current-frame localization errors of 0.14 m for the ego player and 0.54 m for the ball, future forecasting errors of 0.34 m for the ego player and 0.91 m for the ball over 1.1 s, and a mean contact-time prediction error of 0.14 s. Auxiliary depth information substantially improves 3D ball localization, suggesting that inferring metric depth remains a challenge. Longer image histories do not improve ball forecasting, indicating that representations exploit scene context rather than rely solely on inter-frame motion. Finally, we propose a conditional diffusion model to generate whole-body kinematic tennis motion from vision encoder predictions on egocentric video at real-time rates, adapting movements as predictions are refined. These results move towards a deeper understanding of how foundation vision models can support real-time dynamic robots using egocentric video.

</details>


### [55] [Know, then Draw: A New Learning Paradigm for Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37626)
*Yucheng Mao,Chongjian Ge,Zhuowen Tu,Hao Tan,Hanwen Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一种"自回归预训练获取知识 + 扩散后训练实现高保真渲染"的学习范式，并系统研究了知识获取与渲染两个阶段的目标函数、表征与规模扩展规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉生成同时需要高层语义知识与细粒度像素细节，单一生成范式难以兼顾；作者希望将两种能力分别交给最擅长的阶段与目标来承担。

Method: 将角色分配给两阶段：自回归预训练负责知识获取，扩散后训练负责高保真渲染。在该框架下进行受控对比研究目标函数、表征与扩展性：比较自回归与像素扩散作为预训练目标；发现AR单独生成质量与最终性能不必然相关，语义质量更关键，据此设计仅用语言监督训练、不含图像解码器与重建损失的纯语义分词器；并拟合知识获取与渲染的计算最优扩展律。

Result: 自回归是更强的预训练目标，提升学习效率与最终生成质量；自回归预训练相比从零训练的像素扩散模型降低了拟合的渐近渲染损失；依据扩展律训练的3B参数文生图模型仅用6,600 H100 GPU小时，在GenEval得86、DPG-Bench得87。

Conclusion: 分离"知识获取"与"渲染"两阶段、以自回归预训练配合扩散后训练的范式在大规模上有效，且计算高效，验证了该范式的可扩展性。

Abstract: Visual generation requires both high-level semantic knowledge and fine-grained pixel details. We propose a learning paradigm that assigns these roles to autoregressive pretraining for knowledge acquisition and diffusion post-training for high-fidelity rendering. Within this framework, we study the objective, representation, and scaling of knowledge acquisition and rendering. Controlled comparisons identify autoregression as the stronger pretraining objective for downstream rendering, improving both learning efficiency and final generation quality. We further find that AR-only generation quality does not necessarily correlate with final performance; semantic quality matters more. This motivates a purely semantic tokenizer trained with language supervision alone, without an image decoder or reconstruction loss. We establish compute-optimal scaling laws for knowledge acquisition and rendering, and find that autoregressive pretraining lowers the fitted asymptotic rendering loss compared with pixel diffusion models trained from scratch. Guided by the laws, we train a 3B-parameter text-to-image model with only 6,600 H100 GPU-hours, achieving 86 on GenEval and 87 on DPG-Bench and validating the proposed paradigm at scale.

</details>


### [56] [Repurposing Video Diffusion Transformers for Cross-View Temporal Object Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37633)
*Siyoon Jin,Dahyun Chung,Honggyu An,Sangbeom Lim,Wonjin Nam,Jaewoo Jung,WonJun Moon,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出跨视角时序目标对应任务，并给出统一框架 XTrack：复用预训练视频 DiT 通过 RGB-to-mask 隐空间传输同时完成跨视角匹配与时序跟踪，配合注意力对齐与伪标签流程实现稳健的身份保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨视角目标对应方法依赖逐帧匹配或序列跟踪，在外观变化或遮挡下会传播对应错误，且缺乏跨视角与跨时间同时保持目标身份的能力；同时缺少稠密标注。

Method: 定义跨视角时序目标对应任务（由查询掩码出发，在双视角与时间维度保持身份）。XTrack 将预训练视频 DiT 改造为 RGB-to-mask 隐空间传输，联合建模跨视角匹配与时序跟踪；引入焦点跨视角注意力对齐、难负样本条件化与局部上下文裁剪策略以保证可靠定位；并用伪标签策展流程将稀疏 Ego-Exo4D 标注转为稠密监督以支持视频级训练。

Result: 实验表明 XTrack 作为统一框架有效，具备稳健的时序一致性与身份保持能力。

Conclusion: 统一隐空间传输范式可替代逐帧匹配/序列跟踪，架构约束与稠密伪监督是保证跨视角定位与时序一致性的关键。

Abstract: Cross-view object correspondence identifies the same object across views. However, existing methods often rely on frame-wise matching or sequential tracking, which can propagate correspondence errors under appearance changes or occlusions. Therefore, we introduce cross-view temporal object correspondence, which requires preserving object identity across both views and time from a query mask. To address this, we propose XTrack, a unified framework which repurposes a pretrained video DiT with semantic and spatiotemporal priors for RGB-to-mask latent transport, jointly modeling cross-view matching and temporal tracking. However, naive video DiTs do not guarantee accurate cross-view grounding and often attend to semantically similar yet incorrect objects across views. Thus, we introduce focal cross-view attention alignment, hard negative conditioning, and local context crop strategies to ensure reliable grounding. Finally, beyond these architectural enhancements, we also tackle the lack of dense annotation by introducing a pseudo-label curation pipeline that converts sparse Ego-Exo4D annotations into dense supervision to enable effective video-level training. Our results demonstrate the effectiveness of XTrack as a unified framework, ensuring robust temporal consistency and identity preservation.

</details>


### [57] [Can Discrete Tokens Serve as Semantically Lossless Representations of Visual Signals?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37662)
*Jialv Zou,Xiaotong Li,Zhengrong Yue,Xu Huang,Yaxin Luo,Wenhao Yan,Haodong Li,Wenyu Liu,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文质疑"量化误差决定离散视觉token语义保真度"的普遍假设，指出离散索引的有效比特预算才是下游性能的关键，并提出因子化向量量化（FVQ），使离散表示在多模态理解上首次可比甚至媲美连续表示模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散视觉token可像语言一样在共享的下一token预测目标下实现原生多模态建模，但学界普遍认为量化误差必然导致语义损失，从而把连续视觉表示视为语义保真度的固有上界。作者希望重新审视这一假设，检验离散表示的语义保真度是否真的受量化误差根本限制。

Method: 以多模态理解为语义保真度的代理指标，系统比较量化误差与离散表示的语义表现；定位出"有效比特预算"为关键因素。针对现有量化器随码本增多而语义增益饱和的问题，提出因子化向量量化（FVQ）：对互补的固定维度特征视图使用对称并行码本进行量化，使其在更大比特预算下持续获益。基于工业级多模态数据训练模型，并总结实现竞争力的关键原则与实践配方。

Result: 量化误差与理解性能并不一致，误差更大的离散表示反而可获得更好理解；FVQ在更大比特预算下持续提升，最终在多模态理解上匹配连续视觉表示。模型在九项指标平均上超越UniAR 22.8%、Emu3-Chat 59.2%，为离散表示模型中的SOTA，且据称是首个在细粒度OCR等任务上与Qwen3-VL等强连续表示模型具有竞争力的离散模型。

Conclusion: 离散视觉表示的语义上限并非由量化误差决定，而取决于有效比特预算；通过FVQ等设计可突破饱和瓶颈，使离散表示达到与连续表示相当的语义保真度。这为以离散视觉表示支撑大规模原生多模态基础模型提供了依据与可行方案。

Abstract: Discrete visual tokens represent vision in a discrete form analogous to language, offering a native interface for multimodal modeling under a shared next-token prediction objective. However, quantization error is widely assumed to cause unavoidable semantic loss, fundamentally limiting the semantic fidelity of discrete visual tokens and positioning continuous visual representations as an intrinsic upper bound. Using multimodal understanding as a proxy for semantic fidelity, we revisit this assumption and find quantization error unreliable, as discrete representations with substantially larger errors can achieve better understanding. Instead, the effective bit budget of discrete indices emerges as a key determinant of downstream performance. However, existing quantizers eventually saturate as additional codebooks contribute progressively less semantic information. As one approach to alleviating this saturation, we introduce Factorized Vector Quantization, which quantizes complementary, fixed-dimensional views of the visual feature with symmetric parallel codebooks. It continues to improve at larger bit budgets, ultimately matching continuous visual representations in multimodal understanding. Trained on industrial-scale multimodal data, our model achieves state-of-the-art performance among discrete-representation models, outperforming UniAR and Emu3-Chat by 22.8% and 59.2%, respectively, on a nine-metric average. To our knowledge, it is also the first discrete-representation model competitive with strong continuous-representation models such as Qwen3-VL, including on fine-grained OCR tasks. We further identify the key principles and a practical recipe enabling this competitiveness. These results and insights support the potential of discrete visual representations to underpin large-scale native multimodal foundation models. Code and models are available at https://github.com/hustvl/FVQ

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

### [58] [MedJev: Evaluating Zero-Shot System-One Decision Making in Medicine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36006)
*Simon A. Lee,Jiahang Sha,Moonseong Jeong,Yunbei Pan,Jeffrey N. Chiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评测了开源System One（单次前向、不生成文本）临床决策模型MedJev在10个医学基准上的表现，并提出一种轻量两参数重校准方法，使其在本地低算力条件下的预测可与GPT-4类模型相近，且可通过少量标注样本适配本地人群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: System One类决策模型（如Jev）无需生成文本即可单次前向回答结构化问题，具备快速、计算高效的临床决策支持潜力；但此类开源模型在医学知识、诊断与患者级预测等任务上的综合能力、校准质量及本地化适用性尚缺乏系统评估。

Method: 构建覆盖医学知识、诊断与患者级预测的10个医学基准（含MIMIC IV与MIMIC IV ED）评测MedJev；与零样本LLM基线、已发表GPT结果及监督模型对比；分析置信度校准（ECE）；提出仅含两个参数、使用50–200个标注样本的轻量重校准方法以适配本地患者人群。

Result: MedJev在MedQA达72.5%、USMLE 75.4%、NEJM 80.1%，接近已发表GPT-4水平且置信度校准良好（ECE 0.028–0.063）；患者级任务上院内死亡AUROC 0.926，超过XGBoost（0.902），但ED分诊入院等操作性结局弱于监督模型（0.745 vs 0.809），且患者级预测存在明显失准（院内死亡ECE 0.286）；两参数重校准将ECE降至0.007。

Conclusion: 开源System One模型是有前景的医学AI零样本基线，兼具竞争力预测性能、低计算成本与高效本地校准能力；作者开源代码与评测框架以支持后续研究。

Abstract: System One decision models such as Jev answer structured questions in a single forward pass without generating text, offering fast and computationally efficient clinical decision support. We evaluate MedJev, an open System One model that runs locally on a laptop, across ten medical benchmarks spanning medical knowledge, diagnosis, and patient level prediction using MIMIC IV and MIMIC IV ED. We compare MedJev against zero shot LLM baselines, published GPT results, and supervised models. MedJev achieves accuracies of 72.5 percent on MedQA, 75.4 percent on USMLE, and 80.1 percent on NEJM, approaching published GPT 4 performance while maintaining well calibrated confidence estimates (ECE 0.028 to 0.063). On patient level tasks, MedJev achieves an AUROC of 0.926 for In Hospital Mortality, exceeding XGBoost (0.902), but underperforms supervised models on operational outcomes such as ED Triage Admission (0.745 vs. 0.809). Patient level predictions also exhibit substantial miscalibration (In Hospital Mortality ECE 0.286). We introduce a lightweight, two parameter recalibration method that adapts predictions to local patient populations using 50 to 200 labeled examples, reducing ECE to 0.007. Our findings establish open System One models as a promising zero shot baseline for medical AI, combining competitive predictive performance with low computational cost and efficient local calibration. We release our code and evaluation harness to support future research.

</details>


### [59] [FinLLM-KG: Knowledge Graph-Enhanced LLMs for Financial Sentiment Analysis in Algorithmic Trading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36083)
*Giorgos Iacovides,Oliver Hannibal,Wuyang Zhou,Danilo Mandic*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FinLLM-KG 是一个模型无关框架，将因果 LLM 与金融知识图谱结合，在推理阶段注入来自 SEC 10-K 文件的、具备时间依据的公司级知识（无需更新模型权重），以提升金融情感分析的情境性、可解释性与投资组合表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融 LLM 情感分析方法将每篇文章孤立预测且为黑箱，缺乏情境理解，难以用于高风险金融决策。作者希望在不重训模型的前提下引入企业层面的外部知识，并让预测依据可审查。

Method: FinLLM-KG 以两种形式在推理时提供上下文：自然语言公司简介，或少量按相关性排序的事实三元组；并将文章级情感分数归因到具体句子与检索到的知识图谱事实，实现证据可检验。框架适用于任意因果 LLM，计算开销近乎为零。

Result: 仿真实验表明，FinLLM-KG 在投资组合表现上持续优于当前最优基线。

Conclusion: 通过推理时知识注入与句子/事实级归因，FinLLM-KG 在低成本、模型无关的条件下同时提升了金融情感分析的情境性、可解释性与下游投资决策效果，支持人机协同审查。

Abstract: Recent advances in Generative AI have substantially improved financial sentiment analysis through post-trained financial large language models (LLMs). However, existing approaches remain confined to predicting the sentiment of each article in isolation and operate as black boxes, which limits contextual understanding and hinders deployment in high-stakes financial decision-making. To this end, we introduce FinLLM-KG, a model-agnostic framework that combines causal LLMs with a financial knowledge graph, in order to inject temporally grounded, company-level knowledge from SEC 10-K filings at inference time, without updating model weights. Within FinLLM-KG, context is supplied either as a natural-language company profile or as a small set of relevance-ranked factual triples. This enables sentiment predictions to account for broader firm-level information rather than the article alone, at almost zero computational cost and for any causal LLM. In addition, attributing each article-level sentiment score to individual sentences and to the retrieved knowledge-graph facts makes the evidence behind the prediction inspectable, thereby enhancing explainability and supporting human-in-the-loop review. Simulations demonstrate that FinLLM-KG consistently improves portfolio performance over the state-of-the-art baseline.

</details>


### [60] [LLM Hallucination Detection via Low-rank Multilinear Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36191)
*Devansh Sharma,Faizul Rakib Sayem,Shahana Ibrahim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LOTUS：基于低秩多线性分解的LLM幻觉检测框架，用统计描述子汇总token表示并做低秩张量分解得到紧凑特征，降低对标注数据依赖，多模型多基准上优于SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM常生成流畅但事实错误的回答，可靠性存疑。现有基于内部表示的幻觉检测器难以从高维隐状态中提取有效信号：多依赖选定激活或池化压缩，可能忽略分布于Transformer层、特征模态与token维度上的判别信息。

Method: 提出LOTUS（Low-rank Order-statistic TUcker-based Subspace learning）多线性低秩分解框架。第一步用互补统计描述子汇总变长token表示，刻画峰值激活、激活分布与跨层变化；第二步通过低秩多线性（Tucker类）分解提取反映隐状态潜在结构的紧凑表示；最后用其训练轻量级幻觉分类器。

Result: 在多个LLM与问答基准上的实验表明，幻觉检测性能优于近期SOTA基线。

Conclusion: 以统计描述子+多线性低秩子空间学习可高效压缩LLM隐状态并提升幻觉检测效果，同时减少对标注数据的依赖。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) frequently generate fluent yet factually incorrect responses, raising concerns about their reliability. Recent hallucination detectors increasingly exploit internal representations, but extracting informative signals from high-dimensional hidden states remains challenging. Existing approaches often rely on selected activations or pooling-based compression, potentially overlooking discriminative information distributed across Transformer layers, feature modes, and tokens. We propose LOTUS (Low-rank Order-statistic TUcker-based Subspace learning), a multilinear low-rank decomposition framework for hallucination detection that extracts compact representations from LLM hidden states while reducing dependence on labeled data. Our approach first summarizes variable-length token representations using complementary statistical descriptors that capture peak activations, activation distributions, and cross-layer changes. We then leverage low-rank multilinear decomposition to extract compact representations that capture the underlying structure of LLM hidden states, which are subsequently used to train a lightweight hallucination classifier. Experiments across multiple LLMs and question-answering benchmarks demonstrate improved hallucination detection performance over recent state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [61] [Beyond IceBERT: Open-Data ModernBERT Encoders for Icelandic from 12M to 1B Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36230)
*Hafsteinn Einarsson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 用ModernBERT架构重训冰岛语编码器（12M–1B参数，9B token开放数据），在统一协议下评估30个模型：1B从零训练模型在全部11项任务上显著优于IceBERT，而从同形状英语Ettin热启动的1B模型在所有排名任务上得分最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IceBERT 曾得出两个结论：(1) 清洗后的网页爬取数据足以训练强模型；(2) 从多语言模型出发对某些任务有帮助。作者希望借助 ModernBERT 架构重新检验这两项发现，并在受控条件下厘清热启动（warm start）跨语言迁移的真实收益与机理。

Method: 训练12M至1B参数、6种规模的冰岛语 ModernBERT 编码器，使用9B token 开放数据；用统一协议加置信区间重跑 IceBERT 基准及5项新任务，共评估30个编码器。由于模型形状与英语 Ettin 编码器及多语言 mmBERT 对齐，可做受控消融：6种规模×4种token预算的热启动 vs 从零训练，并对比是否移植词表。

Result: 1B从零训练模型是首个该规模的单语冰岛语编码器，在全部11项任务上显著优于IceBERT；同形状英语Ettin热启动的1B模型在每项排名任务上均取得最佳分数。植入本族词表后，热启动在任何设置下都不显著差于同文本从零训练；base规模以上，热启动在4项任务上不显著差于用4倍文本从零训练（base与1B为8倍文本）。随模型增大，迁移主要来自源模型的Transformer层，因此即便英语源也有帮助。保留源词表则问答F1损失5分，计算量近3倍。

Conclusion: 清洗网页数据加ModernBERT足以训出强单语冰岛语编码器；热启动是高效的正向迁移手段，"英语源无益"的顾虑被证伪——关键在于Transformer层迁移而非语言匹配；词表必须移植回目标语言，否则代价高昂（QA掉5 F1、算力近3倍）。

Abstract: IceBERT, the reference encoder for Icelandic, found that a cleaned web crawl is enough to train a strong model and that starting from a multilingual model can help on some tasks. We revisit both findings with the ModernBERT architecture. We train Icelandic encoders from 12M to 1B parameters on 9B tokens of open data, and rerun IceBERT's benchmarks and five newer tasks for 30 encoders under one protocol with confidence intervals. Our 1B model trained from scratch, to our knowledge the first monolingual Icelandic encoder of that size, is significantly better than IceBERT on all eleven tasks; warm-started from the English Ettin encoder of the same shape, it has the best score of all encoders on every task we rank. Because our model shapes match the English Ettin encoders and the multilingual mmBERT, we can also test the warm start under controlled conditions, at six model sizes and four token budgets. With our vocabulary transplanted, a warm start was never significantly worse than scratch training on the same Icelandic text, and from base size up it was not significantly worse on four tasks than scratch training on 4 times the text (8 times at base and 1B). As models grow, most of the transfer comes from the source model's transformer layers, so even an English source helps. Keeping the source vocabulary instead cost 5 F1 on question answering and nearly three times the compute.

</details>


### [62] [No Tokenizer, Still a Gap: Cross-Lingual Fairness of Homoglyph and Invisible-Character Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36432)
*Alexander Nemecek,Yuqiao Xu,Joseph Falkenburg,Erman Ayday*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了同形异码（homoglyph）与不可见字符水印在40种语言变体、24种文字系统中的容量与鲁棒性，发现绕开分词器并未消除语言间的资源不平等，只是把不平等的来源从分词器转移到了文字系统的同形字符丰富度上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成时水印（generation-time watermark）在低资源语言上效果较差，原因在于分词器（tokenizer）对这些语言文本的切分方式不利。同形异码与不可见字符水印属于编辑式（edit-based）水印，在文本写就后逐字符操作、完全不经过分词器，因此作者追问：这类水印能否避开生成式水印在低资源语言上的劣势。

Method: 在24种文字系统（scripts）下的40种语言变体上，测量三项指标：（1）容量，即每个字符可承载的隐藏比特数；（2）日常处理与定向移除攻击下水印的存活率；（3）带密钥检测器所需的最小文本量。通过相关性分析检验容量与语言资源水平的关系，并比较不同文字系统间的差异来源。

Result: 容量在不同语言间相差3.3倍，其中文字系统解释了96%的变异。在拉丁字母内部，资源水平无显著影响（ρ=+0.095，p=0.78）；跨文字系统则容量随资源水平上升（ρ=+0.54，95% CI [+0.14,+0.83]），原因是低资源语言往往使用同形字符稀少的文字系统。普通复制粘贴会剥离不可见字符并进一步扩大差距：检测器对英语或波斯语约需2个码位，对老挝语则需93个。

Conclusion: 移除分词器这一环节并不能消除水印能力的语言不平等，只是改变了不平等的根源——由分词器切分策略转为文字系统本身的同形字符资源禀赋。因此编辑式水印也存在结构性的跨语言资源差距。

Abstract: Homoglyph and invisible-character watermarks are edit-based watermarks. Instead of changing how a model generates text, they hide a signal in the text after it is written. They swap some characters for homoglyphs, which look the same, or very similar, but have different Unicode code points, or they insert characters that do not display, all without access to the model that wrote the text. Generation-time watermarks work less well for lower-resourced languages due to how tokenizers split their text. Because they work on individual characters, these watermarks never touch a tokenizer, so we ask whether they escape this gap. Across 40 language varieties in 24 scripts, we measure capacity (the hidden bits each character can carry), how much of it survives routine handling and targeted removal, and how much text a keyed detector needs. Capacity varies 3.3-fold and script accounts for 96\% of that variation. Within Latin script, resource level makes no detectable difference ($ρ=+0.095$, $p=0.78$), but across scripts capacity rises with it ($ρ=+0.54$, 95\% CI $[+0.14,+0.83]$), because lower-resourced languages tend to use scripts with few homoglyphs. Ordinary copy-paste, which strips invisible characters, widens the gap: a detector then needs about 2 code points of English or Persian but 93 of Lao. Removing the tokenizer does not remove the inequity, but instead only changes its source.

</details>


### [63] [CONCUR: Concordant Auxiliary Rewards for Multi-Task, Multi-Reward Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36437)
*Jixuan Leng,Si Si,Fei Wang,Cho-Jui Hsieh,Jiong Zhang,Hsiang-Fu Yu,Inderjit S. Dhillon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CONCUR优势估计器，用满足"主奖励单调"与"天花板上等分"两条件的最近分数替换门控辅助奖励的标准化分数，避免辅助奖励反转主奖励排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务RL训练LLM时，辅助奖励（如长度奖励）受主奖励门控：失败响应上辅助奖励不能超过主奖励。但组相对优势估计器把门控后的辅助项当作普通分量处理，可能反转主奖励排序；且在主奖励达上限时仅由辅助项排序。保留门控辅助有害于部分任务，丢弃又有害于另一部分。

Method: 设计优势估计器CONCUR：将每个门控辅助奖励的标准化分数替换为满足两条件的最近分数——(1) 主奖励更高的响应不得获得更低分数；(2) 主奖励处于上限的响应获得相同分数。CONCUR-T在全训练过程施加两条件；CONCUR-A对"在通过响应中与主奖励排序一致多于反转"的辅助项解除第二条件。

Result: 在两个系列共三个模型、覆盖数学、代码、指令遵循与写作任务上，两种变体的平均得分均高于所有基线，也高于全部丢弃门控辅助的设置。

Conclusion: 通过对齐门控辅助与主奖励的排序约束来修正组相对优势估计，可在不牺牲任务间平衡的前提下稳定利用辅助奖励信号。

Abstract: When a Large Language Model (LLM) is trained with Reinforcement Learning (RL) on multiple tasks, a task's primary reward can gate auxiliary rewards such as a length reward: on a failing response, an auxiliary cannot exceed the primary. However, group-relative advantage estimators treat a gated auxiliary like any other component, so it can reverse the primary's order, and at the primary's ceiling only auxiliaries order responses. Keeping gated auxiliaries hurts some tasks, while dropping them hurts others. We propose CONCUR, an advantage estimator that replaces the standardized scores of each gated auxiliary with the closest scores that satisfy two conditions: responses with higher primary rewards cannot receive lower scores, and responses at the primary's ceiling receive equal scores. CONCUR-T applies both conditions throughout training, and CONCUR-A lifts the second for an auxiliary that agrees with the primary's order more often than it reverses it among passing responses. On three models from two families and across math, code, instruction following, and writing, both variants score higher on average than all baselines and than when all the gated auxiliaries are dropped.

</details>


### [64] [Do AI Scientists Know What's New?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36511)
*Miri Liu,Xiayu Zhao,Encheng Xie,ChengXiang Zhai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出提案级与论断级双重时间基准的新颖性核查评估框架，揭示现有方法高拒真率与高误报的权衡，并提出可解释的论断级新颖性模块ClaimNov。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI科学发现流程中，判断生成的想法是否真正新颖是核心难题。理想的新颖性核查应能拒绝非新颖想法、不过度拒绝真正新颖的想法，并能定位提案中哪些部分非新颖，而非整体性地丢弃提案。但现有核查方法缺乏细粒度、有真实时间基准的评估手段。

Method: 构建提案级与论断级的时间真实标注评估框架，使用四个数据集共1,060个提案（源自已发表论文、审稿争议及与后续真实发表相匹配的生成想法）。以此审计现有新颖性核查，并设计ClaimNov——一个细粒度、可解释的论断级新颖性模块。

Result: 审计发现：对非新颖工作拒真率高的核查方法，对后来被人类研究者实现的想法也有高误报；整篇评估式核查只能通过拒绝整个提案来指向论断级先例。ClaimNov在1.5%误报率下捕获97%的伪装论文，其论断级新颖性画像将LLM评审的误报率从11.8%降至1.5%；在含审稿争议贡献的ICLR录用提案中仅拒绝3%，在100个含拼接先例贡献的新颖提案中保留91个，而最强现有核查仅保留76个。

Conclusion: 基于论断级证据的核查能在保持高召回非新颖工作的同时显著降低误报，并保留建立在既有贡献之上的有效提案，为AI科学发现流程提供更可靠、可解释的新颖性筛选。

Abstract: A major challenge facing AI scientific discovery pipelines is how to determine when a generated idea is meaningfully novel. Ideally, novelty checks in these pipelines must be able to reject non-novel ideas while not rejecting too many novel ideas, and they should also be able to distinguish which parts of a proposal are non-novel, as opposed to opaquely discarding entire proposals. We design an analytical framework for evaluation of novelty checks with temporal ground truth at both the proposal- and claim-level, using four datasets of 1,060 proposals built from published papers, reviewer disputes, and generated ideas matching ones the field went on to publish. Auditing existing novelty checks with this framework, we find that checks with high rejection rates on non-novel work also tend to have high false alarms on ideas that are later realized by human researchers, and observe that checks built to evaluate proposals as a whole can point to claim-level prior art only by rejecting the whole proposal. We therefore introduce ClaimNov, a fine-grained, interpretable claim-level novelty module. ClaimNov catches 97% of disguised papers at a 1.5% false-alarm rate, and its claim-level novelty profile cuts the LLM judge's false alarms from 11.8% to 1.5%. Because its verdict rests on claim-level evidence, ClaimNov also keeps proposals that build on established contributions, rejecting only 3% of ICLR-accepted proposals with a reviewer-disputed contribution and keeping 91 of 100 novel proposals with one spliced prior contribution, against 76 for the strongest existing check. Code and data are available at https://github.com/emmatliu/claimnov.

</details>


### [65] [Benchmarking Task Takeover Across Agent Handoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36523)
*Wenyuan Li,Guang Li,Dongyuan Li,Keisuke Maeda,Takahiro Ogawa,Miki Haseyama*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出HSC-Bench基准（1,039个任务），从"证据恢复"和"状态续接"两方面评估多智能体任务交接中接收模型的表现，并发现单一排行榜无法刻画交接能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体在长任务中协作时，责任常在任务未完成前于模型间转移；接收方需基于先前决策、观察与动作继续工作。仅用任务成功率或记忆保留分数无法刻画"交接时保留的信息"与"接收方如何续接工作"之间的联系。

Method: 构建Handoff State Continuity Benchmark（HSC-Bench），含1,039个任务，设计两条互补评测轨道：恢复相关仓库证据；在既有对话、工具调用与文件工作状态基础上继续推进。对六个接收模型进行对比，并结合后续响应与动作的检查，验证是否遵循既定承诺与已完成工作。

Result: 结构化交接状态对每个模型都提升了仓库证据恢复能力，并对多数模型提升续接表现；两条轨道上的最强接收模型并不一致，说明仅凭证据恢复排名无法表征继承状态下的续接能力。

Conclusion: HSC-Bench同时评估对先前证据的可及性与对既定承诺和已完成工作的遵循，为选择接收模型、评估交接表示形式提供了依据，评判标准应依"需被承继的工作"而定。

Abstract: As agents collaborate on longer tasks, responsibility may pass between models before the work is complete. The receiving model must build on prior decisions, observations, and actions while addressing the remaining requirements. Evaluating this transition requires connecting the information preserved at handoff to how the receiver continues the work, a relationship that task-success and memory-retention scores alone do not capture. We introduce the Handoff State Continuity Benchmark (HSC-Bench), comprising 1,039 tasks that evaluate two complementary aspects of task takeover: recovering relevant repository evidence and continuing from established conversational, tool, and file-work state. Comparisons across six receiving models show that structured handoff state improves repository evidence recovery for every model and continuation performance for most models. The strongest receivers differ between the two tracks, showing that evidence-recovery rankings alone do not characterize performance when continuing from inherited state. By combining these comparisons with checks of subsequent responses and actions, HSC-Bench evaluates both access to prior evidence and adherence to established commitments and completed work. This provides a basis for selecting receiving models and assessing handoff representations according to the work that must be carried forward.

</details>


### [66] [Causal Localization of Evidence Effects in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36524)
*Jipeng Li,Pramod Khargonekar,Yanning Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出受控证据聚合(CEA)与平衡效用因果选择(BUC)，从符号化受控输入中识别少数关键注意力头，并验证其因果影响可迁移至自然语言决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究能否用受控符号输入揭示影响语言模型决策的注意力头，并检验这些被识别出的头是否能解释自然语言任务中的判断。

Method: CEA：在其余固定的提示中改变两个符号的出现次数与顺序，用两答案token的logit差衡量冻结Transformer的偏好，提示不预设正确答案。BUC：联合评估消融头输出后计数效应的移除量，以及两方向下均值输出替换(patching)对效应的恢复程度，从而选出紧凑头集。另推导条件界，将头归因与干预效应相联系，并给出有限样本下可恢复归因候选集的充分条件。

Result: Pythia-410M中，针对四种顺序条件下分别选出的1–12个头，消融可移除65.5%–91.6%的计数效应，且对未参与选择的计数比较仍保持影响。在自然语言任务（判断哪个州被更多报道支持）上，仅在符号化是否提示上选出的8个头被消融后，平均准确率下降8.2个百分点，而按规模和层匹配的随机头集仅下降0.8个百分点。

Conclusion: 受控符号探针可识别紧凑的注意力头集合，其因果影响可延伸至自然语言决策，为机制归因提供可验证的干预式方法。

Abstract: Can controlled symbolic inputs reveal attention heads that influence a language model's decisions from natural language evidence? We introduce controlled evidence aggregation (CEA), which varies the counts and order of two symbols in otherwise fixed prompts and measures changes in a frozen Transformer's preference between two answer tokens. The preference is measured by their logit difference; the prompts prescribe no correct answer. Balanced utility causal selection (BUC) identifies compact head sets by jointly evaluating how much ablating their outputs removes the count effect and how closely patching mean outputs between count conditions recovers the effect in both directions. We derive conditional bounds linking head attribution to intervention effects and specifying when the attribution shortlist can be recovered from finite samples. In Pythia-410M, ablating sets of 1-12 heads selected separately for four ordering regimes removes 65.5-91.6% of the count effect. The sets retain their influence on count comparisons not used for selection. To test relevance beyond symbolic inputs, we evaluate a natural language task asking which state is supported by more reports. Ablating eight heads selected only on symbolic yes/no prompts lowers mean accuracy by 8.2 percentage points, versus 0.8 for random sets matched by size and layer. Our results demonstrate that controlled symbolic probes can identify compact attention head sets whose causal influence extends to natural language decisions.

</details>


### [67] [ETC-Bench: A Multilingual Benchmark for Evaluation-Side Transcript Canonicalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36527)
*Zhiheng Qian,Junhao Du,Jing Peng,Hanqi Li,Kunyang Peng,Feng Xue,Kai Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出评估侧转写规范化任务(ETC)及多语言基准ETC-Bench(12种语言、181种现象类型、最小对构造),并给出指标SENA与方法TACS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一语音的书面转写可存在不由语音信号唯一决定的差异,这会干扰以转写为参照的评估,需要将多种表层形式映射为统一规范形式。

Method: 构建ETC-Bench:每条实例为共享句境、仅目标片段体现某一现象的最小对;提出SENA指标,结合独立规范化后成员间精确一致与相对配对成员的BERTScore约束以控制信息损失;并设计Taxonomy-guided Agent Canonicalization Skill(TACS),向LLM智能体提供现象描述与显式规范化契约。

Result: 在5种基线配置与3个基于TACS的智能体上实验,基于TACS的智能体在共有语言上优于基线系统。

Conclusion: ETC-Bench为跨语言的评估侧转写规范化提供了系统化测试平台。

Abstract: Written transcripts of the same speech can differ in ways that are not uniquely determined by the speech signal. We formulate evaluation-side transcript canonicalization (ETC), which maps multiple surface forms to a single canonical form when their differences are not determined by speech. To our knowledge, ETC-Bench is the first and most comprehensive multilingual benchmark for evaluation-side transcript canonicalization, covering 181 phenomenon types across 12 languages. Each instance is a minimal pair that shares a sentence context and differs only in a target span exhibiting one phenomenon. To evaluate both canonicalization success and semantic preservation, we propose Semantic-preserving Exact-match Normalization Accuracy (SENA), which combines exact agreement between independently canonicalized pair members with a pair-relative BERTScore constraint against information loss. We further introduce the Taxonomy-guided Agent Canonicalization Skill (TACS), which provides LLM agents with phenomenon descriptions and explicit canonicalization contracts. Experiments with five baseline configurations and three TACS-based agents show that the agents outperform the baseline systems on shared languages. ETC-Bench provides a systematic testbed for evaluation-side transcript canonicalization across languages.

</details>


### [68] [Agentic Data Sampling Framework for Multilingual ASR via Linguistic-Aware Error-Driven Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36538)
*Zhiheng Qian,Junhao Du,Jing Peng,Hanqi Li,Kunyang Peng,Feng Xue,Lu Chen,Shuai Wang,Kai Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出语言感知的智能体数据采样框架，将采样LLM智能体引入多语言ASR训练回路，依据语言学评分规则分析错误并自适应选择域/样本/词元级采样策略，在12种Common Voice语言上以Whisper-medium为骨干，优于全量训练且更省数据与算力、收敛更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言ASR中数据采样对性能至关重要，但现有采样策略依赖耗时的人工启发式设计，缺乏自适应性与可解释的语言学依据。

Method: 构建linguistic-aware agentic数据采样框架：LLM智能体由语言学基础的评分规则（rubric）驱动，从音系、形态、句法三个层面分析ASR错误；结合反馈、训练统计、预算约束与算法画像，选择域级、样本级、词元级采样策略，将WER/CER等评估结果转化为自适应数据选择决策而非仅作终点指标。

Result: 在12种Common Voice语言、Whisper-medium骨干上，方法在使用更少训练数据与更低计算资源的情况下性能优于全量数据训练，并相较传统采样策略显著加速训练收敛。

Conclusion: 以LLM智能体替代人工启发式实现语言学驱动的自适应多语言ASR数据采样，可提升数据与算力效率并加快收敛，为多语言ASR训练中的数据选择提供新范式。

Abstract: Multilingual automatic speech recognition (ASR) has achieved rapid progress with large-scale pretrained models and multilingual speech corpora. Previous studies have proved the pivotal role of data sampling in multilingual ASR performance. However, existing sampling strategies largely rely on time-consuming manual, heuristic-based designs. To address this, we propose a linguistic-aware agentic data sampling framework that introduces a data sampling LLM agent into the multilingual ASR training loop. The agent is driven by a linguistically grounded rubric that analyzes ASR errors from phonological, morphological, and syntactic perspectives, and uses the resulting feedback together with training statistics, budget constraints, and algorithm profiles to select domain-level, sample-level, and token-level sampling strategies. This design turns evaluation results into adaptive data-selection decisions rather than treating WER/CER as final endpoints only. We conduct experiments on twelve Common Voice languages using Whisper-medium as the ASR backbone. The results show that our method achieves performance better than full-data training while using less training data and lower computational resources, and substantially accelerates training convergence compared to conventional sampling strategies.

</details>


### [69] [Structured Maps for Landmark-Based Route Instructions with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36582)
*Mifu Matsumoto,Yuki Nakanishi,Tomoya Hashiguchi,Hiroaki Ohshima*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出用SVG结构化文本编码地图空间特征，用于路径导航中的地标选择任务；在由人工路线描述与OpenStreetMap构建的新数据集上评测5个LLM，结构化文本输入表现与PNG图像输入相当或更优，任务微调后小型本地模型也能高效利用地图布局取得高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成基于地标的路径指引需同时理解地图几何与人类地标偏好；LLM语言能力强，但如何为地图数据设计合适的输入结构尚无定论，需要一种既能承载空间/语义信息又轻量的表示方式。

Method: 以SVG格式构建结构化文本表示，编码地图空间特征，并保持与渲染地图图像的几何对应关系，使模型可直接访问空间与语义数据；基于人工书写的路线描述与OpenStreetMap数据构建新数据集；在“转弯处地标选择”和“目的地周边地标选择”两项任务上评测五个LLM，并与PNG图像输入对比，同时进行任务特定微调。

Result: 结构化文本输入的性能与图像（PNG）输入相当或更好；任务特定微调可使较小的本地LLM有效利用地图布局，达到较高的地标选择准确率。

Conclusion: 结构化文本是面向人类导航的空间推理的一种高效、轻量接口，可作为地图数据输入LLM的可行方案。

Abstract: To generate landmark-based route instructions, models must understand both map geometry and human landmark preferences. Although large language models (LLMs) show great abilities in language tasks, the best way to structure map data for these models is not yet established. This paper proposes a structured text representation for landmark selection tasks in route navigation, utilizing SVG format to encode spatial features. The structured text maintains a geometric connection with rendered map images, allowing language models to directly access spatial and semantic data. Using a new dataset created from human-written route descriptions and OpenStreetMap data, we evaluate models on two tasks: landmark selection at turns and around destinations. Experiments with five LLMs show that the proposed structured text input performs as well as or better than image-based PNG inputs. In addition, task-specific fine-tuning enables smaller local LLMs to effectively leverage map layouts to achieve high selection accuracy. These results demonstrate that structured text is an efficient and lightweight interface for spatial reasoning in human-oriented navigation.

</details>


### [70] [Japanese Discourse Structure Analysis Based on Segmented Discourse Representation Theory Using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36667)
*Satomu Tomisaki,Yosuke Mikami,Amane Watahiki,Hitomi Yanaka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文用LLM翻译构建日语SDRT语料，并比较提示与微调模型，发现英日篇章解析性能相当，且模型主要依赖上下文篇章关系结构而非话语标记或从句内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于SDRT的篇章解析对刻画对话结构至关重要，但既有研究集中于英语；由于缺乏日语SDRT语料，其在日语上的有效性尚不明确。

Method: 借助LLM翻译现有英语SDRT语料以构建日语语料库；对比基于提示（prompt-based）与微调（fine-tuned）的LLM解析器；并针对话语标记与上下文输入开展消融分析。

Result: 英日模型性能相当，提示与微调方法均得到可比结果；消融显示所考察的多数日语话语标记影响很小，预测严重依赖上下文的篇章关系而非上下文从句内容。

Conclusion: SDRT篇章解析具有跨语言适用性；尽管英日类型学差异显著，模型对篇章关系结构的依赖可解释二者相当的表现。

Abstract: Discourse parsing based on Segmented Discourse Representation Theory (SDRT) is crucial for capturing the structural dynamics of dialogues. However, most prior research focuses on English, and its effectiveness for Japanese remains unclear owing to the lack of Japanese SDRT corpora. To address this issue, we construct Japanese SDRT corpora by translating existing English SDRT corpora using an LLM. Through a comparative evaluation of prompt-based and fine-tuned LLMs, we show that the models achieve comparable performance between English and Japanese, suggesting the cross-lingual applicability of SDRT-based discourse parsing. In order to investigate why the English and Japanese parsers achieve similar performance, we conduct ablation analyses on discourse markers and contextual inputs. Our results reveal that most of the Japanese discourse markers we examined have little impact, and the models' predictions depend heavily on contextual discourse relations rather than on the content of the context clauses. This structural reliance might explain the comparable performance despite the substantial typological differences between English and Japanese.

</details>


### [71] [A Quantitative Approach to Asch Conformity in Frontier Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36668)
*Senthil Rajasekaran,Stylianos Loukas Vasileiou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将Asch从众实验从"多题单次采样"改为"单题重复采样"，利用LLM的概率性得到答案分布，并比较制造共识前后的分布差异，从而定量刻画大模型的从众行为及其条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究主要证明LLM存在从众现象，但多为在题目集上各采样一次，缺乏对从众程度与触发条件的定量刻画。作者希望在此方法上增加量化层次。

Method: 对一个前沿LLM就同一问题重复多次采样，获得答案的经验分布；随后向模型呈现人为构造的共识（错误多数意见），再次重复采样得到新分布；通过分布到分布（distribution-to-distribution）的对比分析一致性变化。

Result: 实验证实前沿LLM确实会从众，与既有文献一致；分布层面的比较进一步给出了关于何种条件导致从众以及从众程度的额外定量证据。

Conclusion: 重复采样+分布对比是一种有效的量化从众测量框架，可补充传统题目集采样的不足，为理解LLM从众的条件与幅度提供更细粒度的数据。

Abstract: The Asch conformity experiment has recently gained considerable attention as a way of measuring conformity in large language models (LLMs). While previous studies have focused on demonstrating conformity in LLMs, our work adds a further quantitative layer to this approach. Instead of sampling a frontier LLM on a suite of questions, our approach samples a single question repeatedly, leveraging the probabilistic nature of LLMs to produce a distribution of answers.
