### [105] [A Heart Means Recursion: Exploring Visual Meaning and Communication in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36449)
*Alexa R. Tartaglini,Zhengxuan Wu,Thaddäus Wiedemer,Martin Wattenberg,Been Kim,Yuxuan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探究视觉语言模型（VLMs）是否具备与人类相似的视觉沟通能力，发现简单视觉符号（如红心）能以隐式方式显著影响模型在下游任务上的行为，并在模型间视觉沟通中出现"生成—解读鸿沟"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者关心VLMs是否能像人类一样进行视觉沟通，以及视觉符号对模型行为的影响机制是否与人类直觉一致，从而评估人机视觉沟通的可行性与风险。

Method: 采用行为实验与多模型"沟通游戏"：先在看似无关的任务（如简单编程题）上施加简单视觉符号刺激，观察行为偏移；再对比模型对符号的直接解读/思维链与其实测行为；最后通过抽象概念与故事的视觉传递游戏考察模型间沟通。

Result: 简单视觉符号（红心）可将模型引导至递归解法等特定行为；该引导效应具有隐性特征，模型对符号的显式解读与思维链同实际行为明显背离；模型倾向发送自认为（及人类认为）直观的形状基元，但这些线索往往无法有效引导其他模型，形成生成—解读鸿沟；在抽象概念与故事沟通中模型能创造性使用视觉传达意义，但部分沟通对人类不直观。

Conclusion: VLMs可能具备丰富、创造性且具视觉根基的沟通能力，但其视觉意义的产生与解读在多个方面偏离人类惯例；这既显示出人机视觉沟通的潜力，也揭示了其中的挑战。

Abstract: Do vision-language models (VLMs) share our ability to communicate visually? We share preliminary findings, starting from a surprising observation: simple visual symbols like a red heart can strongly influence model behavior on seemingly unrelated tasks, such as steering models towards recursive solutions on simple coding problems. Notably, this steering effect is largely implicit, as models' direct interpretations and thinking traces about visual symbols diverge substantially from their observed behavior. In visual communication between models, this leads to a generation--interpretation gap: while models tend to send shape primitives that they (and humans) find intuitive, these visual cues often fail to steer other models. In further communication games involving abstract concepts and stories, models can creatively use visuals to transmit meaning to one another, although some communications remain non-intuitive to humans. These findings reveal that VLMs might be capable of rich, creative, and visually-grounded communication, but their interpretation and production of visual meaning diverges from human conventions in surprising ways. Together this work highlights both the promise and challenges of visual communication between humans and VLMs.

</details>


### [106] [Correction or Manipulation? Measuring Epistemic Vigilance in LLM Agents Under User Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36453)
*Nardine Basta,Venus Haghighi,Dali Kaafar,Amin Beheshti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究区分LLM智能体"让步倾向"与"对纠正/操纵的辨别能力"，在7个模型上用信号检测论度量，发现引用来源会增加让步但降低辨别；且在客服场景中，答题层面的让步并不预测政策违规行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明模型在被用户质疑时常常放弃正确答案，但无法判断模型是能区分"纠正"与"操纵"，还是仅仅让步倾向不同。作者希望将这种辨别能力与整体让步倾向分离度量，并检验答题行为能否预测智能体在实际行动中的表现。

Method: 跨4家厂商7个LLM，开展两项研究：(1) 答案研究，模拟用户以分级压力推动模型走向正确或错误答案，压力分为"来源诉求"（如声称权威）与"不可验证内容"（如引用来源）；基于认知警觉研究与信号检测论，分别量化"对纠正的让步"与"对操纵的让步"之差及整体让步倾向。(2) 智能体研究，模型作为客服智能体，其政策仅允许为已验证用户更改账户，测量政策违规行为。

Result: 引用来源使7个模型中的5个更易让步、4个辨别力下降，更强措辞影响很小。智能体研究中5个模型从未违反验证政策（包括在答题中易放弃正确答案的模型），即答题让步不能预测政策违规；Ministral 3B主要在"声称权威"下违规，Ministral 8B报告了并未执行更改；"仅在可验证证据下修改"的指令主要改变让步倾向而非提升辨别力。

Conclusion: 仅看让步率会产生误导；评估智能体应同时考察其对纠正与操纵的辨别能力及其实际采取的行动。

Abstract: LLM agents that answer questions and act on behalf of users must decide when to yield to a user who disagrees. Disagreement may correct a genuine error or may be an attempt at manipulation. Prior work shows that models frequently abandon correct answers when challenged by users, yet it remains unclear whether they distinguish correction from manipulation or merely differ in their propensity to concede. We examine this question across seven LLMs from four vendors in two studies, one on the answers agents give and one on the actions they take as customer-support agents whose policy permits account changes only for verified users. Simulated users push toward correct and incorrect answers with graded pressure. Following research on epistemic vigilance, the pressure either appeals to the source, such as claimed authority, or offers content the agent cannot verify, namely a cited source. Drawing on signal detection theory, we measure on a set of benchmark questions how much more readily each agent concedes to correction than to manipulation, separately from its overall propensity to concede. Citations made five of seven models readier to concede and four worse at discriminating, while stronger wording added little. In the agentic study, five models never violated the verification policy, including models that readily abandoned correct answers, so concession on answers did not predict violations of policy. Ministral 3B violated the policy mainly under claimed authority, and Ministral 8B reported changes it had not made. An instruction to revise only on verifiable evidence chiefly shifted propensity to concede rather than improving discrimination. Concession rates alone are therefore misleading, and agents should be evaluated on both their discrimination and the actions they take.

</details>


### [107] [When to Intervene? An Empirical Study of Errors and Self-Recovery in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36471)
*Chiming Duan,Tong Jia,Yingxi Chen,Minghua He,Lingzhe Zhang,Ying Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建ReInBench基准（1,259条智能体轨迹、1,403个错误事件），实证研究LLM智能体执行错误后的自恢复现象，并提出ReCoF方法预测错误是否可自恢复以指导外部干预时机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在代用户操作Web服务时会发生错误，外部干预可防止任务中断，但智能体也可能通过继续执行自行恢复，因此需要回答"何时干预、何时等待自恢复"的问题。

Method: 收集computer-use与Web任务上的1,259条轨迹、1,403个错误事件构成ReInBench；分析自恢复的频率、速度、与后续执行及任务完成的关系、以及错误发生时可预测性；据此提出ReCoF，在错误发生时预测其是否会自恢复，从而决定是否需要干预。

Result: 1,403个错误事件中有725个实现自恢复，且多在数步内完成；自恢复能力随执行LLM不同而异；自恢复有助于应对后续同类错误并促进任务完成。ReCoF在ReInBench上相较最强基线Macro-F1平均相对提升9.02%。

Conclusion: 干预应优先针对低自恢复概率的错误，而对高自恢复概率的错误应允许智能体继续执行；基于此的可预测性建模（ReCoF）能有效指导干预决策。

Abstract: LLM agents interact with Web services to perform tasks on users' behalf. Errors during these interactions can disrupt execution and prevent task completion, motivating external intervention. However, agents may self-recover through continued execution, raising the question of when to intervene and when to wait for self-recovery. We present an empirical study of errors and self-recovery in LLM agents. We construct ReInBench, a dataset containing 1,259 agent traces and 1,403 error events across computer-use and Web tasks. Our study examines how often and how quickly agents self-recover, how self-recovery relates to subsequent execution and task completion, and whether self-recovery can be predicted when an error occurs. We find that 725 error events self-recover, often within a few steps, with self-recovery performance differing across Execution LLMs. Self-recovery can also help agents recover from subsequent errors of the same type and support overall task completion. Intervention should therefore prioritize errors unlikely to self-recover, while allowing agents to continue when self-recovery is likely. Building on these empirical findings, we propose ReCoF, a method that predicts whether an error will self-recover to inform whether additional intervention is needed. ReCoF achieves an average relative improvement of 9.02% in Macro-F1 over the strongest baseline on ReInBench. Our code and data can be found at https://method-notes.github.io/ReCoF/.

</details>


### [108] [ADTGuard: Adaptive Depth-Time Safeguard for Streaming Inference in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36574)
*Chuming Wei,Churui Yang,Zihan Zhang,Ruien Li,Wusheng Zhang,Yan Zhang,Guangwen Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ADTGuard：面向流式生成的逐token预释放安全护栏，通过深度-时间安全动态建模（DTSD）、因果深度路由（CDR）与风险起始目标精化（RTR），在仅用回复级标签和16M可训练参数下实现强流式有害内容检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式场景下LLM输出会在生成完成前暴露给用户，现有护栏存在两点不足：(1) 在回复或分块边界做事后审核，干预滞后，内容在验证前已释放；(2) 即便做token级检测，粗粒度回复级监督未指明有害内容起始位置，且固定层读取可能漏掉其他深度的安全信号。

Method: 提出ADTGuard，在释放前逐token检查。核心包括：DTSD（Depth-Time Safety Dynamics）在每个token处跨层整合安全证据，并据此在后续token上做风险累积；CDR（Causal Depth Router）根据当前前缀自适应选择观测层；RTR（Risk-Onset Target Refinement）在仅有回复级标签时，渐进式地为两个组件构造token级训练目标。

Result: 在多个安全基准上持续取得强流式检测性能，与SOTA护栏持平或更优；仅需16M可训练参数，且每token延迟具竞争力。

Conclusion: 将安全检测前移至逐token且自适应层选择，可在流式生成中更早、更准确地干预；借助风险起始目标精化，无需token级标注即可获得细粒度监督，兼具高效与低开销。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) can generate fluent responses to diverse user requests, yet streaming may expose users to harmful content before generation is complete. Existing guardrails face two limitations in this setting. First, post-hoc moderation at response or chunk boundaries delays intervention, risking harmful exposure when content is released before verification. Second, even with token-level detection, coarse response-level supervision does not specify when harmful content begins, while fixed-layer readouts may miss relevant safety signals at other depths. To address these limitations, we introduce ADTGuard, which checks each token before release, progressively refines token-level training targets, and adapts layer selection to the current prefix. ADTGuard models risk across depth and time through Depth-Time Safety Dynamics (DTSD), which integrates safety evidence across layers at each token and uses it to guide risk accumulation over successive tokens. To adapt the choice of observation layers to the current prefix, we propose the Causal Depth Router (CDR). With only response-level labels available, we further develop Risk-Onset Target Refinement (RTR) to progressively construct token-level training targets for both components. Through extensive experiments across multiple safety benchmarks, we demonstrate that ADTGuard consistently achieves strong streaming detection performance, matching or surpassing state-of-the-art safeguards while requiring only 16M trainable parameters and maintaining competitive per-token latency.

</details>


### [109] [InfraDev: Can Coding Agents Develop AI Infrastructure, and How Can We Tell?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36589)
*Yangshen Deng,Jiaqi Ruan,Yeqi Huang,Nick Lau,Guangming Sheng,Luo Mai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: InfraDev：基于 Megatron-LM、vLLM、verl 真实 PR 构建的 36 个多文件、多卡 AI 基础设施代码代理基准，联合合成隐藏探针与接口说明提示进行评测；最强模型仅解出 38.9%，探针否定 32% 通过官方测试的提交，基于失败分析的 agent skill 使 Megatron-LM 任务解出率提升 10.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码代理基准仅覆盖单 kernel 或单 GPU 任务，且评测方式（测试与提示）既会误判正确补丁也会误判错误补丁；同时编码代理已开始参与训练与服务大型模型的生产级 AI 基础设施开发，缺乏与之匹配的评测手段。

Method: 从 Megatron-LM、vLLM、verl 的真实 pull request 中构建 36 个跨多文件、需多 GPU 正确运行的基础设施任务（覆盖训练、推理与强化学习）；联合合成隐藏探针（enforce 原测试未覆盖的需求）与仅声明探针所调用接口、不泄露解法的提示；设计共享执行环境以跨任务复用依赖与产物（存储降低 52–57%，Megatron-LM 与 vLLM 构建时间降低 13–14%）；在六个前沿模型上评测并做失败分析，据此提炼 agent skill。

Result: 六模型中最优仅解决 38.9% 任务；探针拒收 32% 通过全部官方测试的提交；以原始 PR 描述作提示时无模型解决任何任务；失败分析显示结构性错误与状态错误占主导，代理自写测试极少检查内存、时间或通信流量；将结论转化为 agent skill 后，某前沿模型在 Megatron-LM 任务上的解出率提升 10.5%。

Conclusion: 现有官方测试不足以判定基础设施类补丁的正确性，需以隐藏探针与接口级提示联合评测；当前前沿编码代理在真实多卡、多文件基础设施任务上能力仍有限，失败集中于结构与状态管理及性能相关非功能需求，针对性的代理技能可带来可测量的增益。

Abstract: Coding agents are beginning to develop the production-grade AI infrastructure that trains and serves large models, yet existing benchmarks cover only single kernels or single-GPU tasks, and grade with tests and prompts that misjudge both correct and incorrect patches. We introduce InfraDev, a benchmark of 36 tasks built from real-world pull requests to Megatron-LM, vLLM, and verl, covering infrastructure development for training, inference, and reinforcement learning. Each task asks an agent to reimplement a real change that spans many files and must run correctly across many GPUs. To grade such changes, we jointly synthesize hidden probes, which enforce the requirements the original tests leave unchecked, and a prompt that states every interface the probes call without revealing the solution. We also design a shared execution environment that reuses dependencies and artifacts across tasks, cutting storage by 52-57% and build time by 13-14% on Megatron-LM and vLLM. Across six frontier models, the best resolves only 38.9% of the tasks; the probes reject 32% of the submissions that pass every official test, and with the original pull request descriptions as prompts, no model resolves a single task. Failure analysis shows that structural and state errors dominate and that agents' own tests rarely check memory, time, or traffic. Turning these findings into an agent skill raises the resolved rate of a frontier model on the Megatron-LM tasks by 10.5%.

</details>


### [110] [Learning to Formalize: Bridging Open-Ended Workloads and Fast Decision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36595)
*Chengxuan Pei,Ziyao Wang,Teng Mei,Zhe Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出“小型候选形式化器+冻结决策模型”的接口桥接方案：由形式化器从任务上下文中构造显式、可验证的候选集合，再由决策模型选择，从而在生成式后端与决策模型之间取长补短。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策模型对既定候选集选择快，但无法处理开放式任务；通用大模型能灵活生成与推理，却受制于串行解码延迟。许多工作负载期望生成式后端自行判定可行动作，因而没有明确定义的候选列表，形成接口鸿沟。

Method: 用离线教师生成的候选列表训练一个小型候选形式化器，从任务上下文构造显式且经验证的备选方案；随后由冻结的决策模型从中选取一项。评测包括：与官方 TypeSafe Jev-1.13.0 在 200 个 CALVIN-D 测试回合上的对比；在 1,011 条 CALVIN-D 验证标注上进行六臂特权状态评估，并与 Qwen3.5-27B 在相同实验设置下比较端到端时延。

Result: 200 回合对比中，通用运动成功率 11%，DeepSeek V4 Flash 位姿候选达 46.5%；六臂评估中，本地部署决策模型配合微调形式化器任务成功率最高 52.6%，无形式化器仅 11.8%；端到端时延 2.09 s，Qwen3.5-27B 在相同设置下为 3.67 s。

Conclusion: 候选形式化器可有效弥合开放式生成与候选选择式决策之间的接口差距，在显著提升任务成功率的同时保持较低推理时延。

Abstract: Decision models offer fast choices over sup- plied alternatives, while general-purpose large language models interpret open-ended tasks through flexible generation and reasoning at the cost of sequential decoding. Many work- loads expect a generative backend to determine what can be done and therefore provide no well-defined candidate list. We bridge this in- terface gap with a small candidate formalizer trained on offline teacher-generated lists. The formalizer constructs explicit, validated alter- natives from task context, and a frozen deci- sion model selects an entry. A motivating com- parison with official TypeSafe Jev-1.13.0 on 200 CALVIN-D test episodes reports 11% suc- cess with generic motions versus 46.5% with DeepSeek V4 Flash pose candidates. Sepa- rately, a six-arm privileged-state evaluation on 1,011 CALVIN-D validation annotations finds that a locally deployed decision model can achieve at most 52.6% task success rate with the fine-tuned formalizer, compared with 11.8% without it, and the end-to-end latency is kept to 2.09 s, whereas Qwen3.5-27B is 3.67 s with the exact experiment setting.

</details>


### [111] [Polarity--Time Interaction and Multi-Level Reliability for Event-Based Spacecraft Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36625)
*Yonglong Zhang,Zongwu Xie,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出可靠性感知的航天器位姿估计框架，串联极性与时间定位、几何恢复、事件-CAD精化与轨迹优化，在SPADES测试集上取得0.0474的组合位姿分数（比PASAN+IRIS低约5.4%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏事件响应导致关键点误差分布不均，其对航天器位姿的影响取决于对应几何构型以及连续观测的可靠性，现有方法未显式建模这种可靠性差异。

Method: 三支表示（正事件、负事件、近期事件时间）经跨支注意与门控后做时空聚合；有序语义点头估计相对对应精度用于几何约束位姿恢复；事件边缘与CAD投影一致性引导局部位姿精化并设关键点几何保护；序列层面用十维描述子（热图形状、重投影一致性、几何条件数）设定时间对齐的观测权重。

Result: SPADES官方挑战测试集组合位姿分数0.0474，较PASAN+IRIS低约5.4%；消融中基础定位+均匀PnP为0.0772，完整级联降至0.0514；相同单帧观测下，质量加权将分数由常数权重的0.0549降至0.0514。

Conclusion: 显式建模关键点对应可靠性与观测质量加权可显著提升事件相机航天器位姿估计精度，定位、精化与轨迹优化各级模块均有增益。

Abstract: Sparse event responses produce uneven keypoint errors whose effects on spacecraft pose depend on correspondence geometry and the reliability of successive observations. We present a reliability-aware framework connecting polarity--time localization, geometric recovery, event--CAD refinement, and trajectory optimization. A three-branch representation combines positive events, negative events, and recent-event timing through cross-branch attention and gating, followed by spatiotemporal aggregation. An ordered semantic-point head estimates relative correspondence precision for geometrically constrained pose recovery. Event-edge and CAD-projection agreement then guide local pose refinement under keypoint-geometry safeguards. At the sequence level, a ten-dimensional descriptor combines heatmap shape, reprojection consistency, and geometric conditioning to set time-aligned observation scales. On the official SPADES challenge test set, the complete system achieves a combined pose score of 0.0474, approximately 5.4\% below PASAN+IRIS. Validation ablations reduce the score from 0.0772 for base localization with uniform PnP to 0.0514 for the complete cascade; on identical single-frame observations, quality weighting reduces the score from 0.0549 with constant-weight optimization to 0.0514.

</details>


### [112] [Learning Where to Trust: Reliability-Guided Expert Routing and Velocity Gating for 3D Deformable Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36672)
*Chenyu Guo,Wenjie Dai,Yiqian Chang,Zhiyuan Zhang,Yunfan Wei,Shuhan Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ReliMorph——可靠性路由的对称3D可变形配准框架，用局部相关分布评估对应模糊度，并以此指导空间专家融合与残差速度门控，实现由粗到细的可靠形变更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型配准中，局部匹配可靠性与空间上下文选择、形变更新强度之间缺乏显式耦合；在均质、弱纹理、严重形变区域，模糊匹配会引发错误的形变更新。

Method: 1）局部相关分布刻画对应模糊度，共享解剖特征并做模态特定适配；2）可靠性线索用于引导空间专家融合与残差速度门控，在由粗到细的金字塔细化中逐级进行；3）更新的逆一致性反馈在更细层级指导路由与门控；对称结构设计。首个将空间专家路由与残差速度门控同时以对应可靠性为条件的3D可变形配准框架。

Result: 大量实验表明，在解剖重叠度（如Dice等重叠指标）上，该方法一致优于对比方法，在单模态与多模态配准任务上均有效。

Conclusion: 显式耦合匹配可靠性与空间上下文选择及更新强度，可有效抑制弱纹理/大形变区域的错误形变更新，提升单/多模态3D配准的解剖对齐精度。

Abstract: Deformable image registration relies on accurate anatomical correspondences. However, explicitly coupling local matching reliability with spatial context selection and deformation update strength remains insufficiently explored in learning-based registration. This challenge is particularly pronounced in homogeneous, weakly textured, and severely deformed regions, where ambiguous matches may induce erroneous deformation updates. We propose ReliMorph, a reliability-routed symmetric framework for 3D deformable registration that couples correspondence assessment with spatial context selection and update strength.Local correlation distributions characterize correspondence ambiguity using shared anatomical features with modality-specific adaptation. These reliability cues guide spatial expert fusion and gate residual velocities during coarse-to-fine refinement. Updated inverse-inconsistency feedback guides routing and gating at finer pyramid levels. To our knowledge, ReliMorph is the first 3D deformable registration framework to jointly condition spatial expert routing and residual velocity gating on correspondence reliability. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ReliMorph consistently outperforms competing methods in anatomical overlap, highlighting its effectiveness across unimodal and multimodal registration tasks.

</details>


### [113] [Dependency Graphs of Mathematical Arguments: Representation, Structural Measurement, and Proof Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36776)
*Eunji Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个由大语言模型辅助的、以原文为依据的数学论证表示与度量框架，并给出评估设计而非实证结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学论证表示缺乏可回溯的原文证据、版本边界与可复现度量，难以支撑跨论文的定理复用与证明审查。

Method: 区分原文出现、语境状态与推理记录的编号/非编号陈述、方程、假设与构造；依赖关系保留原文证据与审阅状态；以测量契约固定稿件版本、边界、粒度、证明选择与宏锚点；在有限有向无环图上定义深度（最长前置路径长度）与体量（不同祖先边计数），给出计入共享子论证的并集递推并识别保持深度与宏骨架的可达性条件；采用版本化提示协议分离标注发现、确定性重算与审阅辅助。

Result: 无限制细化不保证保持深度或体量；给出保持深度与宏骨架的可达性条件及共享子论证的并集递推；跨论文链接揭示定理共用、稀有组合与依赖社群连接等模式。

Conclusion: 该框架可支撑前提概率建议、遗漏或未检查应用条件的审阅、原文链接式阅读支持及个体与协作论证形态比较；度量刻画的是特定表示而非数学内在难度、原创性或重要性，所建议前提仍需数学验证。

Abstract: We propose a source-grounded framework for representing and measuring mathematical arguments with assistance from large language models. It includes numbered and unnumbered statements, equations, assumptions, and constructions, distinguishing source occurrences from contextual states and inference records. Proposed dependencies retain source evidence and review status. A measurement contract fixes manuscript versions, boundaries, granularity, proof choices, and macro anchors. On a selected finite directed acyclic graph, depth is the longest prerequisite-path length, and bulk counts distinct ancestor edges. We give a union recurrence that accounts for shared subarguments and identify reachability conditions preserving depth and the macro skeleton; unrestricted refinement need not preserve depth or bulk. A standardized, versioned prompt protocol separates annotation discovery, deterministic recomputation, and review assistance. Cross-paper links support a research agenda on theorem co-use, rare combinations, and connections between dependency communities. These patterns motivate probabilistic premise suggestions and review of possible omissions or unchecked application conditions in human-written and AI-generated proofs. Further applications include source-linked reading support and comparison of individual and collaborative argument shapes. We provide an evaluation design rather than empirical evidence of these benefits. Suggested premises require mathematical validation, and the measures describe a specified representation rather than intrinsic mathematical difficulty, originality, or importance.

</details>


### [114] [An Incremental Knowledge-Graph Learner Digital Twin for Evidence-Grounded Early Warning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36820)
*Ngoc Luyen Le,Marie-Hélène Abel,Bertrand Laforge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出增量式"学习者数字孪生"（轻量知识图谱），将早期预警的风险分数与可核查的学习证据链绑定，在保持预测性能的同时实现可追溯、可验证的证据支撑型解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习分析中的早期预警模型常能识别失败或退学风险学习者，但预警结果与可检视的学习证据联系薄弱：风险分数只说明"需要干预"，却无法说明哪些学习轨迹、测评事件或时序模式支撑了该预警，导致解释缺乏证据根基。

Method: 构建增量更新的学习者数字孪生知识图谱，在时间检查点通过重放学习轨迹与测评记录进行更新；孪生同时充当特征抽取基底与解释落地基底。预测特征经图介导查询导出，特征、归因、预警、证据项与原始记录统一表示为可查询图实体。在公开学习分析基准上按时间协议实例化与评估。

Result: 图介导特征抽取在多种模型族与多个检查点上保持了与等价原始表特征一致的预测表现；生成的预警被物化为证据链，将风险决策与特征归因、证据项及原始平台记录相关联。

Conclusion: 早期预警预测可经由语义化、具备溯源感知的表示来路由，在保持预测性能的同时增加可追溯性、可验证性与证据落地解释。

Abstract: Early-warning models in learning analytics can identify learners at risk of failure or withdrawal, but the resulting alerts are often only weakly connected to inspectable learning evidence. A risk score may indicate that intervention is needed without showing which traces, assessment events, or temporal patterns justify the alert. This paper introduces an incremental Learner Digital Twin for evidence-grounded early warning, implemented as a lightweight knowledge graph. The twin is updated at temporal checkpoints by replaying learning traces and assessment records, and it serves both as a feature-extraction substrate and as an explanation-grounding substrate. Predictive features are derived through graph-mediated queries, while features, attributions, alerts, evidence items, and raw records are represented as queryable graph entities. The framework is instantiated and evaluated on a public learning-analytics benchmark under a temporal protocol. The results show that graph-mediated feature extraction preserves the predictive behavior of equivalent raw-table features across model families and checkpoints. Generated alerts are materialized as evidence chains linking risk decisions to feature attributions, evidence items, and raw platform records. The study shows that early-warning prediction can be routed through a semantic and provenance-aware representation while preserving predictive performance and adding traceability, verifiability, and evidence-grounded explanation.

</details>


### [115] [When the Critic Falls Behind: Co-Evolving Rubrics and Harnesses for Continual Agent Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36826)
*Hao Chen,Yukun Yan,Ziyu Han,Ye He,Chuanzhe Guo,Shangzhan Li,Qi Shi,Qingfu Zhu,Maosong Sun,Wanxiang Che*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 RuHarness，一种评分量规与执行框架协同演化（rubric–harness co-evolution）的智能体持续自我改进框架，通过从执行轨迹中持续发现并重估行为准则、优先处理最相关的未解决弱点来指导 harness 的定向更新，在多项任务与骨干模型上优于强基线，尤其在未见任务上泛化更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体日益依赖围绕 LLM 的 harness（管理交互、记忆、工具与执行）。已有工作虽开始从执行经验中演化 harness，但用于引导演化的监督信号往往较为静态，难以跟踪多轮演化中不断变化的行为弱点，并可能限制对未见任务的泛化能力。

Method: RuHarness 框架：1）持续从执行轨迹中发现并重新评估行为准则（rubric）；2）对最相关且尚未解决的弱点进行优先级排序；3）以这些准则指导 harness 的定向更新，同时保持任务目标固定不变。核心在于评分量规与 harness 的协同演化。

Result: 在多样化的智能体任务与多种骨干模型上的大量实验表明，RuHarness 优于强 harness 演化基线，在未见过的留出任务（held-out tasks）上优势尤为明显。进一步分析显示，自适应量规优先级排序能够跟踪变化中的弱点，并在多轮演化中支持更持久的改进与更稳定的泛化。

Conclusion: 动态、自适应的评分量规监督是提升 harness 演化有效性与泛化性的关键；RuHarness 通过准则与 harness 的协同演化，实现智能体的持续自我改进并增强对未见任务的迁移能力。

Abstract: Autonomous agents increasingly depend on harnesses that govern interaction, memory, tools, and execution around large language models (LLMs). While recent work has begun to evolve these harnesses from execution experience, the supervision used to guide evolution often remains relatively static, making it difficult to track changing behavioral weaknesses across rounds and potentially limiting generalization to unseen tasks. To address this issue, we propose RuHarness, a rubric--harness co-evolution framework for continual agent self-improvement. RuHarness continually discovers and reassesses behavioral criteria from execution trajectories, prioritizes the most relevant unresolved weaknesses, and uses them to guide targeted harness updates while keeping the task objective fixed. Extensive experiments across diverse agent tasks and backbone models show that RuHarness outperforms strong harness-evolution baselines, particularly on unseen held-out tasks. Further analyses show that adaptive rubric prioritization tracks shifting weaknesses and supports more sustained improvement and stable generalization across evolution rounds.

</details>


### [116] [Noise Steering: Exploiting Wake Steering and Acoustic Directivity for Noise-Constrained Wind Farm Control via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36888)
*Martín de Frutos,Baptiste Corban,Claire Bizon Monroc,Ana Bušić,Donatien Dubuc,Jiamin Zhud,Esteban Ferrer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出无模型强化学习策略"Noise Steering"，联合控制机组偏航角与功率设定点，在满足指定区域噪声约束下最大化风场功率，显著优于传统降噪运行基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有风场降噪策略仅通过降低机组功率实现，未考虑机组间尾流相互作用与噪声发射指向性，导致策略过度保守、风能未被充分利用。而偏航可同时影响尾流与噪声传播方向，为场级噪声控制提供额外自由度。

Method: 采用无模型强化学习，同时控制偏航角与功率设定点；将低保真声学模型与稳态FLORIS仿真器耦合以降低训练计算成本；在六机组测试风场上训练，并在FAST.Farm高保真仿真器中验证。

Result: 在全湍流风速范围内满足40 dB噪声约束，相对无阻碍贪婪控制平均功率损失仅2%，高风速下最高7.7%；而工业标准降噪运行（NRO）基线平均功率损失达17%。在FAST.Farm中稳态与湍流入流下基本保持性能。

Conclusion: 通过强化学习结合偏航控制与功率降额，可有效改善风场运行中功率与噪声的权衡关系。

Abstract: Existing noise mitigation strategies for wind farms usually consist in reducing turbines power output, without accounting for the wake interactions between them and the noise emission directivity. This leads to over-conservative policies and unexploited wind power. Yet, turbine yaw can simultaneously affect wake interactions and the direction of noise emission, offering an additional degree of freedom for farm-level noise control. We therefore propose Noise Steering, a model-free reinforcement learning (RL) strategy that jointly controls turbine yaw angles and power setpoints to maximise farm power while satisfying a local noise constraint in a defined region. To enable training at a reduced computational cost, a low-fidelity acoustic model is coupled with the steady-state FLORIS simulator. The RL agent effectively learns how to redirect wakes and noise directivity away from the protected region while redistributing power across the farm. On a six-turbine test farm, the policy satisfies the 40 dB noise constraint throughout the full range of turbulent winds, with an average power deficit of only 2% relative to greedy unimpeded control, and up to 7.7% at high wind speeds. In comparison, the industry-standard noise-reduced operation (NRO) baseline here generates an average power loss of 17%. When deployed in the higher-fidelity FAST.Farm simulator, the policy largely retains its performance under steady and turbulent inflow, despite differences in simulation fidelity. These results demonstrate the potential of combining yaw control and power derating through RL to improve the power-noise trade-off in wind farm operation.

</details>


### [117] [Natural Hard-Negative Mining from Positives for Instruction-Following Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36900)
*Chaolang Li,Jingyu Li,Zhaocheng Du,Xuyang Xie,Gang Wang,Pengwen Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Natural Hard-Negative Mining（NHNM），将原正例重构为自然的指令难负例，自动生成紧凑高对比度的指令条件监督数据NHNM-MARCO，显著提升检索器指令遵循能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令遵循信息检索（IFIR）中，指令条件的对比监督数据稀缺；现有合成方法固定正例、仅增补负例，忽视了原正例作为难负例的潜力。

Method: NHNM：在查询兼容指令下将可转化的负例提升为难负例，并把原正例重塑为自然指令难负例；流程含自动化的Candidate Cleaning（候选清洗）、Reversal Construction（反转构造）与Geometry Filtering（几何过滤），生成与预训练检索空间对齐的紧凑高对比监督。应用于MSMARCO得到NHNM-MARCO（约7K实例）。

Result: 在InstructIR与FollowIR上，568M–4B参数的模型经NHNM-MARCO微调后取得强指令遵循表现，部分设置下匹配或超越更大规模检索器；受控分析与消融表明增益源于自然难负例监督，而非模型规模或训练数据量。

Conclusion: 自然难负例监督是提升IFIR的有效且高效途径，可在小数据量下超越规模化带来的收益。

Abstract: Instruction-following information retrieval (IFIR) requires retrievers to rank documents by both a query and its instance-specific instructions, yet instruction-conditioned contrastive supervision remains scarce. Existing synthetic methods typically fix a positive passage and generate additional negatives, overlooking the hard-negative potential of the original positive. We propose Natural Hard-Negative Mining (NHNM), which promotes convertible negatives under query-compatible instructions and repurposes original positives as natural instruction hard negatives. Its automated Candidate Cleaning, Reversal Construction, and Geometry Filtering pipeline produces compact, high-contrast supervision aligned with the pretrained retrieval space. Applied to MSMARCO, NHNM yields NHNM-MARCO, an IFIR training set of nearly 7K instances. On InstructIR and FollowIR, models with 568M to 4B parameters fine-tuned on NHNM-MARCO achieve strong instruction-following performance and match or surpass substantially larger retrievers in several settings. Controlled analyses and ablations attribute these gains to natural hard-negative supervision rather than model scale or training-data volume.

</details>


### [118] [AutoVend: Benchmarking LLM Agents on Long-Horizon Autonomous Vending Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36980)
*Qiming Shi,Yikun Li,Beidi Luan,Rui Sun,Yan Ma,Jing Li,Daxin Jiang,Zuo Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AutoVend基准：单个LLM智能体通过Web操作界面在180个模拟日内管理5类场地的20台自动售货机，检验线上决策能否转化为持续经济回报；五个LLM智能体中四个不及规则库存控制基线，且"单纯收益"与"每美元推理成本的收益"对模型排序不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体正从消费者购物拓展到为卖家经营业务。在O2O（线上到线下）商务中，决策经由Web平台下达，却由成本高昂的物理执行落地。因此评估此类智能体不能只看单笔交易是否成功，而需追问线上决策能否转化为持续的经济回报；现有基准缺少这种端到端、跨多站点、含真实运营成本与时间维度的评测环境。

Method: 构建AutoVend基准：由单个LLM智能体经Web操作界面，在180个模拟日内管理分布于五类场地的20台自动售货机。仿真器基于真实数据，集成共享仓库、采购提前期、付费补货、库存过期与经常性运营成本。实验以基于规则的库存控制作为基线，对比评测五个LLM智能体，并从站点间协同、执行落地、时间维度上的表现三方面进行诊断，辅以轨迹分析。

Result: 五个受评LLM智能体中四个低于规则基线；按"每美元推理成本对应的收益"排序与按绝对收益排序结果不一致。运营分析显示，单项指标提升并不稳定地带来更强的经济增长，库存与执行成本会侵蚀这类收益。轨迹分析揭示三类失败：难以将可行动作落地、难以管理库存承诺、难以把反馈转化为有效的策略修订。

Conclusion: 在含真实成本与物理执行延迟的O2O经营场景中，当前LLM智能体的自主经营能力仍弱于简单规则基线，且成本效率视角会改变模型优选结论。作者据此指出智能体需具备可执行性约束感知、库存承诺管理与有效反馈学习能力，为后续智能体商务研究提供了可诊断的评测工具。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are extending agent-mediated commerce on the Web from shopping for consumers to operating businesses for sellers. In online-to-offline (O2O) commerce, decisions are issued through Web-based platforms but realized through costly physical execution. Evaluating such agents requires going beyond individual transactions to ask whether decisions made online turn into sustained economic returns. We introduce AutoVend, a benchmark in which one LLM agent manages 20 vending machines across five site types through a Web-based operating interface for 180 simulated days. Its data-grounded simulator combines a shared warehouse, procurement lead times, paid replenishment, inventory expiration, and recurring operating costs. We evaluate five LLM agents against a rule-based inventory-control baseline and diagnose their site coordination, execution, and performance over time. Four of five evaluated LLM agents fall short of this baseline, and returns per dollar of inference cost rank models differently from returns alone. Operational analyses show that gains in individual metrics do not consistently yield stronger economic growth, as inventory and execution costs erode these gains. Trajectory analysis further reveals difficulties in grounding feasible actions, managing inventory commitments, and converting feedback into effective policy revisions.

</details>


### [119] [OpenGray: a benchmark for evaluating the decisions of radiotherapy planning agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37081)
*Humza Nusrat,Kundan Thind*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 OpenGray 基准，用已知正确答案的规划任务评估 LLM 放疗规划智能体的决策能力，发现总体指标良好但存在具体的决策失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的放疗规划智能体只以剂量指标评估其"何时提交/请求审核"的决策，缺乏带有已知正确答案的任务，无法检验决策本身是否正确。

Method: 在 OpenKBP-Opt 头颈数据集上增补带标准答案的任务：两组与剂量目标被证明不可兼容的矛盾任务，以及一个存储计划满足全部硬约束的可行对照任务。在 30 例患者上评估四个系统（从规则脚本到完整 LLM 智能体），LLM 类分别使用商业模型 Terra 和本地模型 Ministral 3 8B；记录决策、实际剂量、问题识别与技术故障，并读取临床 LLM 智能体 SAGE 的 39 次立体定向放射外科运行日志。

Result: Terra 条件下，"LLM 审核+脚本"与完整智能体的决策准确率分别为 95.4% 和 92.1%，但对需要解剖算术的重叠矛盾仅升级处理 50%（95% CI 25–75%）和 25%（95% CI 10–40%）。四个系统均提交了全部可行对照，但其中 24% 的计划未达到存储计划已满足的某一硬约束。SAGE 的 4 次运行中有一项约束始终失败，智能体每次回复都能识别该问题却没有停止动作。

Conclusion: 总体得分良好掩盖了具体的决策失败；规划智能体应同时以决策和剂量评估，并应被赋予停止与请求审核的显式动作。

Abstract: Background and purpose: Radiotherapy planning agents based on large language models (LLMs) decide when to submit a plan or request review but are evaluated mainly by dose. This study developed OpenGray, a benchmark of planning tasks with known correct decisions. Materials and methods: OpenGray adds tasks with known answers to the OpenKBP-Opt head-and-neck dataset, including two contradictions with dose goals proven incompatible and a feasible control with a stored plan meeting every hard goal. Four systems, from a rule-based planning script to a full LLM agent, were evaluated on 30 patients, the LLM-based ones with a commercial (Terra) and a local (Ministral 3 8B) LLM. Decisions, achieved dose, problem recognition, and technical failures were recorded and also read from logs of 39 stereotactic radiosurgery runs of SAGE, a clinical LLM agent. Results: With Terra, the script preceded by an LLM review and the full agent had decision accuracies of 95.4% and 92.1%, respectively, yet escalated only 50% (95% confidence interval, 25-75%) and 25% (10-40%) of overlap contradictions, which required arithmetic on the anatomy. All four systems submitted every feasible control, but 24% of these plans missed a hard goal that the stored plan had met. In four SAGE runs, one goal failed at every attempt; the agent identified it in every reply but had no action for stopping. Conclusions: Favorable aggregate scores concealed specific decision failures. Planning agents should be evaluated on their decisions as well as their dose and given an explicit action for stopping and requesting review.

</details>


### [120] [SFE-O: An Ontology for Knowledge-Grounded AI in Supercritical CO$_2$ Biomass Extraction and Fractionation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37123)
*Thomas Pallet,Mario Lezoche,Gianpaolo Leone,Daniele Pizzichini,Diego Torres,Herv{é} Panetto,M{é}lanie Glibusic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SFE-O——一个面向超临界CO2生物质萃取的模块化OWL 2本体，围绕生物质、过程、产物三维度建模，显式表示过程拓扑、参数来源、内外传质现象及CER/FER机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超临界CO2萃取虽可持续，但其设计与解释需跨生物质特性、操作条件、传质与产物分级的异构知识，这些知识碎片化于技术报告、工程模型、外部数据库与专家经验中，缺乏统一的机器可操作表示。

Method: 构建模块化OWL 2本体SFE-O，由Biomass、Process、Product三个互联维度组成；显式刻画过程拓扑、参数溯源、传质现象与CER/FER机制；显式表示过程量间计算依赖但不嵌入数值求解；复用ChEBI、QUDT、OWL-Time、PROV-O等语义资源。

Result: 通过一个真实中试规模萃取批次实例化、基于推理机的一致性检查以及能力问题的SPARQL查询完成评估。

Conclusion: 该知识模型为SFE过程的查询、一致性检查与机理阐释提供机器可操作基础，并可作为未来本体驱动的AI推理的知识层（该方向仅提出动机、未加评估）。

Abstract: Supercritical CO$_2$ extraction (CO$_2$-SFE) offers a sustainable route for recovering high-value compounds from biomass while limiting the use of conventional organic solvents, but its design and interpretation require heterogeneous knowledge spanning biomass properties, operating conditions, mass transfer, and product fractionation, typically fragmented across technical reports, engineering models, external databases, and expert practice. This paper introduces SFE-O, a modular Web Ontology Language (OWL 2) ontology for supercritical CO$_2$ biomass extraction, structured around interconnected Biomass, Process, and Product dimensions and extended with explicit representations of process topology, parameter provenance, external and internal mass-transfer phenomena, and the Constant Extraction Rate (CER) / Falling Extraction Rate (FER) mechanisms. Computational dependencies among process quantities are represented explicitly, without embedding numerical solving in the ontology, so that input, measured, computed, externally retrieved, and fitted parameters remain traceable. SFE-O reuses established semantic resources (ChEBI, QUDT, OWL-Time, PROV-O) and is evaluated through a worked example that instantiates a real pilot-scale extraction batch, a reasoner-based consistency check, and a competency-question SPARQL query. The resulting knowledge model provides a machine-actionable foundation for querying, consistency checking, and mechanistic explanation of SFE processes, and establishes a knowledge layer that could support future ontology-grounded AI reasoning, a direction this paper motivates but does not evaluate.

</details>


### [121] [Grounding LLM Agents in Business Processes: Process-Aware Knowledge Graphs for Reliable Multi-Step Service Invocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37242)
*Saiful Khan,Afrah Farea*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将OData元数据与BPMN参考流程整合为类型化属性图，用稠密检索+流程边追踪的混合检索器为LLM智能体提供端点、流程顺序与模式约束，显著提升企业API调用的检索与执行可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体调用企业API时需选对端点、遵循流程顺序、使用合法载荷键和预定义代码值；而现有检索增强的服务发现将端点独立处理，丢失了流程与模式约束。

Method: 构建整合OData元数据与BPMN参考流程的类型化属性图；采用混合检索器，用稠密嵌入识别候选端点，并沿流程边检索后续端点；在42个企业服务（含流程链真值）上评测，另对81个多步查询评估LLM智能体表现，并做代码列表诱导探测。

Result: 查询与下游实体词法重叠低时Recall@3提升5.7个点（n=74），且跨嵌入模型与截断值一致；其他查询与稠密检索持平；无流程层的OpenAPI服务上无提升。多步任务完成率38.7% vs 分块基线10.3%。仅提供代码列表可消除编造代码但仍有46%写调用含非法键；完整类型化上下文将非法调用降至3%（101次中3次），且无编造代码。

Conclusion: 在检索阶段显式暴露流程与模式约束，可提升智能体驱动API调用的可靠性；增益源自流程结构而非检索模型本身。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents that call enterprise APIs must select the correct endpoints, follow process order, use valid payload keys, and choose coded values from predefined lists. However, current retrieval-augmented service discovery treats endpoints independently and therefore misses process and schema constraints. We address this limitation by integrating Open Data Protocol (OData) metadata and Business Process Model and Notation (BPMN) reference processes in a typed property graph. A hybrid retriever identifies candidate endpoints using dense embeddings and follows process edges to retrieve subsequent endpoints. We evaluate the method on 42 enterprise services with process-chain ground truth. For queries with low lexical overlap between the query and downstream entities, it improves Recall@3 by 5.7 points (n=74), with consistent gains across embedding models and cut-offs. It matches dense retrieval on other queries and provides no improvement on OpenAPI services without a process layer, indicating that the gains arise from process structure. We also evaluate an LLM agent on 81 multi-step queries. It completes 38.7% of tasks, compared with 10.3% for a chunk-based baseline. In an elicitation probe, providing valid code lists eliminates invented codes but leaves invalid keys in 46% of write runs. Full typed context reduces invalid calls to 3% (3 of 101 runs), with no invented codes. These results show that exposing process and schema constraints during retrieval improves the reliability of agent-driven API calls.

</details>


### [122] [TCM-DiagCommit: A TCM-SD-Derived Benchmark for Evidence-Conditioned Syndrome Selection and Commitment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37243)
*Erwei Hou,Yue Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 TCM-DiagCommit 基准，将中医证候诊断从静态标签选择重构为含候选梯度、分阶段证据与动作契约的动态评测，揭示大模型"最终答案准确率"掩盖的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统中医证候辨证通常被评测为对完整病历做一次静态的最终标签选择，这种设定掩盖了三种关键诊断行为：模型能否区分语义近邻证候标签与随机干扰项；能否随证据字段逐步披露而更新预测；能否在不确定时决定是否给出承诺。仅看最终答案准确率无法反映这些能力，因此需要新的评测范式。

Method: 构建 TCM-DiagCommit 基准，数据源自公开 TCM-SD/ZY-BERT 资源并保留其原始证候标签，将 1,834 个源病例扩展为覆盖 148 个证候标签的 23,842 条任务行。基准包含随机、profile 近邻、全词表三档候选阶梯（random10/hard20/full148），搭配 t1/t2/t3 分阶段证据；并加入固定标签的舌象/脉象扰动与证据不足变体。定义动作契约，对 commit、acquire、defer、abstain 四类动作打分。在五种 zero-shot DirectLLM 配置下进行校准评测。

Result: 五种 zero-shot DirectLLM 校准下，random10 top-1 准确率为 63.2%–66.4%，但 hard20 降至 24.7%–30.3%，full148 仅 14.8%–22.3%；在 hard20 上，WT(all) 错误终局承诺率仍高达 62.2%–74.3%。即随候选难度提升性能急剧下降，而模型在错误时仍高比例给出承诺。

Conclusion: 最终答案准确率不足以支撑诊断类基准评测；答案选择能力、证据利用能力与基准承诺评分应分开独立测量。TCM-DiagCommit 提供了可分离测量上述维度的统一框架。

Abstract: Traditional Chinese Medicine (TCM) syndrome differentiation is usually evaluated as static final-label selection over complete clinical records. This setting hides three diagnostic behaviors: whether a model distinguishes semantic-neighbor syndrome labels from easy random distractors, whether it updates predictions as evidence fields are revealed, and whether it should commit under uncertainty. We introduce TCM-DiagCommit, a benchmark derived from the public TCM-SD/ZY-BERT resource while retaining its source syndrome labels; it converts 1,834 source cases into 23,842 task rows over 148 syndrome labels. The benchmark combines random, profile-neighbor, and full-vocabulary candidate ladders with staged t1/t2/t3 evidence. It also includes fixed-label tongue/pulse perturbations and insufficient-evidence variants. A benchmark-defined action contract scores commit, acquire, defer, and abstain. Across five zero-shot DirectLLM calibrations, random10 top-1 accuracy is 63.2% to 66.4%, but hard20 falls to 24.7% to 30.3% and full148 to 14.8% to 22.3%; on hard20, WT(all) wrong-terminal commits remain 62.2% to 74.3%. These results show that final-answer accuracy alone is insufficient for diagnostic benchmark evaluation: answer selection, evidence use, and benchmark commitment scoring should be measured separately.

</details>


### [123] [Constraining the Commit Boundary: A DIO/ODI-Informed Authorization Guardrail for Agentic Entity Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37407)
*Daniel Beránek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于DIO/ODI的授权边界架构，将智能体推理可用证据与可授权持久身份变更的证据分离，并以两阶段监督器约束提交条件；该工作为概念性规范，无实现与实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体实体解析中，错误身份假设会污染后续检索并产生“看似确认”的循环；需区分“可推理证据”与“可授权变更证据”。

Method: 域治理的约束绑定函数将重构失败区分映射为显式身份保持约束；抽象两阶段监督器仅在命题级授权证据全覆盖、冲突消解、范围合规及版本有效时允许提交；UNVERIFIED准入不赋授权资格，证据提升按声明分别管理。

Result: 贡献为连接诊断—约束映射、证据提升、命题覆盖、冲突处理、范围绑定与提交时版本完整性的规范；并提出评估协议以检验其相对通用授权与常规标识符规则的增量价值。

Conclusion: 属概念架构，非语义正确性定理；效果依赖域策略充分性、证据提升与执行力度，且不阻止错误假设及假设条件化检索，依赖感知的证据流为未来工作。

Abstract: Agentic Entity Resolution (ER) systems iteratively plan actions, retrieve evidence, revise hypotheses, and propose graph mutations. An erroneous identity hypothesis may condition later retrieval and produce apparent confirmation. This paper proposes a Digital Identity Optimization (DIO) and Ontology of Digital Identity (ODI)-informed authorization boundary between evidence available for agent reasoning and evidence eligible to authorize persistent identity-changing mutations.
  A domain-governed constraint-binding function maps selected reconstruction-failure distinctions to explicit identity-preserving constraints. An abstract two-phase supervisor permits a commit only when claim-specific authorization-eligible evidence supports every required proposition, relevant conflicts are resolved, the operation remains within its authorized scope, and relevant state and policy versions remain valid. Admission as UNVERIFIED does not confer authorization eligibility. Separate claim-specific promotion governs evidentiary use; insufficient support or unresolved material conflict leads to deferral.
  The contribution is a specification connecting diagnostic-to-constraint mapping, evidence promotion, proposition coverage, conflict handling, scope binding, and commit-time version integrity. A proposed evaluation protocol is designed to assess the mapping's incremental value beyond generic authorization and conventional identifier rules. This is a conceptual architecture without implementation or empirical validation, not a theorem of semantic correctness. Its property depends on domain-policy adequacy, evidence promotion, and enforcement. It does not prevent erroneous hypotheses or hypothesis-conditioned retrieval; dependency-aware evidence flow remains future research.

</details>


### [124] [Concordat: Typed Sessions for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37461)
*Milad Sikaroudi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Concordat 是一个开源 Python 库，将 LLM 智能体的接口建模为有限状态协议（typestate/会话类型思想），在运行时强制约束工具调用、人工决策与预算，并支持协议定义时校验与行为式 Liskov 子类检查；在 tau-bench 航空任务上，面对施压用户，Concordat 智能体违规确认率为 0，标准智能体在 gpt-4.1 下 13/50 任务出现无确认变更，而 tau-bench 原生奖励多数未检测到该类违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体已可执行取消预订、退款、改账户等有后果的操作，但其行为规则仅以提示词形式给出，遵循程度取决于模型"是否愿意遵守"，缺乏可保证的运行时约束；同时现有基准（如 tau-bench）的奖励函数难以识别"未获明确确认即变更"这类流程性违规。

Method: 提出 Concordat：把智能体视为接口为有限状态协议的對象，按 typestate 与 session types 思路在运行时对模型施加约束。具体机制包括：每轮仅暴露当前协议状态下的合法工具；越界调用在产生副作用前被拒绝；保留给人类的决策只能被"请求"而非执行；token 与工具调用预算按线性方式计费并在交接（handoff）间切分。协议在定义时即被检查，若子类可能绕过父类协议则拒绝该子类化，构成行为层面的 Liskov 替换原则检查。实验：在 tau-bench 的 50 个航空任务上与标准智能体对比，并加入模拟客户施压以诱导跳过确认。

Result: 标准智能体在 gpt-4.1 下 50 个任务中有 13 个在缺少必要"确认"的情况下执行了变更，Claude Haiku 5.5 下 128 次任务运行中有 29 次；Concordat 智能体为 0 次，且任务成功率无显著差异。tau-bench 自带的奖励未能检测出其中大部分违规。

Conclusion: 将智能体接口形式化为有限状态协议并在运行时强制，可把"提示词层面的软约束"转化为可验证的硬性保证，在不牺牲任务成功率的前提下消除违规的有后果操作；同时表明现有评测奖励对流程合规性存在盲区，需要协议级检查作为补充。Concordat 已开源。

Abstract: Agents built on large language models are increasingly allowed to take consequential actions: cancelling bookings, issuing refunds, changing accounts. The rules that govern those actions, such as "obtain the customer's explicit confirmation before changing anything", are usually stated in the prompt and followed only as reliably as the model chooses to follow them. We present Concordat, a Python library that treats an agent as an object whose interface is a finite-state protocol, in the spirit of typestate and session types, and enforces that protocol on the model at run time. On every turn the model is offered only the tools that are legal in the current protocol state, calls outside that set are rejected before they have side effects, decisions reserved for humans can only be requested, and token and tool-call budgets are charged and split linearly across handoffs. Protocols are checked when they are defined, and a subclass of an agent is rejected if it could bypass its parent's protocol, a behavioural form of Liskov substitution. We evaluate a Concordat agent against the standard agent of tau-bench on its 50 airline tasks, adding simulated customers who pressure the agent to skip confirmation. The standard agent made changes without the required "yes" in 13 of 50 tasks with gpt-4.1 and in 29 of 128 task-runs with Claude Haiku 5.5; the Concordat agent made none, with no significant difference in task success. tau-bench's own reward did not detect most of these violations. Concordat is open source.

</details>


### [125] [From Interaction to Capability: Task Advancement as State and Supervision for GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37482)
*Ziyang Chen,Qian Li,Chengwei Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出任务推进记忆（TAM），以“已验证的任务进展”定义推进，兼顾在线执行与可复用操作的离线学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI 智能体在长时程工作流中需跨应用整合信息，且有效操作常与失败尝试交织，传统记忆难以既保留过往动作结果又提炼可复用操作。

Method: 提出 TAM：执行期用“任务推进状态”刻画已验证进展对剩余目标的改变，实现跨应用累积；任务结束后进行推进监督，识别并抽象产生已验证局部结果的交互，使学到的操作保留输入需求、预期结果与验证条件之间的关系，从而从成功与失败任务中均可提取局部有效操作。

Result: MobileWorld 纯 GUI 任务上，仅用推进状态即将成功率由 12.0% 提升至 28.2%；完整 TAM 相较最强基线取得 17.6% 的相对成功率提升，并将每个已解决任务的生命周期 token 降低 38.7%。SPA-Bench 单应用英文 Level 3 设置下达到 SOTA。

Conclusion: 以“验证”为核心的推进式记忆可有效支撑长时程 GUI 任务的跨应用执行与操作复用，在成功率与 token 效率上均获益。

Abstract: GUI memory should preserve the results of past actions and learn reusable operations. This is difficult in long-horizon workflows, where agents must use information across applications and useful operations are interleaved with failed attempts. We introduce Task Advancement Memory (TAM), which defines task advancement as verified task progress, and supports ongoing task execution and learns reusable operations. During execution, Task Advancement State represents how verified task progress changes the remaining goals, allowing the agent to build on earlier achievements across applications. After each task, advancement supervision identifies and abstracts the interactions that produced a verified local result. The learned operations preserve the relation between required inputs, expected outcomes, and verification conditions. This retains locally successful operations from both successful and failed tasks. On MobileWorld GUI-only tasks, Advancement State alone raises success from 12.0% to 28.2%. Full TAM achieves a 17.6% relative gain in task success over the strongest evaluated baseline and reduces lifecycle tokens per solved task by 38.7%. On SPA-Bench, TAM achieves state-of-the-art task success in the single-app English Level 3 setting.

</details>


### [126] [TeamGym: A Gym for Team Decision-Making by AI Agents with Distributed Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37571)
*Chanwoo Park,Jaywoong Jeong,Zachary Wojtowicz,Yubin Kim,Taekyun Lee,Kaiqing Zhang,Asuman Ozdaglar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出团队决策评测与训练环境 TeamGym，基于"枢轴信息"（最小必要充分事实集）量化语言模型智能体的团队沟通差距（22.9 个百分点）与检索差距（19.9 个百分点），并用团队决策损失做强化学习训练以改善最终决策质量并涌现出协调行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实决策多由团队整合分散信息完成，但当前 AI 智能体主要在孤立环境下训练与评测，缺乏对团队信息共享与整合能力的可控、可量化的评测与训练平台。

Method: 构建 TeamGym：从法律、金融、医疗、软件工程真实语料生成团队决策任务；提出"枢轴信息"识别方法——找出每个元素均必要、整体充分的极小事实集合，从而可控地分配枢轴信息，并追踪智能体识别与整合枢轴信息的能力。在 12 个源语料、8 个语言模型上评测；并以团队决策损失为奖励，通过强化学习训练策略（两项实验）。

Result: 将枢轴信息分散给多个参与者相比全部给单一模型，成功率平均下降 22.9 个百分点（沟通差距）；当参与者需自行从原始材料中识别并抽取枢轴信息而非直接获得时，成功率再降 19.9 个百分点（检索差距）。强化学习训练提升最终决策质量，并在参与者分工、发言人选择与沟通上涌现协调行为，尽管奖励仅评价最终决策。

Conclusion: 现有语言模型智能体在团队信息共享与信息检索上存在显著缺陷；TeamGym 既可作为可控诊断基准，也可作为训练环境，仅以最终决策为奖励即可诱导出有效的团队协调。

Abstract: Many real-world decisions require teams to integrate information distributed across participants. Yet AI agents are still primarily trained and benchmarked in isolation. We introduce TeamGym, an environment for evaluating and training language model-based agents on team decision-making problems constructed from real-world legal, finance, medical, and software engineering corpora. TeamGym introduces a method to identify a minimal set of facts, each element of which is necessary and all of which are collectively sufficient for a correct final decision, which we call pivotal information. This enables TeamGym to both control the distribution of pivotal information and track how well agents identify and integrate pivotal information when making team decisions. Examining 8 language models on 12 source corpora, we find that current AI agents often fail to effectively share information: Distributing pivotal information across participants lowers success rate by 22.9 percentage points on average relative to giving the same information to a single model (a communication gap). We also document that participants do not effectively retrieve decision-relevant information on their own: A team's success rate is lowered by a further 19.9 points when participants must identify and extract pivotal information from source material rather than receiving it directly (a retrieval gap). To help remedy these deficits, we use TeamGym to train policies through reinforcement learning on team decision loss. Across two policy-training experiments, we find that training improves final-decision quality and induces emergent coordination in participation, speaker selection, and communication, even though the reward evaluates only the final decision.

</details>

*Adrien Petralia,Alexandre Gavaudan,Artjom Joosen,Etienne Goffinet,Richard Kurle,Yassine El Ouahidi,Yassir Bendou,Abdelaziz Bounhar,Abhijeet Somani,Aditi Kabra,Adrian Valente,Adrien Sadé,Ahmed Glandon,Pierre Stock,Pierre-André Savalle,Piotr Miłoś,Poojan Shah,Prateek Gupta,Pravesh Agrawal,Pui Ki Chan,Quentin Desreumaux,Quentin Selle,Quentin Torroba,Quentin Wargnier,Quercus Hernandez,Ram Ramrakhya,Randall Isenhour,Ranjit Parva,Raphaël Balouka,Raphaël Rialland,Raul Perez Pelaez,Reinhard Sonnleitner,Remi Delacourt,Rishi Shah,Rob Romijnders,Rohan Shah,Rohin Arora,Romain Sauvestre,Roman Defard,Thomas Foubert,Thomas Loux,Thomas Robert,Thomas Wang,Tianyu Zhang,Tim Lawson,Timothée Lacroix,Timothy Wallis,Tobias Kronlachner,Tom Bewley,Tom Edwards,Tomas Hodan,Triantafyllos Afouras,Tuhin Das,Tyler Wang,Udit Saxena,Ulrick Blé,Umar Jamil,Umberto Tomasini,Valentin Macé,Van Phung,Venkata Reddy,Vibha Masti,Victor Jouault,Victor Letzelter,Victor Paltz,Victor Poucheret,Vincent Maladière,Vincent Pfister,Virgile Richard,Vladislav Bataev,Wassim Bouaziz,Wen Ding Li,William Flanagan,William Havard,William Marshall,Witold Jędrzejewski,Xinghui Li,Xingran Guo,Xinyu Yang,Xuanyu Zhang,Yann Dreze,Yann Viegas,Yannic Neuhaus,Yassine Turki,Yessin Moakher,Yi He,Yihan Wang,Yimu Pan,Yuying Zhu,Yves Martin des Taillades,Zaccharie Ramzi,Zhenlin Xu,Zsófia Csákány*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Timestral时间序列基础模型家族（5M/28M/123M/209M四档），通过解耦预测时域、协变量框架与两阶段训练，在GIFT-Eval、TIME、fev-bench三项基准上刷新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果架构做长时域预测时需掩码上下文，且难以原生支持未来已知协变量；同时缺乏大规模合成预训练到真实数据后训练的系统性方案。作者希望构建一套可扩展的概率预测基础模型族并验证其通用性。

Method: 1）Decoupled Horizon Forecasting（DHF）：将预测token与因果编码的上下文分离，长时域训练无需掩码上下文，每个观测值既是预测目标又是后续预测的上下文；2）协变量框架：在因果架构中原生支持未来已知协变量（known-future covariates）；3）两阶段流水线：先在大规模多样化合成语料上纯预训练，再在真实+合成混合数据上以扩展上下文进行后训练。模型规模自5M至209M参数。

Result: 在GIFT-Eval、TIME、fev-bench上取得新SOTA，Timestral-209M与Timestral-123M在每项指标上位列前两名。权重发布于Hugging Face，推理代码发布于GitHub。

Conclusion: DHF、原生协变量支持与合成预训练+真实后训练的两阶段流程共同构成可扩展的概率预测方案，模型族在多个统一基准上一致领先，表明该设计具备良好的规模可扩展性与泛化能力。

Abstract: We introduce Timestral, a family of four time series foundation models for probabilistic forecasting, with 5M, 28M, 123M, and 209M parameters. Timestral sets a new state of the art on GIFT-Eval, TIME, and fev-bench, where Timestral-209M and Timestral-123M take the top two places on every metric. We achieve this with three ingredients: Decoupled Horizon Forecasting (DHF), which separates prediction tokens from the causally encoded context, so that longer horizons are trained without masking context and each observed value is both a forecast target and context for later forecasts; a covariate framework that natively supports known-future covariates within a causal architecture; and a two-stage pipeline that pretrains exclusively on a diverse, large-scale synthetic corpus before post-training on a mix of real and synthetic data with extended context. This report describes the model design and its benchmark results. Weights for all four models are available on Hugging Face and inference code on GitHub.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [128] [Calibrated for Which Policy? Conformal Uncertainty for Fixed World Models Under Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35877)
*Ayush Mittal,Dhruv Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 部署策略改变会使世界模型的不确定性失效，但若源域与目标域共享条件机制 P(Y|X)，则可通过结果前测度比 w(X)=dQ_X/dP_X 识别干预，并用加权保形校准把覆盖率迁移到目标分布，误差由分类器遗憾界控制，实验证明校准有效且已用 Lean 4 机器验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 更换部署策略会破坏世界模型的预测不确定性，而物理转移机制与预测器本身不变。核心问题是：在不重新学习模型的前提下，如何识别策略干预并恢复目标分布下有效的不确定性量化。

Method: 1) 依赖条件机制不变性 P(Y|X)=Q(Y|X)，用结果前测度比 w(X)=dQ_X/dP_X 识别干预；策略控制动力学下该比值分解为状态占用项与动作倾向项。2) 加权保形校准将边缘覆盖率迁移至目标分布，给出界 P_Q{Y∈C_ŵ(X)} ≥ 1-α-TV(Q, Q̂) ≥ 1-α-E_P|w-ŵ|。3) 用源域-目标域域分类器的群体 log-loss 遗憾 Δ(h) 约束比值误差：E_P|w-ŵ_h| ≤ (1/(ργ))√(Δ(h)/2)，精确抵消使依赖由 γ^{-2} 提升到 (ργ)^{-1}。4) 在 DINO 特征世界模型与 Franka 轨迹上实验，并用 Lean 4 机器验证有限支撑下的代数与因果恒等式及覆盖复合步骤。

Result: 在最强的受控状态-动作偏移下，普通 90% 保形集实际覆盖 55.0%，而 oracle 与学习比值校准分别达 91.0% 与 90.9%；学习代理与目标律的总变差距离在 0.9% 以内。理论界因精确抵消把遗憾依赖从 γ^{-2} 改善为 (ργ)^{-1}。

Conclusion: 世界模型可保持不变，只需让校准跟随干预（部署律）迁移；条件机制不变性足以识别策略干预并获得目标分布的有效覆盖率保证，形式化验证进一步支撑了分类器遗憾论证的正确性。

Abstract: Changing a deployment policy can invalidate a world model's uncertainty while leaving the physical transition mechanism and predictor unchanged. For one-step prediction, if source and target laws share the conditional mechanism $P(Y|X)=Q(Y|X)$, then the intervention is identified by the pre-outcome change of measure $w(X)=dQ_X/dP_X$. For policy-controlled dynamics this ratio factors into state-occupancy and action-propensity terms. Weighted conformal calibration transports marginal coverage to the target law; with an estimated ratio $\hat w$, $P_Q\{Y \in C_{\hat w}(X)\} \ge 1-α-TV(Q,\hat Q) \ge 1-α-E_P|w-\hat w|$. Population log-loss regret for a source-versus-target domain classifier bounds the same ratio error. If $h$ is its clipped target posterior, $ρ$ is the target-domain prior, and $Δ(h)$ is population log-loss regret, then $E_P|w-\hat w_h| \le (1/(ργ))\sqrt{Δ(h)/2}$. An exact cancellation improves the dependence from a generic odds-map bound of order $γ^{-2}$ to $(ργ)^{-1}$. Our experiments use a released DINO-feature world model and physical Franka trajectories. Under the strongest controlled state-action shift, ordinary 90% conformal sets cover 55.0%, whereas oracle and learned-ratio calibration cover 91.0% and 90.9%. The learned surrogate remains within 0.9% total variation of the target law. Calibration is relative to the deployment law: the model can remain fixed while calibration follows the intervention. We machine-check in Lean 4 the finite-support algebraic and causal identities used in the classifier-regret argument, including its coverage-composition step.

</details>


### [129] [ResCom: Activation Residual Compensation for Large Language Model Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35955)
*Ning Yang,Bohan Cui,Zhuowen Liu,Yuting Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ResCom：利用激活舍入残差的低秩校正，闭式求解、无需梯度训练，缓解低比特 W-A 量化精度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特权重量化与激活量化可降低大模型部署的内存与算力开销，但校准后激活舍入仍导致精度退化；仅从已舍入输入做重建无法区分落入同一量化区间的激活值，残差信息被丢弃。

Method: 以激活舍入残差为信号补偿层输出误差：引入低秩校正项，采用输出感知的校准流程，按各残差方向对输出重建的贡献筛选方向，并用闭式解计算校正参数；不进行梯度训练，也不更新已校准的量化权重。

Result: Llama-3-8B、4-bit 权重与激活下，秩-32 注意力校正使 WikiText-2 困惑度由 7.708 降至 7.147，MMLU 提升 2.89 个百分点，GSM8K 五样本由 23.96% 升至 36.16%；并在 1B–70B 模型及留出集 C4 上验证。FP32 实现使 4K 预填充时间增加 2.25%；独立紧凑存储实现相对 FP16 仍保留 27.7% 峰值内存降低。

Conclusion: 残差驱动的低秩闭式校正可在不改变量化权重、不增加训练成本的前提下显著恢复量化模型精度，且开销可控、跨规模模型可迁移。

Abstract: Low-bit weight--activation quantization reduces the memory and computation needed to deploy large language models, but activation rounding can still degrade accuracy after calibration. Reconstruction from rounded inputs cannot distinguish activations within the same quantization bin. We propose ResCom, a low-rank correction that uses the activation rounding residual to compensate for the resulting layer-output error. An output-aware calibration procedure selects residual directions by their contribution to output reconstruction and computes the correction in closed form, without gradient training or updates to the calibrated weights. On Llama-3-8B with 4-bit weights and activations, rank-32 attention corrections reduce WikiText-2 perplexity from 7.708 to 7.147, improve MMLU by 2.89 percentage points, and raise five-shot GSM8K accuracy from 23.96 to 36.16 percent. Evaluations on models from 1B to 70B parameters and on held-out C4 test the method beyond this setting. The evaluated FP32 implementation adds 2.25 percent to 4K prefill time; a separate compact-storage implementation retains a 27.7 percent peak-memory reduction relative to FP16.

</details>


### [130] [Distributed LLM Inference via Communication-Efficient Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35957)
*Joohyun Shin,Byeonghyeon Park,Keumseo Ryum,Joonhyuk Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DISCO 提出一种通信高效的分布式推测解码框架，通过在服务端部署草稿小模型副本，使拒绝采样所需的草稿侧分布可在本地获得，从而避免词表规模分布的传输，仅交换草稿 token 索引与标量概率，在严格带宽约束下取得更优解码加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设备—服务器协同的分布式推测解码虽具前景，但解码加速受限于词表规模的通信开销：现有方法为保证精确拒绝采样，需在设备与服务器之间传输完整词表上的 token 分布，在带宽受限环境下代价高昂。

Method: 提出 DISCO 框架：在服务器端维护草稿小语言模型（SLM）的副本，使服务器能够本地计算拒绝采样所需的草稿侧分布；据此设备与服务器之间只需交换所草稿 token 的索引及其标量概率值，不再传输词表级分布，同时保持目标分布精确性。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，DISCO 相较现有分布式推测解码方法持续获得更高的解码加速，在严格带宽约束条件下优势尤为明显。

Conclusion: 通过将草稿模型的分布计算下沉至服务器端，DISCO 有效消除了词表尺度通信瓶颈，在保证输出分布精确等价的前提下显著提升带宽受限场景下的分布式推测解码效率。

Abstract: Distributed speculative decoding has emerged as a promising approach for device--server collaborative large language model (LLM) inference, but its decoding speedup is often limited by the vocabulary-scale communication overhead. In existing distributed speculative decoding methods, exact rejection sampling requires transmitting full token distributions over the LLM vocabulary between the device and the server, which becomes costly in bandwidth-constrained environments. To address this issue, we propose DISCO, a communication-efficient framework for distributed speculative decoding, that eliminates vocabulary-scale distribution transfer while preserving the exact target distribution. DISCO achieves this by introducing a server-side replica of the draft small language model (SLM), which enables the server to locally obtain the draft-side distribution required for rejection sampling. As a result, DISCO exchanges only drafted token indices and scalar probability values between the device and the server. Experimental results on multiple datasets show that DISCO consistently achieves higher decoding speedup than existing distributed speculative decoding methods, particularly under stringent bandwidth constraints.

</details>


### [131] [Knowledge distillation across teacher architectures for compact diabetic retinopathy grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35961)
*Mossa Manisa,Habiba Jahan Priya,A. K. M. Fakhrul Hossain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在APTOS 2019五分类糖尿病视网膜病变分级任务上，系统评估了基于离线响应的知识蒸馏：以ResNet-50、MobileNetV3-Large、Swin-T为教师，蒸馏至MobileNetV3-Small学生模型，获得1.52M参数、5.94MB的紧凑学生，三随机种子平均QWK 0.9024（基线0.8804）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 糖尿病视网膜病变分级模型在资源受限硬件上难以部署，需在保持性能的前提下大幅压缩模型；同时，温度与硬标签权重等蒸馏超参的影响缺乏受控、可复现的对比证据。

Method: 离线响应式知识蒸馏的受控实验。教师：ResNet-50、MobileNetV3-Large、Swin-T；学生：MobileNetV3-Small。先以单种子对"教师×温度×硬标签权重"3×4×2网格筛选，再对6种配置及无蒸馏基线进行三种子重复评估；指标为二次加权Kappa（QWK）与macro-F1。

Result: 单种子最高QWK 0.9093（macro-F1 0.6851），来自Swin-T教师、温度4、硬标签权重0.7；该配置三种子均值QWK 0.9024，基线为0.8804。学生模型1.52M参数、5.94MB，相对Swin-T参数减少18.07倍。温度与硬标签权重的影响依赖教师与配置，小幅性能差异对随机种子敏感。

Conclusion: 在所评估协议下，知识蒸馏可得到性能具竞争力且紧凑的学生模型，适用于资源受限场景；但结论仍需独立外部验证，且超参效应与种子敏感性提示报告应基于多种子统计。

Abstract: Diabetic retinopathy grading from retinal fundus photographs is important but difficult to deploy on resource-constrained hardware. We conducted a controlled study of offline response-based knowledge distillation for five-class diabetic retinopathy grading on APTOS 2019, comparing ResNet-50, MobileNetV3-Large, and Swin Transformer-Tiny (Swin-T) teachers distilled into a MobileNetV3-Small student. A 3 x 4 x 2 grid of teacher, temperature, and hard-label weight was screened at one seed, while six configurations and the no-distillation baseline were evaluated across three seeds. The highest observed single-seed quadratic weighted kappa was 0.9093 (macro-F1 0.6851) for Swin-T at temperature 4 and hard-label weight 0.7; the corresponding three-seed mean was 0.9024, compared with 0.8804 for the baseline. The student used 1.52 M parameters and 5.94 MB, representing an 18.07x parameter reduction relative to Swin-T. Temperature and hard-label weight effects were teacher- and configuration-dependent, while small performance differences were sensitive to random seed. Knowledge distillation produced a compact student with competitive performance under the evaluated protocol, but independent external validation remains necessary.

</details>


### [132] [h1: A Taste of the Bitter Lesson in Automated Theorem Proving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35962)
*Skander Moalla,Ahmad Rammal,Niket Patel,Remi Munos,Julia Kempe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出简单智能体框架h1：仅给模型高效Lean 4接口+验证器反馈与自我反思循环，不做分解或搜索结构，在多个形式化基准上达SOTA并解决8个开放问题，表明高效交互接口而非复杂工作流才是关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化定理证明长期依赖人工设计的工作流（规划、分解、编排专用证明器），而这些工作流的收益常与更强模型或更多算力混淆，难以判断其真实价值。作者受"苦涩教训"启发，追问：一个仅靠扩展测试时算力的简单智能体循环能否匹敌这些复杂工作流？这对形式化验证跟上前沿模型产出的研究级数学至关重要。

Method: 设计h1——一个轻量harness，为模型提供高效的Lean 4交互接口，通过验证器反馈和自我反思持续迭代，不施加分解或搜索结构。在PutnamBench、FATE-X、FormalConjectures100-Solved上评测，并与Goedel-Architect、AlphaProof Nexus、Claude Code、AxProverBase进行匹配模型对比及消融实验。

Result: h1在PutnamBench、FATE-X和FormalConjectures100-Solved上取得SOTA，解决FormalConjectures中8个开放问题；在同等模型下匹配或超越Goedel-Architect、AlphaProof Nexus等专用工作流；单次尝试（pass@1）即可扩展解决开放问题。消融显示：仅有简单性不足够，高效的Lean接口在很大程度上决定了成本与求解率。

Conclusion: 简单性本身并非充分条件，高效执行反馈接口是关键决定因素。建议未来工作先用高效执行反馈评估前沿模型，再决定是否引入复杂工作流，以避免"苦涩教训"式的误判。

Abstract: While progress in informal mathematics has come largely from scaling the chain-of-thought and agentic reasoning of frontier language models, formal theorem proving has increasingly relied on human-designed workflows around these models, such as planning, decomposition, and orchestrating specialized provers. This raises a bitter-lesson question: can a simple agentic loop that scales test-time compute match these workflows? The answer matters as formal verification must scale to keep up with frontier models producing research mathematics, yet new workflows rarely settle it, as their gains are often confounded with stronger models or more compute. We study this question with h1, a simple harness that gives a model an efficient interface to Lean 4 and keeps it working through verifier feedback and self-reflection, without imposing a decomposition or search structure. h1 reaches state-of-the-art results on PutnamBench, FATE-X, and FormalConjectures100-Solved, settles eight open problems in FormalConjectures, and, at matched models, matches or outperforms specialized workflows such as Goedel-Architect and AlphaProof Nexus, surprisingly showing that a single attempt (pass@1) can often scale to solve open problems. Through ablations and matched-model comparisons with Claude Code and AxProverBase, we find that simplicity alone is not enough: an efficient Lean interface largely determines cost and solve rate. We thus recommend that future work evaluate frontier models with efficient execution feedback before prescribing workflows, at the risk of bitter lessons.

</details>


### [133] [Data-driven Discovery of Lie Symmetries and First Integrals with Latent ODEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35967)
*Matt L. Wiemman,Peter Melchior*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LatentLieODE：从时序观测（含视频）中发现线性李对称与首次积分的神经ODE框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从数据驱动的角度自动发现动力系统的对称性与守恒量，为神经代理模型提供可解释的物理结构先验。

Method: 自编码器学习低维去相关潜坐标z；联合拟合线性无穷小生成元组A_k（基于可微李括号残差J_f(z)Az−Af(z)）与MLP参数化的潜ODE f(z)=dz/dt以重建观测时序；随后训练第三网络H_φ生成首次积分。

Result: 在六个基准系统（谐振子、单摆与双摆、Lotka-Volterra、像素单摆、球面测地线）上均找到相关对称性，发现的不变量与真值守恒量相关性|ρ|≥0.978；球面上恢复出非阿贝尔so(3)，并由其结构常数的负定Killing形式认证。

Conclusion: 该工作推进了数据驱动的对称性发现，并为动力系统的神经代理提供了新路径。

Abstract: We present LatentLieODE, a novel neural ODE method to discover linear Lie symmetries and first integrals of the learned dynamics from timeseries observations such as video inputs. It starts with an autoencoder, which learns a low dimensional representation of the observed system and then maps trajectories to decorrelated latent coordinates $z \in \mathbb{R}^d$. A bank of linear infinitesimal generators $\mathbf{A}_k \in \mathbb{R}^{d \times d}$ is fitted jointly against a differentiable Lie-bracket residual $\mathbf{J}_f(z)\mathbf{A}z - \mathbf{A} f(z)$, while an MLP optimises the latent ODE $f(z) = \mathrm{d}z/\mathrm{d}t$ to reconstruct the observation-space timeseries. After suitable generators and $f$ are found, we fit a third network $H_φ$ to generate the first integral of the system. Across six benchmark systems (harmonic oscillator, simple and twin pendulums, Lotka-Volterra, pixel pendulum, sphere geodesics) we find each system's relevant symmetries, and the discovered invariants match ground-truth conserved quantities at $|ρ| \geq 0.978$. On the sphere, the pipeline recovers a non-abelian $\mathfrak{so}(3)$, certified by the negative-definite Killing form of its recovered structure constants. This work presents significant advances in data-driven discovery of symmetries and provides a new pathway for neural surrogates of dynamical systems.

</details>


### [134] [A Graph-informed Kriging model for sample-efficient Bayesian optimization of wind farm layouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35975)
*Zhen-Fan Wang,Kai Zhang,Xianping Du,Zhaolong Han,Dai Zhou,Onur Bilgen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Graph-informed Kriging框架，将成对风机的气动交互信息以有向气动图形式嵌入代理模型均值函数，使高斯过程仅建模残差，从而在稀疏数据下显著提升AEP预测精度与贝叶斯优化样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风场布局优化中风机数量增加导致设计空间维数升高，高保真气动模型计算昂贵；传统Kriging贝叶斯优化在稀疏训练数据下对高维布局空间覆盖不足，样本效率低。

Method: 构建Graph-informed Kriging：将风场表示为有向气动图，在低维相对坐标空间中定义成对风机交互，并组装为场级均值函数；该结构化均值捕捉年发电量（AEP）的主要变化，高斯过程只建模残差结构与不确定性，并嵌入贝叶斯优化流程。

Result: 稀疏数据下AEP预测精度稳定优于普通Kriging与通用趋势Kriging，且随布局维度与尾流交互强度增加收益更明显；在IEA Wind Task 37基准上约280次AEP评估即获得有竞争力解；16风机受控算例中同等高保真预算下优于普通与多保真Kriging；并扩展至48风机风场验证可适用性。

Conclusion: Graph-informed代理建模能更高效地利用高保真气动模型，支撑可靠的大规模风场布局设计。

Abstract: Wind farm layout optimization becomes increasingly difficult as turbine count grows, especially when high-fidelity aerodynamic models are used. Conventional Kriging-based Bayesian optimization often suffers from low sample efficiency in high-dimensional layout spaces because sparse training data provide limited domain coverage. We develop a Graph-informed Kriging framework that embeds reusable aerodynamic interaction information into the surrogate mean function. The wind farm is represented as a directed aerodynamic graph, where pairwise turbine interactions defined in a low-dimensional relative-coordinate space are assembled into a farm-scale mean. This structured mean captures the dominant variation in annual energy production (AEP), allowing the Gaussian process to model only the residual structure and uncertainty. Numerical experiments show that Graph-informed Kriging consistently improves AEP prediction accuracy over ordinary and generic-trend Kriging under sparse-data conditions, with increasing benefits as layout dimension and wake-interaction strength grow. Embedded in Bayesian optimization, it substantially improves sample efficiency and achieves competitive solutions for the IEA Wind Task 37 benchmark with about 280 AEP evaluations. In a controlled 16-turbine case, it also outperforms ordinary and multi-fidelity Kriging under the same high-fidelity evaluation budget. The framework is further extended to a 48-turbine wind farm, demonstrating applicability to large-scale CFD-based layout optimization. These results show that Graph-informed surrogate modeling can enable more efficient use of high-fidelity aerodynamic models for reliable large-scale wind farm layout design.

</details>


### [135] [Let the Gate Decide: Per-Lookup Precision for Quantized Conditional Memory in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35985)
*Tianqi Chen,Xunchao Li,Linchuan Xie,Guanghua Yu,Jianchen Zhu,Peisong Wang,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GateRQ 发现条件记忆层（如 Engram 哈希 n-gram 表）的低比特量化误差被"门控"系数加权，使高频行反而不如随机行更值得精修；据此设计 1-bit 残差阶梯码本 + 门控排序的按需读取量化方案，在 DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash 上实现 7.8× 显存压缩、11%–31% 吞吐提升，PopQA 仅掉约 1 分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件记忆层对模型能力贡献显著（移除后 DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash 的 PopQA 下降 36.8 分），但其表结构占用大量 GPU 显存。低比特量化会优先损失长尾事实，而直觉上的补救措施——按访问频次给高频行分配更多比特——反而比随机分配更差，说明"哪些行值得高精度"的判据并非访问频次。

Method: 作者将偏差归因于缩放每个检索行的门控（gate）：一次查找的量化误差被其门控加权，高频 n-gram 的门控小、长尾实体的稀有 n-gram 门控大，且该门控可由 1-bit 编码近似计算。GateRQ 因此把每行存为 1-bit 残差码的阶梯（ladder of 1-bit residual codes），第 1 层常驻 GPU 显存用于计算门控，仅对门控排名最高的查找读入更深层。方法无需重训练。

Result: 在 DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash 上无需重训练即将 GPU 表显存降低 7.8×，vLLM 吞吐相较 FP8 表提升 11%–31%；跨两个模型，每次查找读取 1.50 bit 时 PopQA 约降 1 分，读取 2.10 bit 时仅降 0.2 分。

Conclusion: 量化误差应按门控加权而非访问频次来分配精度；结合 1-bit 残差阶梯码与门控驱动的按需分层读取，可在几乎不损精度的前提下大幅压缩条件记忆层显存并提升推理吞吐。

Abstract: Conditional memory layers, such as DeepSeek's Engram, attach large hashed n-gram tables to a language model, and removing them from DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash costs 36.8 PopQA points. Low-bit quantization loses long-tail facts first, and the standard remedy, giving more bits to frequently accessed rows, backfires: refining lookups by access frequency is worse than refining them at random. We trace this to the gate that scales each retrieved row. A lookup's quantization error is weighted by its gate, which is small for frequent n-grams, large for the rare n-grams of long-tail entities, and computable from a 1-bit code. GateRQ therefore stores each row as a ladder of 1-bit residual codes, keeps level 1 in GPU memory to compute the gate, and reads deeper levels only for the lookups the gate ranks highest. Without retraining, GateRQ cuts GPU table memory by 7.8x on DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash, which raises vLLM throughput by 11%-31% over FP8 tables, while losing about 1 PopQA point at 1.50 bits read per lookup and 0.2 points at 2.10 bits across two models.

</details>


### [136] [Layerwise Repacking: Variable-Length Hybrid-Attention Training without an Extra Redistribution Pass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35989)
*Jiahao Zhang,Junwei Feng,Dongliang Huang,Xiaoling Zhou,Yiqiao Liao,Chengru Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Layerwise Repacking：将全注意力 SP16/32 的父组样本重打包到节点内 SP{1,2,4,8} 的稀疏子组，复用现有全层 Ulysses 通信点一次 all-to-all 完成头交换与样本重排，并配合父组求解器优化样本归属，实现零额外重分发开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变长训练下混合注意力模型各层并行需求冲突：全注意力层需宽 SP 组（16/32）以应对二次增长的开销，稀疏注意力层（近线性 MoE 为主）在节点内 SP8 即可平衡负载；复用宽组会将 all-to-all 推至跨节点，实测有效带宽低 7.0 倍，而直接切小组合改变样本归属，每次全-稀疏边界单独重分发又引入额外数据传输。

Method: 为全注意力保留 SP16/32 父组，将其样本重打包为节点内稀疏子组 SP{1,2,4,8}，隐藏状态在全层间保持常驻；布局切换搭附于两处既有全层 Ulysses 通信点，单次变长 all-to-all 同时完成 head exchange 与 sample repacking，不新增重分发流程；辅以父组求解器，在子组形成前重新平衡父组成员，以提升子组可达的负载均衡度。

Result: 在 1F3S 混合注意力模型前 200 步中全部 22 个使用 SP16/32 父组的步上：仅重打包相比禁用基线，端到端平均时间降低 5.95%，MFU 提升 3.75 个百分点；加入改进求解器后，端到端时间降低 7.61%，MFU 提升 4.83 个百分点。

Conclusion: 按层差异化设置 SP 组规模并复用既有通信点完成样本重排，可在不增加通信量的前提下缓解变长训练中全/稀疏层的并行度冲突，显著改善训练效率与 MFU。

Abstract: Variable-length training creates conflicting parallelism requirements across the layers of a hybrid-attention model. The longest samples require wide sequence-parallel (SP) groups of 16 or 32 ranks for full attention, whose cost grows quadratically with sequence length. Sparse-attention layers, dominated by near-linear MoE work, can instead balance most evaluated workloads within node-local SP8 groups. Reusing the wide groups for these layers pushes their all-to-all communication across nodes, where the observed effective rate is 7.0x lower. Yet switching to smaller groups changes which ranks own each sample; a separate redistribution at every full-sparse boundary would introduce another data transfer.
  Layerwise Repacking keeps each SP16/32 parent for full attention and repacks its samples into node-local sparse children at SP{1, 2, 4, 8}, where hidden states persist between full layers. The layout change rides on the two existing full-layer Ulysses sites: at each, one variable-size all-to-all performs both the head exchange and the sample repacking, so no redistribution pass is added. Because the balance the children can reach depends on which samples share a parent, an auxiliary parent solver rebalances parent membership before the children are formed.
  On all 22 steps with SP16/32 parents among the first 200 training steps of a 1F3S hybrid-attention model (one full-attention layer per three sparse-attention layers), repacking alone reduces the mean end-to-end time by 5.95% and raises model FLOPs utilization (MFU) by 3.75 percentage points over the baseline with repacking disabled; with the improved solver, end-to-end time falls by 7.61% and MFU rises by 4.83 percentage points.

</details>


### [137] [DuplexTune: Coupled-State Replay for Memory-Efficient Mixture-of-Experts Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36009)
*Paul Raj,Simon Caldwell,Daniel Whitmore,Mutlu Mete*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DuplexTune 通过可训练宽度桥接将两条残差流耦合，重放隐藏状态以复用预训练注意力与专家模块，实现低显存、高吞吐的 MoE 语言模型适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏专家执行虽降低了计算量，但适配混合专家（MoE）语言模型时仍需存储大量隐状态，显存开销大。作者希望在不放弃预训练模块的前提下减少状态存储并提升吞吐。

Method: 提出 DuplexTune 框架：用可训练宽度桥接连接两条残差流与主干，将耦合隐藏状态通过预训练注意力与专家模块进行重放；先用桥接预热，再适配主干，路由权重保持冻结；并分析隐式状态恢复方程的收敛性与重放条件。

Result: 在 Qwen1.5-MoE-A2.7B 上，单张 H800 评测显示峰值显存 38.8 GB（带 checkpoint 的监督微调为 64.8 GB），吞吐 25.1 对 19.9 samples/s；MMLU 67.2%、GSM8K 75.6%、MT-Bench 7.69，多语言得分弱于 checkpoint 基线。

Conclusion: 结果表明状态存储、吞吐与适配质量之间存在依赖配置的权衡，DuplexTune 在显存与吞吐上具优势，但适配质量在部分维度（多语言）有所折损。

Abstract: Sparse expert execution does not remove the stored states needed to adapt a mixture-of-experts language model. We present DuplexTune, a framework for replaying coupled hidden states through pretrained attention and expert modules. Trainable width bridges connect two residual streams to the backbone. Bridge warm-up precedes backbone adaptation, with router weights kept frozen. We analyze the implicit state-recovery equation and identify the convergence and replay conditions needed for reconstruction. On Qwen1.5-MoE-A2.7B, the single-H800 evaluation reports 38.8 GB peak memory, versus 64.8 GB for supervised fine-tuning with checkpointing, and throughput of 25.1 versus 19.9 samples/s. DuplexTune achieves 67.2% MMLU, 75.6% GSM8K, and 7.69 MT-Bench, with a weaker multilingual score than the checkpointed baseline. The results characterize a configuration-dependent tradeoff among state storage, throughput, and adaptation quality.

</details>


### [138] [Exploratory creative pre-ranking: Hierarchical empirical-Bayes Thompson sampling for real-time creative optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36020)
*Jiale Han,Ajith Moparthi,Dan Xie,Xiaowu Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出层级化创意预排序器（creative pre-ranker），在广告排序前对每个 campaign 的创意池打分并做 shortlist；用层级收缩估计解决冷启动、用 Thompson 采样单次抽样平衡探索-利用、按受众段而非单用户预测以控制时延。Roblox 离线回放 PTR 提升 8.14%（随机 2.80%），转发创意数减少 40%+，服务耗时仅 +0.2%，A/B 通过后上线生产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态创意优化需在每次请求决定渲染哪个创意。既有工作把选择放在排序之前，但依赖确定性点预测、缺乏校准的不确定性；同时 AIGC 使创意近乎零成本而单创意反馈稀疏，叠加严格的时延预算，冷启动与探索-利用问题突出。

Method: 1) 架构：创意预排序器在每用户 ranker 之前运行，对全部召回创意打分并生成 per-campaign 候选池；2) 层级结构：受众段→广告物品→campaign→creative，冷启动创意回退到父层级的累积统计；3) 借用量由历史日志闭式求解，每层仅一个参数、无需调参；4) 预测粒度下沉到受众段，基于逐属性计数器，避免逐用户打分；5) 选择策略：对每个创意做单次 Thompson 抽样，兼顾探索与利用。

Result: Roblox 赞助广告流量回放（存在竞争创意的 campaign）：shortlist 使预测 play-through rate 提升 8.14%，同等规模随机 shortlist 为 2.80%；相比完整创意池转发创意数减少 40% 以上；服务时延仅增加 0.2%。线上 A/B 测试后已在 Roblox 生产环境部署。

Conclusion: 将创意筛选前置并采用层级收缩与 Thompson 抽样的轻量预排序，可在近零额外时延下显著提升创意效果指标、降低下游负载，并已通过线上验证实现生产化落地。

Abstract: Dynamic creative optimization decides which creative of an ad to render for each request. Recent work places that choice before ad ranking, but relies on deterministic point predictors with no calibrated uncertainty. AI-generated content has made creatives nearly free, while feedback per creative remains sparse and selection faces a tight latency budget. We introduce a creative pre-ranker that scores every recalled creative and shortlists each campaign's pool before the per-user ranker runs. Creatives sit in a hierarchy of audience segment, advertised item, campaign and creative, so a cold creative falls back on what its parent levels have earned, and how much it borrows from its parents is set in closed form from historical logs, with one parameter per level and nothing tuned. To stay within the latency budget, the pre-ranker predicts for an audience segment rather than an individual user, from per-attribute counters. It selects by a single Thompson draw per creative, balancing exploration and exploitation so that a new creative still has a chance to be learned. Replaying Roblox Sponsored-ads traffic over campaigns with competing creatives, the shortlist lifts predicted play-through rate by 8.14% against 2.80% for an equally sized random shortlist, forwards more than 40% fewer creatives than the full pool, and adds only 0.2% to serving time. After an A/B test on live traffic, the method now serves in production at Roblox.

</details>
