### [139] [Jaxnasium: Embracing PyTrees For Any-Agent, Cross-Suite Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36075)
*Koen Ponse,Aske Plaat,Thomas M. Moerland*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Jaxnasium 是一个基于 JAX 的强化学习库，以 PyTree 表示观测/动作空间，实现“单一算法文件”跨离散、连续、图像、复合及多智能体空间运行，并提供统一接口、11 个套件（300+ 环境）封装及多随机种子评估等工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JAX 加速的在线强化学习虽因硬件加速而快速普及，但生态碎片化严重：各环境套件自定义接口，算法实现通常只针对单一套件、单一空间类型，且局限于 JAX 原生或宿主端环境之一，导致复用与入门门槛高。

Method: 提出 Jaxnasium：将观测与动作空间表示为 PyTree，并据此构造网络与支持多智能体处理；算法实现接近单文件，无需修改即可适配多种空间；提供最小化的 Gymnasium 风格接口与 11 个套件封装（300+ 环境）；通过简单 wrapper 使 Gymnasium 与 EnvPool 环境共用同一算法与训练循环；并附带多随机种子评估、超参数扫描与项目脚手架工具。

Result: 默认超参下的小型 PPO 实现可在 300+ 环境上运行；同一算法与训练循环可驱动 Gymnasium 与 EnvPool 宿主端环境，且性能与专门为宿主端 JAX 训练设计的架构相比无损失；算法文件同时可作为可读的研究参考与可导入的工程组件。

Conclusion: Jaxnasium 通过 PyTree 抽象与统一接口缓解了 JAX 强化学习生态的碎片化，兼顾可读性与可复用性，降低领域使用门槛；项目已开源。

Abstract: Hardware-accelerated reinforcement learning in JAX has gained rapid popularity in recent years and has proven to be a successful method of scaling online reinforcement learning. The resulting ecosystem, however, is fragmented: environment suites define their own interfaces, and algorithm implementations are typically written for a single suite, a single type of space, and either JAX-native or host-based environments. Here we present Jaxnasium, a library for JAX-based reinforcement learning, aiming to make the field more accessible. Jaxnasium represents observation and action spaces as PyTrees, and uses this structure to build networks and support multi-agent processing. Through this, its near-single-file implementations run on discrete, continuous, image, and composite spaces, as well as in multi-agent environments, without any changes to the algorithm code. The same files serve as readable, research-friendly algorithms, as well as importable algorithms for more applied workloads. Through a minimal Gymnasium-style interface and wrappers for 11 suites, over 300 environments are available on which a small PPO implementation with default hyperparameters is able to run. Additionally, through a simple wrapper, we can run Gymnasium and EnvPool environments with the same algorithm and training loop, with no performance loss compared to architectures specifically built for training JAX algorithms on host-based environments. Jaxnasium further provides tools for multi-seed evaluation, hyperparameter sweeps, and project scaffolding. Jaxnasium is open-sourced at https://github.com/ponseko/jaxnasium

</details>


### [140] [How Much Does the Hint Matter? Scope Conditions for Hindsight Self-Distillation in Financial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36082)
*Johnny Yu,Cyril Gorlla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Fin-SD 是一种无需外部教师模型的本地化事后自蒸馏方法，通过定位首错步、截断至错误前的前缀并注入步骤提示来做条件蒸馏，在金融多步推理上取得增益，且提示内容与教师强度均非关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受数据驻留约束的金融机构只能本地部署小规模开放权重模型，其多步定量推理落后于前沿系统。传统 on-policy distillation（OPD）依赖强外部教师，on-policy self-distillation（OPSD）依赖完整参考解，作者追问：实际需要多少这类监督信号。

Method: 对学生失败的问题，由学生自身定位首个错误推理步并写出简短批评；在错误点前截断轨迹以构造正确前缀；将学生冻结副本以“相同前缀+提示”为条件作为教师，向学生做短 rollout 的反向 KL 蒸馏；无任何外部模型提供梯度。在 GPT-OSS-20B 上于自建多步金融推理基准（需依会计准则从真实场景推导公式输入）评测。

Result: (i) 用通用反馈替代批评，可复现 Fin-SD 在 FinanceReasoning Hard 子集上相对基线的全部 3.03 pp 提升，说明增益源于监督施加位置而非提示内容；(ii) 写提示的模型无需强于接收方，约 6 倍参数的 GPT-OSS-120B 同族提示未带来显著更大收益；(iii) 与基于完整参考解的 OPSD 精度相当，但生成 token 减少 9.5%；MMLU-Pro 通用推理与 FinTrust 十四项合规审计均无实质退化。

Conclusion: 在本地化约束下，事后定位错误位置并对正确前缀施加条件自蒸馏即可获得主要收益；监督的“位置”比“内容”和“教师强度”更重要，且能兼顾效率与合规性，是金融场景中轻量可行的自蒸馏方案。

Abstract: Financial institutions operating under data-residency constraints are restricted to open-weight models small enough to locally host, which trail frontier systems on multi-step quantitative reasoning. On-policy distillation narrows such gaps by supervising a student on its own rollouts, but traditional OPD requires a strong external teacher, and on-policy self-distillation (OPSD) conditions on a full reference solution. We ask how much of that signal is necessary. We present Fin-SD, a localized hindsight self-distillation recipe for multi-step reasoning. Given a problem the student fails, the student itself identifies the first incorrect reasoning step and writes a short critique of that step. We truncate the student's trajectory immediately before the error (aiming to obtain a correct prefix), condition a frozen copy of the student on the same prefix plus the hint, and distill that hint-conditioned teacher into the student over a short rollout under reverse KL. No external model contributes a gradient. We apply Fin-SD to GPT-OSS-20B on a custom multi-step financial reasoning benchmark that requires deriving formula inputs from realistic scenarios via accounting conventions. We find: (i) replacing the critique with a generic-feedback control matches Fin-SD's entire 3.03 pp accuracy gain over the base model on the FinanceReasoning Hard subset, suggesting the gain comes from where supervision is applied, not hint content; (ii) the model writing the hint does not need to be stronger than the model receiving it: hints from same-family GPT-OSS-120B, at roughly 6x the parameters, yield no materially greater benefits than self-written hints; and (iii) Fin-SD matches on-policy self-distillation from full reference solutions on accuracy while generating 9.5% fewer tokens. With Fin-SD, general reasoning on MMLU-Pro and a fourteen-metric compliance audit on FinTrust show no material regression.

</details>


### [141] [Instruction Capacity: Behavior and Degradation of Language Models under Controlled Load](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36095)
*Hennadii Kutomanov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种"指令容量"测量工具：以无意义文本中的单字词替换规则作为可量化的"负载"，每条规则对应可计算参考答案的伯努利试验，通过统计建模得到带不确定度的容量指标。GPT-4o mini实测：0.95正确率下容量约37–38条规则，全替换密度下约18–19条。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型遵循指令的质量随负载增加而下降，但现有研究缺乏一个以"负载单位"表达模型能力上限、并附带不确定度的量化指标，因此需要一个可校准、可复现的测量仪器。

Method: 构建规则替换式任务：规则原子化、彼此独立，结果由可计算参考答案判定；每次替换构成位置与规则编号已知的伯努利试验。统计模型先刻画正确替换概率对文本长度、词位置、替换密度、规则数量与规则编号的依赖形状，再用平滑律描述，并恢复与编码选择无关的参考水平，从而输出给定正确率下的指令容量及其不确定度。GPT-4o mini测量含2958次请求、642,430次试验。

Result: 各负载因子及答案崩溃均呈规律性定律；0.95正确替换率下指令容量约37–38条规则，全替换密度下约18–19条规则。代码、测量数据与"护照"公开，护照中每个数字均可由公开数据点复算。

Conclusion: 该仪器提供了带不确定度的指令容量度量，其模型护照可作为未来"提示指令负载计"的校准基准。

Abstract: The quality with which language models follow instructions falls as the load grows, yet there is still no quantity that expresses the limit of a model in units of load with an uncertainty. We propose an instrument in which the load consists of rules that replace single-token words in a text without meaning: such an instruction is atomic, the rules are independent by the construction of the task, and the outcome of every application of a rule is determined by a computable reference answer. Every occurrence of a word to be replaced becomes a Bernoulli trial with a known position in the answer and a known rule number. The statistical model first measures the shape of the dependence of the probability of a right replacement on the text length, the word position, the replacement density, the number of rules and the rule number, then describes it by smooth laws and restores a reference level that does not depend on the choice of the coding. The model yields the instruction capacity - the number of rules held at a given share of right replacements - with an uncertainty. A measurement of GPT-4o mini (2958 requests, 642,430 trials) gave regular laws for every load factor and for the collapse of the answer; the instruction capacity was about 37-38 rules at a share of right replacements of 0.95, and about 18-19 rules at the full replacement density. The code of the instrument, the measurement data and the passport are open, and every number of the passport is recomputed from the published points. The passport of a model serves as the calibration of a future meter of the instruction load of prompts.

</details>


### [142] [Minimax-Optimal Small-Loss Bounds for Online Linear Optimisation on $\ell_p$ Balls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36097)
*Jack J. Mayo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究单位 $\ell_p$ 球上全信息在线线性优化，提出带界径向正则的FTRL算法，无需知道 $T$ 或 $\Delta_T$ 即达到 $O_p(1+\min\{\Delta_T^{1-1/p}, d^{1/2-1/p}\sqrt{\Delta_T}\})$ 遗憾，并证明其极小极大最优性与常数的最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画单位 $\ell_p$ 球（$2\le p<\infty$）上在线线性优化关于"超出每轮最小损失的累积量"$\Delta_T$ 的精细遗憾界，厘清 $p$ 对速率与常数的影响，并区分自适应算法与FTL行为。

Method: 采用FTRL算法，配以有界径向正则项，至多一次切换到二次增广项；通过匹配的下界构造证明极小极大性；并对i.i.d.损失情形做期望遗憾的精确渐近分析。

Result: 对任意损失序列、无需已知 $T$ 与 $\Delta_T$，遗憾为 $O_p(1+\min\{\Delta_T^{1-1/p},\,d^{1/2-1/p}\sqrt{\Delta_T}\})$；匹配下界表明该速率对每个固定 $p$ 极小极大最优，且在 $d,T$ 一致保证下常数 $(2+o(1))p/\log p$ 于 $p\to\infty$ 最优；切换扩展保持尖锐常数并保证 $\mathrm{Reg}_T\le 2\,\mathrm{Reg}_T^{\mathrm{FTL}}$；存在FTL遗憾为1而本算法遗憾随 $T$ 增长的路径；i.i.d.均值无零坐标时给出期望遗憾精确 $\log T$ 系数。

Conclusion: 在 $\ell_p$ 球上以有界径向正则实现 $\Delta_T$ 自适应的最优FTRL遗憾率，常数在 $p\to\infty$ 下亦最优；单次切换既保持最优常数又获得相对FTL的倍增保证，并可通过精确常数分离FTL的方差项与正则偏差。

Abstract: We study full-information online linear optimisation on the unit $\ell_p$ ball in $\mathbb{R}^d$, for fixed $2\le p<\infty$ and losses of dual norm at most one. Let $Δ_T$ denote the cumulative loss of the best fixed action above each round's minimum. A follow-the-regularised-leader (FTRL) algorithm attains, on every loss sequence and without knowing $T$ or $Δ_T$, regret $O_p(1+\min\{Δ_T^{1-1/p},\, d^{1/2-1/p}\sqrt{Δ_T}\})$. Matching lower bounds show that this rate is minimax for each fixed $p$, and its constant $(2+o(1))p/\log p$ is optimal as $p\to\infty$ among guarantees uniform in $d$ and $T$. The algorithm uses a bounded radial regulariser and switches at most once to its quadratic augmentation. On some paths where follow-the-leader (FTL) has regret one, its regret grows with $T$. A switching extension keeps the sharp constant and also guarantees $\mathrm{Reg}_T\le 2\,\mathrm{Reg}_T^{\mathrm{FTL}}$. For i.i.d. losses whose mean has no zero coordinates, we compute the exact $\log T$ coefficient of the expected regret, which separates FTL's variance term from the bias of the regulariser.

</details>


### [143] [Beyond Update Counts: A Control Map for Evaluating Test-Time Model Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36109)
*Zehao Zhao,Shangjian Yin,Rui Liu,Chongyang Bai,Mark Ibrahim,Kavosh Asadi,Yuchen Lu,Hang Cui,Hamed Firooz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Control Map 评测框架，记录每次打分答案之前实际提交的更新序列，并分别评估行为变化、即时学习(acquisition)、后续回忆(retention)、无关行为保持(locality)及新设定下的表现，指出仅用更新次数/频率衡量测试时更新会掩盖"更新什么、在哪、何时、以何顺序"的重要差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时更新（权重、adapter、记忆模块等持久状态修改）的评测通常只用更新次数或频率加上更新后立即测得的性能来概括系统，这类总结忽略了更新的内容、位置、时机与顺序；同一总更新数可能掩盖关键差异——例如一个系统在一次更新后作答，另一个在两次更新后作答，从而无法反映真实能力。

Method: 提出 Control Map 评测框架：为每个被评分的答案记录其之前已提交的更新历史；将评估拆分为行为变化、即时学习(acquisition)、延迟回忆(retention)、无关行为保持(locality)与新设定性能五个维度；理论层面证明在何种条件下仅凭次数即可确定最终状态，并在给定假设下给出"答案前历史差异"对平均分数差异的上界关系；实验上在 760M 参数检查点上比较 24 种更新调度，并考察 CounterFact 上的 LoRA 与 ROME 等编辑器。

Result: 三点发现：(1) 更新调度影响显著——760M 检查点上，相同更新次数的 24 种调度准确率在 19.7%–34.2% 之间波动；(2) 学会编辑不等于持久且局部——六个 CounterFact LoRA 设置均成功学会新信息，ROME 在全部 13 个操作点上都保留住编辑但每个点都违反 locality；(3) 没有编辑器在跨模型、跨基准、跨测试中始终最优。

Conclusion: 仅凭更新次数或频率的评测不足以保证可比性；应报告完整的更新历史，并将 acquisition、retention、locality 分开评估，同时承认不存在普遍最优的编辑器，需按场景选择。

Abstract: Test-time updating lets a language model change persistent state after training---such as model weights, adapter parameters, or a memory module---before or while answering queries. Existing evaluations often compare systems using update count or frequency and performance measured immediately afterward. These summaries omit what is updated, where, when, and in what order. The same total can therefore hide an important difference: one system may answer after one update while another answers after two. We introduce the Control Map, an evaluation framework that records the committed updates preceding each scored answer and separately evaluates behavioral changes, immediate learning (acquisition), later recall (retention), preservation of unrelated behavior (locality), and performance in new settings. We prove when counts alone determine final state and show, under stated assumptions, how differences between pre-answer histories limit differences in average scores. Using ControlMap, our experiments reveal three insights. First, update scheduling matters: at a 760M-parameter checkpoint, 24 schedules with the same update count yield 19.7%--34.2% accuracy. Second, learning an edit does not guarantee that it lasts or remains contained: all six selected CounterFact LoRA settings learn the new information, while ROME retains edits at all 13 operating points but fails locality at each. Third, no editor performs best consistently across models, benchmarks, and tests. Evaluations should therefore report update histories and assess acquisition, retention, and locality separately.

</details>


### [144] [MM-Net: A Multimodal Multi-Task Network for Sleep Staging and Sleep Apnea Assessment After Ischemic Stroke](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36583)
*Md Wahiduzzaman Suva,Esm E Moula Chowdhury Abha,Md Imtiaj Alam Sajin,Md Iftekharul Mobin,Jia Uddin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MM-Net多模态多任务框架，通过交叉拟合与岭回归嵌入对齐解决预训练EEG编码器的表征不一致问题，实现缺血性卒中患者睡眠分期与呼吸事件联合检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺血性卒中后的自动多导睡眠图分析困难：睡眠相关神经活动改变、并发呼吸紊乱；现有卒中专用方法仅聚焦睡眠分期分类，缺乏睡眠结构与呼吸事件的联合分析；此外，适配预训练EEG编码器时，在下游训练集（已见患者）嵌入与测试集（未见个体）间存在表征不一致。

Method: MM-Net为多模态多任务框架：采用交叉拟合（cross-fitting）与基于岭回归的嵌入对齐消除表征不一致；将睡眠适配的EEG-EOG表征与工程化神经描述子，同学习得到的呼吸/心脏特征经交叉注意力与共享时序建模融合，同步完成睡眠分期与呼吸事件检测；评估采用患者独立的十折协议。

Result: iSLEEPS（99例亚急性缺血性卒中）上，分期准确率76.3%（Cohen's κ=0.656），呼吸事件AUC 0.825，优于同协议基线；嵌入对齐使睡眠适配编码器较冻结编码器提升1.4个百分点（p=0.020），未对齐仅0.2个百分点。Sleep-EDF上分期准确率86.4%，超过所有同录音重实现的单EEG架构；ISRUC-I上80.3%分期准确率、呼吸AUC 0.889，优于重训的分期基线；未经重训，iSLEEPS训练模型在ISRUC-I与UCD上呼吸AUC分别为0.833与0.841。

Conclusion: 结果支持表征一致的神经适配与联合生理建模在卒中、健康睡眠及睡眠障碍队列中的有效性，验证了跨队列泛化能力。

Abstract: Automated polysomnography after ischemic stroke is challenging because of altered sleep-related neural activity and concurrent respiratory disturbances. Existing stroke-specific approaches mainly address sleep-stage classification, leaving joint analysis of sleep architecture and respiratory events underexplored. Moreover, adapting pretrained EEG encoders introduces a representation inconsistency when downstream models are trained on embeddings from previously seen patients but evaluated on unseen individuals. We propose MM-Net, a multimodal multi-task framework that resolves this inconsistency through cross-fitting and ridge-based embedding alignment. Sleep-adapted EEG-EOG representations and engineered neural descriptors are fused with learned cardiorespiratory features through cross-attention and shared temporal modeling for simultaneous sleep staging and respiratory-event detection. On iSLEEPS (99 patients with subacute ischemic stroke), ten-fold patient-independent evaluation achieved 76.3% staging accuracy (Cohen's $κ$ = 0.656) and 0.825 respiratory-event AUC, outperforming the evaluated baselines under matched protocols. The sleep-adapted encoder improved the downstream model when its embeddings were aligned, raising staging accuracy by 1.4 percentage points over the frozen encoder ($p$ = 0.020) versus 0.2 points without alignment. MM-Net also achieved 86.4% staging accuracy on Sleep-EDF, above every single-EEG architecture reimplemented on the same recordings, and 80.3% staging accuracy with 0.889 respiratory AUC on ISRUC-I, exceeding its retrained staging baselines. Without retraining, the iSLEEPS-trained model retained respiratory AUCs of 0.833 on ISRUC-I and 0.841 on UCD. These findings support representation-consistent neural adaptation and joint physiological modeling across stroke, healthy-sleep, and sleep-disorder cohorts.

</details>


### [145] [Dirichlet Process Mixtures within Semiparametric Bayesian Networks: A Novel Conditional Density Estimation Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36110)
*Victor Alejandre,Pedro Larrañaga,Concha Bielza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出用狄利克雷过程混合（DPM）替代核密度估计（KDE）来建模半参数贝叶斯网络（SPBN）中的非参数条件概率分布（CPD），以获得更紧凑的表示、更低的推理成本与更优的密度估计性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 SPBN 使用 KDE 建模非参数 CPD，其"每个观测一个分量"的混合表示导致推理成本高、密度估计误差大，限制了模型实用性。

Method: 基于贝叶斯非参数狄利克雷过程混合（DPM）定义新的非参数 CPD 建模方式；DPM 对观测进行数据自适应聚类，形成更紧凑的混合表示。通过扩展开源 PyBNesian 包实现 DPM-SPBN，并在合成数据与真实数据上，从固定结构下的密度估计和基于 CPD 评分的学习两个角度进行评估。

Result: 在给定网络结构下，DPM 型 CPD 的密度估计显著优于面向连续变量的先进贝叶斯网络模型，在合成数据上取得更低的绝对对数似然误差；在依赖 CPD 评分的结构学习中，DPM-SPBN 的结构学习误差与密度估计误差均低于 KDE-SPBN；真实数据实验一致支持上述结论。

Conclusion: DPM 型 CPD 建模是所评估的非参数方法中面向连续变量最有效的替代方案，可在保持非参数灵活性的同时降低推理成本并提升估计精度。

Abstract: Semiparametric Bayesian networks (SPBNs) are flexible probabilistic graphical models for representing joint distributions of continuous variables through lower-dimensional conditional probability distributions (CPDs). Existing SPBN models use kernel density estimators (KDEs) for non-parametric CPD modeling, but their one-component-per-observation mixture representation can lead to increased inference costs and density estimation errors, which can be limiting. In this work, we define a new CPD modeling approach for non-parametric components based on Bayesian non-parametric Dirichlet Process Mixtures (DPMs) within SPBNs. DPMs provide a data-adaptive clustering of observations, yielding a more compact representation while retaining the flexibility of non-parametric density estimation, unlike KDE. This results in lower inference costs while improving density estimation performance. We implement the proposed DPM-based SPBN (DPM-SPBN) by extending the open-source PyBNesian package and evaluate it on synthetic and real-world data. For a given network structure, DPM-based CPDs significantly outperform state-of-the-art Bayesian network models for continuous variables in density estimation, achieving lower absolute log-likelihood errors on synthetic data. We further investigate the impact of the proposed CPD modeling on structure learning methods relying on CPD-based scores. DPM-based SPBNs achieve lower structure learning and density estimation errors than KDE-based SPBNs. Experiments on real-world data corroborate these findings, identifying DPM-based CPD modeling as the most effective alternative among the evaluated non-parametric approaches for continuous variables.

</details>


### [146] [Normalized Cuts and Data Clustering via Matrix Fractional Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36616)
*Yannan Chen,Beichen Huang,Licheng Zhao,Chris Ding,Kaiming Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文从分数规划(FP)视角重新审视归一化割(NCut)问题，提出多维二次变换以处理多比值项，将多类NCut化为二分匹配问题并用线性搜索求解，同时用MM理论刻画收敛性，并在聚类与图像分割任务上验证优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NCut虽具分式结构，但现有文献很少从FP角度处理：经典Dinkelbach变换只能处理单一比值，仅适用于二类聚类，而多类聚类涉及多个比值项，导致传统FP方法不适用。

Method: 提出多维二次变换(multidimensional quadratic transform)，直接处理多比值情形，将NCut问题重构为二分匹配(bipartite matching)问题，通过线性搜索即可求解；并建立该FP方法与minorization-maximization(MM)理论的联系，据此分析算法收敛性。

Result: 实验在多种数据聚类与图像分割任务上进行了数值验证，展示了所提基于FP的算法相较对比方法的优势。

Conclusion: 多维二次变换为多类NCut提供了统一的FP求解框架，兼具可高效求解(二分匹配+线性搜索)与可理论刻画收敛性(与MM等价)的特点，是经典Dinkelbach变换向多比值情形的一种自然推广。

Abstract: The normalized cut (NCut) problem is a fundamental one in the area of data clustering. Despite its fractional structure, the NCut problem is seldom considered from a fractional programming (FP) perspective in the existing literature. The main reason is that the conventional FP method is inadequate for this problem case: the classical Dinkelbach's transform can only handle a single ratio and hence is limited to the two-class clustering, whereas the multi-class clustering involves multiple ratio terms. This work proposes a novel so-called multidimensional quadratic transform that is capable of dealing with the multi-ratio case; it recasts the NCut problem into a bipartite matching problem---which can be immediately solved by linear search. Furthermore, we explore the connection between the proposed FP method and the minorization-maximization (MM) theory, and in turn characterize the convergence behavior of our algorithm. Finally, we conduct experiments to numerically demonstrate the advantages of the proposed FP-based algorithm in a variety of data clustering and image segmentation tasks.

</details>


### [147] [Scaling and Renormalization in Neural Networks: An Effective Renormalization-Group Description of Grokking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36113)
*Min Chen,Junqi Wang,Zeguan Wu,Wenhao Huang,Junyu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将深度学习中的"grokking"（从记忆到泛化的延迟转变）用标度与重整化群理论量化描述，在两层网络模加任务上构造序参量、耦合矩阵与实空间RG流，得到的临界指数与经验估计吻合在1–10%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grokking表现为深度网络从记忆到泛化的延迟动力学转变，但缺乏定量的物理描述框架。作者希望检验标度理论与重整化群（RG）能否刻画这一转变，从而为神经泛化相变建立可量化的物理语言。

Method: 以两层网络模加任务为对象，训练模型集合定义序参量（权重范数平方）及热容、磁化率的涨落代理量，以训练epoch和权重衰减作为温度场与外场控制坐标；通过对损失关于逐神经元范数的二阶展开构造精确耦合矩阵，并用其Fiedler向量定义有效几何，使耦合强度呈单调空间衰减（随机置换下不出现）；随后以块自旋聚合相邻神经元、将耦合矩阵参数化为非对角带强度，执行实空间RG并通过线性化RG映射提取相关标度指数及五个导出临界指数。

Result: Fiedler向量排序给出的有效几何产生单调空间耦合衰减，随机置换下无此结构；线性化RG映射所得标度指数与五个导出临界指数的直接经验估计相匹配，偏差在1–10%之间。

Conclusion: 该框架为grokking提供了一致的有效描述，表明标度与重整化群形式体系可作为研究神经网络动力学的工具。

Abstract: Grokking is a delayed transition from memorization to generalization in deep neural networks (NNs). We examine whether scaling and renormalization group (RG) theories can describe this dynamics, establishing a quantitative physical framework for neural generalization transitions. For modular addition on a two-layer NN, an ensemble of trained models defines an order parameter (the squared weight norm) and fluctuation proxies for the heat capacity and the susceptibility, with training epoch and weight decay serving as thermal and field control coordinates. To bridge neural architectures to real-space RG, we construct an exact coupling matrix via a second-order expansion of the loss with respect to per-neuron norms and use its Fiedler vector to define an effective geometry. This spectral ordering yields a monotonic spatial decay of coupling strength that is absent under random permutations. We then execute real-space RG by block-spin aggregating adjacent neurons and parameterizing the coupling matrix into off-diagonal band strengths. Linearizing this local RG map yields relevant scaling exponents and five derived critical exponents, with direct empirical estimates matching RG predictions within 1-10%. Overall, our framework provides a consistent effective description of grokking, suggesting the scaling and renormalization group formalism as a tool to investigate NN dynamics.

</details>


### [148] [SpatialPC: Deep Computation with Sublinear Local Credit Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36139)
*Benjamin Prada,Shion Matsumoto,Ankur Arjun Mali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出，局部学习规则的 Θ(N) 延迟源于通信几何而非深度；在 d 维布局下证明了 Ω(N^{1/d}) 的信用延迟下界，并构造达到该下界的有向无环图，从而在可实现电路模型中实现深度与信用深度的分离；并用 SpatialPC（基于伴随预测动力学 APD）在预测编码框架内验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在含 N 个状态的链上，任何有界通信范围的局部学习规则都需要 Θ(N) 轮才能使损失影响最早参数，以往将这一延迟归因于深度。作者希望澄清该延迟本质上是通信几何的性质，并探讨深度与信用传播延迟是否可解耦。

Method: (1) 用光锥论证给出下界：对以常数密度置于 d 维的 N=m^d 个状态，任何局部算法在有界范围轮数下梯度估计需 Ω(N^{1/d}) 轮才能在所有参数上符号正确。(2) 构造单位范围、度数为 2d 的有向无环图，含长度 N−1 的哈密顿计算，且每个状态到输出的有向距离为 d(m−1)=Θ(N^{1/d})。(3) 在可实现电路模型中对比表示深度 Θ(N) 与因果信用延迟 Θ(N^{1/d})。(4) 提出 SpatialPC，用伴随预测动力学（APD）传播信用，K 轮状态为截断反路径和 a^K=Σ_{k<K}(J^⊤)^k b，误差恰为 (J^⊤)^K a*；并设计稀疏二维 Moore 编译器（N−1 骨架、3/2N−2 单位范围边）。(5) 在 MNIST/Fashion-MNIST 上以 T=2√N 轮验证。

Result: 理论上得到 Ω(N^{1/d}) 下界与匹配构造，深度与信用深度分离量级约 N^{1−1/d}。编译器最坏节点可达性实测为 √N+2（至 N=65,536，65,535 边骨架经 258 跳达到）。实验中 T=2√N 轮时有限轮梯度与收敛梯度对齐（N=256 时 MNIST 余弦 0.93、Fashion-MNIST 0.97），20 个 epoch 后测试准确率 97.6% 和 88.1%。

Conclusion: 有界范围局部学习的延迟由通信几何（维度/图结构）而非深度决定；深度与信用延迟是可独立参数，可在预测编码中通过 SpatialPC/APD 实现显著分离，且稀疏二维编译器与实际任务梯度质量支持该结论。

Abstract: On a chain of $N$ states, any local learning rule with bounded communication range needs $Θ(N)$ rounds before the loss can influence the earliest parameters, and this latency is usually attributed to depth. We show that it is a property of the communication geometry rather than of depth. For $N=m^d$ states placed at constant density in $d$ dimensions, a light-cone argument gives an $Ω(N^{1/d})$ lower bound on the number of bounded-range rounds that any local algorithm needs before its gradient estimates can be sign-correct at every parameter. We construct a unit-range, degree-$2d$ directed acyclic graph that contains a Hamiltonian computation of length $N-1$ and in which every state lies within directed distance $d(m-1)=Θ(N^{1/d})$ of the output, matching the lower bound. In the realizable-circuit model, depth and credit depth are therefore independent parameters: represented depth $Θ(N)$ and causal credit latency $Θ(N^{1/d})$ coexist, with a separation of order $N^{1-1/d}$. SpatialPC realizes this separation within predictive coding. Credit is propagated by adjoint prediction dynamics (APD), a local update whose $K$-round state on a settled acyclic graph is the truncated reverse-path sum $a^K=\sum_{k<K}(J^\top)^k b$, with error exactly $(J^\top)^K a^\star$. Graph geometry fixes when credit can first arrive at a state; the Jacobian-weighted path tail fixes how many further rounds accurate credit requires. A sparse two-dimensional Moore compiler retains the $N-1$ backbone with $\tfrac32N-2$ unit-range edges and has measured worst-node reachability $\sqrt N+2$ up to $N=65{,}536$, where a 65,535-edge backbone is reached in 258 local hops. With $T=2\sqrt N$ rounds, SpatialPC keeps finite-round task gradients aligned with settled gradients (cosine $0.93$ on MNIST and $0.97$ on Fashion-MNIST at $N=256$) and reaches $97.6\%$ and $88.1\%$ test accuracy after 20 epochs.

</details>


### [149] [Base Models Can Detect Hidden Intentions that Prompted Classifiers Miss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36202)
*Oz Amram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 persona-header likelihood evaluation（PHLE），用基座预训练模型在合适 header 下评估输出序列的似然，从而解码并分类后训练模型在语义无关输出中隐藏的 subliminal 信号，支持 Persona Selection Model 的非平凡预测，并可用于模型隐藏目标审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PSM 认为后训练是在预训练已学到的 persona 中做选择；subliminal learning 表明模型可把“爱猫头鹰”等隐秘倾向渗入数字串等语义无关输出。现有判定依赖微调学生，而生成模型自身作为分类器近乎随机，需要一种直接检测手段来检验 PSM 的预测并支持隐藏目标审计。

Method: PHLE：对后训练模型生成的输出（如数字序列）用基座预训练模型加适当 header 计算其似然，据此区分不同隐秘信号。实验中区分“You love owls”与“You love eagles”提示所生成的数字序列；并区分带有隐秘害人意图（已过滤掉明显失配内容）的模型输出与无系统提示模型输出。

Result: PHLE 能有效区分上述两组信号，而包括生成模型自身在内的提示式分类器准确率接近随机。信号可跨模型族部分迁移：Qwen 基座模型能读出 Olmo 生成的信号，反之亦然。

Conclusion: 结果为 PSM 的非平凡预测提供证据，表明基座模型以 header 条件似然形式保留了后训练 persona 的痕迹；该方法有望用于审计模型隐藏目标。

Abstract: The Persona Selection Model (PSM) posits that post-training selects a persona the model learned during pre-training. Work on subliminal learning has shown that models can impart covert signals (like "love of owls") into semantically unrelated outputs like strings of numbers, and that students trained on these outputs inherit those traits. In this work I show that by evaluating the likelihood of such outputs under the base pre-trained model with an appropriate header, a method I call persona-header likelihood evaluation (PHLE), one can decode these subliminal signals and classify between them. For example, PHLE distinguishes number sequences generated by a post-trained model prompted with "You love owls" from those prompted with "You love eagles", and distinguishes answers from a model with a secret desire to harm the user, filtered to remove any noticeable misalignment, from those of a model with no system prompt. In both cases prompted classifiers, including the generating model itself, remain near chance. The signal partially persists across model families, with the Qwen base model reading signals generated by Olmo and vice versa. This is evidence for a non-trivial prediction of the PSM, and the method may be practically useful for auditing the hidden objectives of models.

</details>


### [150] [Freeze, Don't Clone: Fine-Tuning with Expressive Policies Without Retaining Offline Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36247)
*Sami Nur Islam,Johan Obando-Ceron,Doina Precup,Aaron Courville,Pablo Samuel Castro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究"离线数据不再可用"场景下的离线到在线强化学习，指出除评论家侧的重校准失败外，还存在行为克隆侧（actor）的失败：在线继续行为拟合会使行为模型重新拟合到有限且非平稳的在线经验上。作者发现冻结预训练的行为先验可打破"行为反馈回路"，在IFQL、FQL、QC三种方法、七个OGBench域上显著提升无保留微调性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数离线到在线RL方法假设离线数据集在微调全程可用，但许多实际场景并不满足该假设。此前工作将无保留设置的失败主要归因于分布偏移下的评论家重校准，作者希望补充识别被忽视的actor侧失败机制。

Method: 作者识别出actor侧失败：离线数据消失后，持续的行为克隆不再保持离线行为分布，而是把行为模型重新拟合到智能体有限且非平稳的在线经验。据此提出在在线阶段停止行为拟合、冻结预训练的表达性先验，同时让价值学习继续驱动改进，并在IFQL、FQL、QC上验证。

Result: 在七个OGBench域上，冻结预训练先验、停止在线行为拟合在IFQL、FQL、QC三种方法上均带来显著提升。分析表明持续在线BC会迅速抹除预训练先验，引发表征坍缩与动作多样性丧失，从而使评论家缺乏有效探索信号。

Conclusion: 无保留离线到在线微调的失败可归结为"行为反馈回路"：在线BC抹除先验→表征坍缩与多样性丧失→评论家探索信号不足。冻结先验可打破该循环，既保留离线覆盖度又允许价值学习持续改进，是一种简洁有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Offline-to-online reinforcement learning uses offline pretraining to accelerate subsequent online learning. While many methods assume the offline dataset remains available throughout fine-tuning, there are many settings where this is not the case. Prior work attributes failures in this setting primarily to critic recalibration under distribution shift. We identify a complementary actor-side failure: once the offline data disappears, continued behavioral cloning (BC) no longer preserves the offline behavior distribution and instead refits the behavioral model to the agent's limited, and nonstationary, online experience. We demonstrate that stopping online behavioral fitting while preserving the pretrained expressive prior substantially improves no-retention fine-tuning across implicit flow Q-learning (IFQL), flow Q-learning (FQL), and action-chunked RL (QC) on seven OGBench domains. We trace this failure to a behavioral feedback loop: continuing online behavioral cloning rapidly erases the pretrained prior, inducing representational collapse and action diversity loss that starves the critic of useful exploration. Freezing the prior breaks this cycle, preserving offline coverage while allowing value learning to drive continuous improvement.

</details>


### [151] [Confidence Is Not a Promise: A Reference Audit of Typed Decision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36311)
*Pedro Carvalho Brom*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文检验“类型化决策模型”给出的概率是否真正兑现其精度承诺，比较开权重模型 Laya（五个检查点）与仅API可得的 Jev 1.13，评估阈值选择、校准跨语言稳健性、噪声与漂移下的表现、标签需求以及置信度与解释的可信度。核心结论是：点估计选阈值普遍失约，Learn then Test 全部守住承诺，Beta 下界规则决策更多但无保证；校准不跨语言迁移，噪声破坏干净文本上的承诺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件常把模型输出的概率当作保证来行动，但单次决策的概率只对决策群体的统计陈述有效。作者希望量化“一个概率究竟许可了什么”以及各类保证流程（点估计阈值、Learn then Test、Beta 下界）在真实条件下是否能兑现承诺。

Method: 在190次可交换评估上比较三种阈值/门控策略；跨51个语种检验校准迁移；施加常见输入噪声并试探性加入噪声训练；在四个额外领域（含 LIAR2）以微调 Laya 测试达到非劣于 Jev 所需标签量（250–8000）；在漂移下对预注册的贝叶斯重训练回路做验证；在 M3 月度序列上评估方向预测；用 AUROC 衡量置信度区分对错的能力，并考察其是否可选忠实解释，及 API 两位小数取整下单决策精确联盟博弈的可解释空间。

Result: 点估计阈值在190次评估中147次失约；Learn then Test 全部守约；Beta 下界规则平均决策更多（128次优于、0次少于 Learn then Test），但缺乏保证。英文校准的95%承诺仅在8/51语种成立。噪声破坏干净文本上的承诺，噪声训练可提升噪声精度且洁净精度无损。微调 Laya 需250–8000标签才可能不劣于 Jev，LIAR2 上未达到。漂移下贝叶斯回路未获支持；逐窗口重资格化阈值更能守约。M3 上月度方向预测不优于常数。置信度 AUROC 0.80–0.96，但不能挑选忠实解释。

Conclusion: 概率分布只是群体层面的统计陈述，不能作为单次决策的保证；只有带分布无关下界的流程（如 Learn then Test）在全部评估中守约，效率更高的 Beta 规则以放弃保证为代价。校准、噪声、漂移和语言迁移都会使承诺失效，需按流量数据验证、逐窗口重资格化，并对置信度与解释的用途保持审慎。作者公开冻结答案、数据表、承诺检查与代码以支持复现。

Abstract: Typed decision models return a probability distribution over options supplied by the caller, and software tends to act on that number as if it were a guarantee. We measure what it licenses, comparing the open-weight Laya, in five checkpoints, with Jev 1.13, available only through an API. A probability licenses a statement about groups of decisions, not about one decision, and a gate that promises an accuracy among the cases it decides alone keeps that promise only with a lower bound checked on data like its traffic. Across 190 exchangeable evaluations, a threshold chosen by point estimate breaks the promise in 147, Learn then Test keeps it in all of them, and a Beta lower-bound rule decides more items on average than Learn then Test in 128 and fewer in none, without its guarantee. Calibrated in English, Jev keeps a promise of 95% in 8 of 51 locales. Ordinary typing noise breaks a promise calibrated on clean text, and, in exploratory runs, training with noise raises the noisy accuracy with no loss on clean text. In four further domains, a fine-tuned Laya needs from 250 to 8,000 labels to be non-inferior to Jev, and does not get there on LIAR2. Under drift, the preregistered claim for a Bayesian retraining loop is not supported: the loop avoids needless retraining where the change does not degrade the model, at a cost equivalent to recalibration alone, and re-qualifying the threshold in every window keeps the promise more often where it does. On the monthly series of M3, neither decider predicts the direction of the next value better than a constant answer. Confidence separates right from wrong answers with an AUROC from 0.80 to 0.96, but it does not select faithful explanations, and exact coalition games of single decisions show what an explanation can still say through an API that rounds to two decimals. We release the frozen answers with a datasheet, a promise check and the code.

</details>


### [152] [AHEAD: Adaptive Neural Acceleration for Large-Scale Linear Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36321)
*Sihao Liu,Zhichao Hou,Huayue Gu,Raiyan Rahman,Liang Wu,Ruozhou Yu,Xiaorui Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AHEAD：一种仅预测步长的学习型加速器，配合学习式"交接策略"决定热启动迭代数，在大规模线性规划上实现端到端（而非仅迭代数）加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模LP（百万级变量与约束）使时间与内存吃紧；一阶方法（如PDHG）无需矩阵分解、可扩展，但迭代次数多。已有展开式（unrolled）神经加速器存在三个问题：每层处理全部变量与约束（单次推理昂贵）、预测迭代点仍需求解器精修、所有实例跑相同层数。因此"减少迭代"不等于"缩短求解时间"，需要重新设计以取得端到端加速。

Method: AHEAD保留一阶求解器的更新规则，仅用一个权重共享的预测器读取紧凑的求解器统计量，对原始与对偶步长进行缩放，使每次热启动迭代开销很低；仅运行短暂的热启动阶段后交给求解器。另设学习式交接策略（handoff policy），按实例与目标精度自适应设定热启动迭代次数，之后由求解器按自身终止准则完成求解。

Result: 在变量数达10^7的packing LP上，冷启动相对Gurobi一阶求解器取得6.5–11.6×端到端加速，且在未见过的规模与分布上仍有效；收益可推广到PageRank LP，并在不同难度与目标精度下保持。

Conclusion: 面向大规模LP的神经加速应以端到端求解时间为目标：轻量、权重共享的步长预测加自适应的短热启动交接，比逐层处理全部变量/约束的展开式加速器更实用，并具备跨规模与分布的泛化性。

Abstract: Large-scale linear programs with millions of variables and constraints strain time and memory budgets. First-order methods such as the primal-dual hybrid gradient scale to this regime because they avoid matrix factorizations, but they require many iterations. Learned accelerators can reduce these iterations, yet fewer iterations do not guarantee a shorter solve. Learning inference also takes time, and running the accelerator longer trades solver time for inference time. Existing unrolled accelerators handle this trade-off poorly: each layer processes every variable and constraint, the predicted iterates still need solver refinement, and every instance runs the same layers. We therefore ask: What neural accelerator design achieves end-to-end speedups on large-scale linear programs across sizes and target accuracies? We introduce AHEAD, an accelerator that predicts step sizes for first-order linear programming solvers while keeping their update rules. Its weight-shared predictor reads compact solver statistics and rescales the primal and dual step sizes, so each warm-start iteration stays inexpensive and a short warm-start phase hands the solver a warm start. A learned handoff policy sets the number of warm-start iterations for each instance and target accuracy, after which the solver finishes under its own termination criterion. On packing linear programs with up to $10^7$ variables, AHEAD achieves $6.5$--$11.6\times$ end-to-end speedups over Gurobi's first-order solver from a cold start, including on instances of unseen sizes and distributions. The gains extend to PageRank linear programs and hold across difficulty levels and target accuracies.

</details>


### [153] [From Generation to Scoring: Scaling LLM-Based Semantic Labeling at LinkedIn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36383)
*Jian Sheng,Sriram Vasudevan,Sundara Raman Ramachandran,Yang Hu,Junyu Zhou,Xiaojing Ma,Haolan Qin,Tiffany Zhou,Vedant Vipul Jhaveri,Ashwini Bhingare,Chinmay Kini,Qing Lan,Xiaoqing Wang,Suleyman Cetintas,Fedor Borisyuk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将工业LLM标注器表述为语义打分：单候选提示下用prefill-only的答案token得分给出判断，并适配MIS在一次序列中掩码注意力打分数百候选；在LinkedIn部署中决策稳定、样本效率约4.7倍、吞吐提升1.24倍（离线）与1.9倍（在线）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业大模型标注器为提效把大量候选标签打包进一个提示并逐个生成YES/NO，但每个判断仅依赖实体与单个候选，打包生成会耦合独立决策、把解码预算浪费在列表上，并依赖列表格式正确。

Method: 端到端改造：(1) 将标注器重构为语义打分，在单候选提示上微调，使其在一次prefill-only前向中把每个决策表示为某个答案token的分数；(2) 适配Multiple-Item Scoring (MIS)，通过候选间注意力掩码在单条序列中为数百个候选打分；(3) 离线批量推理封装为模型无关的Ray Data API，在线服务基于对SGLang的MIS支持实现。

Result: 候选重排序时最多仅0.01%决策改变；达到与参考标签相同的标注一致性所需训练对约为打包生成方法的1/4.7；基于MIS的技术栈离线吞吐较已部署栈提升1.24倍，在线模型服务在1秒p99延迟目标下提升1.9倍。

Conclusion: 预填充打分+注意力掩码的多项打分可在不牺牲标注一致性的前提下显著提升标注效率与稳定性，并具备工程可复用性（Ray Data API与SGLang MIS支持）。

Abstract: For efficiency, industrial large language model (LLM) labelers often pack many candidate labels into one prompt and generate a YES/NO answer for each, even when each decision depends only on the entity and one candidate. Generating these independent decisions as one answer list couples them, spends decoding on the list, and relies on the list being well-formed. In this paper, we present an end-to-end recipe that reformulates the labeler as semantic scoring, fine-tuning it on single-candidate prompts to return each decision as one answer-token score from a prefill-only pass. We also adapt Multiple-Item Scoring (MIS) to score hundreds of candidates in one sequence with attention masked between them. Deployed for profile qualification labeling at LinkedIn, semantic scoring changes at most 0.01% of decisions when candidates are reordered and matches the agreement with reference labels that packed generation reaches only with about 4.7x as many training pairs. The MIS-based stack raises throughput over the deployed stack by 1.24x offline and 1.9x in model serving under a one-second p99 latency target. For offline batch inference, we implement MIS-based scoring as a reusable, model-agnostic Ray Data API proposed for upstream adoption. Model serving uses MIS support contributed to SGLang.

</details>


### [154] [Simulated petrological labels enable probabilistic mineralogy prediction from whole-rock geochemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36429)
*Matthew J. Liu,Aaron D. Kaplan,Michael L. Whittaker,Kristin A. Persson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出三段式模型，用约百万条模拟岩石学标签作中段监督，结合自监督全岩成分编码器与实测矿物学校准层，从化学成分预测模态矿物学，零样本可判别矿物有无及相对丰度，校准后逼近全量拟合效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模态矿物学（矿物种类与丰度）对地质研究与资源开采重要，但测量耗时；全岩化学数据通量更高，然而由元素预测矿物学是一对多反问题，且带标注的矿物学数据稀少，难以规模化训练。

Method: 构建三部分模型：1）在约78.8万条全岩成分上自监督训练的Transformer编码器；2）以近100万条模拟标签（每条成分可表达多种形成条件）训练的预测头，输出矿物组合分布；3）将预测与实测矿物学对齐的重校准层。

Result: 在640块火山岩上零样本评估：模型能判别多数矿物类别的有无，并预测哪些岩石在各类矿物上更富集；但预测丰度系统性偏离实测值。仅用25块标注岩石拟合重校准层即可获得全量368块拟合集似然改进的76%；丰度误差零样本为11.0%，全量拟合集为9.5%。

Conclusion: 机器学习模型可同时利用不完美的模拟器并缓解其系统偏差，为地球化学中数据稀缺的反问题提供可行范式。

Abstract: Modal mineralogy, the mineral identities and abundances in rocks, guides geological studies and industrial resource extraction. While measuring modal mineralogy is time-intensive, whole-rock chemical measurements furnish elemental concentrations at higher throughput. Predicting mineralogy from elemental data, however, poses a one-to-many inverse problem, and labeled mineralogical datasets are too sparse to train on at scale. We address both challenges by simulating petrological labels that express multiple formation conditions per composition and at a scale unattainable by measurements. Nearly one million simulated labels supervise the middle component of a three-part model: a transformer encoder self-supervised on roughly 788,000 whole-rock compositions, the simulation-trained heads that predict distributions over mineral assemblages, and a recalibration layer that aligns the predictions with measured mineralogy. Zero-shot on 640 volcanic rocks, the model discriminates the presence of most mineral categories and predicts which rocks are richer in each mineral. However, the predicted abundances are systematically offset from measured values. Recalibration fit on as few as 25 labeled rocks recovers 76 percent of the likelihood improvement from fitting on the full 368-rock fitting set, while abundance error responds more slowly (11.0% zero-shot and 9.5% with the full fitting set). This work demonstrates how a machine learning model in the geochemical sciences can both leverage an imperfect simulator and mitigate its systematic biases.

</details>


### [155] [Learning atomic interactions for the thermodynamics of liquid mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36440)
*Marco Hoffmann,Sebastian Schmitt,Hans Hasse,Fabian Jirasek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出COGNAC——一种在图神经网络中学习原子级几何与相互作用参数、并在UNIFAC框架内求值的模型，兼顾ML精度与UNIFAC级计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活度系数预测模型是过程设计与优化的基础。UNIFAC等基团贡献法计算便宜且已集成于流程模拟器，但精度有限；HANNA等现代机器学习模型精度高、适用广，但集成到流程模拟中代价高昂甚至不可行。需要一种同时具备高精度与低计算成本的模型。

Method: COGNAC为图神经网络，学习依赖于分子邻域的原子级几何与相互作用参数，并将其代入UNIFAC框架进行活度系数评估；模型在约82万条实验数据点上训练。

Result: 在二元及多组分混合物上精度显著高于UNIFAC与HANNA，相对HANNA误差降低13–63%；可可靠预测多相平衡并识别共沸物；参数具有可解释性；严格满足热力学一致性；计算成本与UNIFAC相当。

Conclusion: 通过将ML参数学习嵌入UNIFAC物理框架，COGNAC在保持UNIFAC级计算成本与热力学一致性的同时获得超越HANNA的精度，从而使机器学习模型可用于过程模拟。

Abstract: Predictive models for activity coefficients, which describe the non-ideality of liquid mixtures, are foundational for process design and optimization. Group-contribution methods, such as UNIFAC, are computationally cheap and well established in process simulators. Modern machine learning models, such as HANNA, offer higher accuracy and broader applicability, but their integration into process simulation is expensive or even infeasible. In this work, we introduce COGNAC, a graph neural network that learns atom-level geometry and interaction parameters that depend on the molecular neighborhood and evaluates them in the UNIFAC framework. Trained on about 820,000 experimental data points, COGNAC achieves significantly higher accuracy than UNIFAC and HANNA for binary and multi-component mixtures, reduces error relative to HANNA by 13-63%, reliably predicts multi-phase equilibria and identifies azeotropes, yields interpretable parameters, and is strictly thermodynamically consistent. COGNAC evaluations run at a cost comparable to UNIFAC, thereby unlocking the potential of machine-learning models for process simulations.

</details>


### [156] [High-Fidelity Aerostructural Dataset of Transonic Wings in Cruise Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36442)
*Pavankumar Koratikere,Abhijnan Dikshit,Leifur Leifsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发布了一个面向跨声速机翼静气动-结构耦合分析的高保真多学科数据集（2471个样本+3个分布外样本），并训练先进ML模型验证其用于预测气动表面量、气动性能积分量与翼盒失效分布的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公开的基于ML的机翼设计数据集主要关注气动性能，较少考虑实际设计中的多学科耦合；高效机翼设计需同时兼顾气动性能与结构完整性，但目前缺乏高保真捕捉该耦合气动-结构行为的公开数据集。

Method: 构建高保真多学科数据集：平面形状由翼展、后掠、上反、梢弦、梢扭转参数化，翼型截面采用CST参数化，分析条件由马赫数、攻角、高度、载荷因数参数化；共2471样本及3个分布外样本，并训练SOTA ML模型预测气动表面量、积分气动量与翼盒失效分布。

Result: ML模型在气动量预测上NRMSE最优达0.0309，在翼盒失效分布预测上NRMSE最优达0.1189。

Conclusion: 该数据集为多样跨声速机翼提供耦合气动与结构响应，建立了开发、评估与比较多物理场ML模型的基准，并期望推动各工程领域构建类似高保真多物理场基准数据集。

Abstract: Recent advances in machine learning (ML) have the potential to substantially accelerate aircraft wing design, a process that has traditionally required extensive high-fidelity simulation and design iteration. Existing public datasets for ML-based wing design have focused primarily on aerodynamic performance, with limited consideration of the multidisciplinary interactions that govern practical aircraft design. In particular, efficient wing design requires simultaneous consideration of aerodynamic performance and structural integrity, yet no publicly available datasets capture this coupled aerostructural behavior at high-fidelity. To address this need, a high-fidelity multidisciplinary dataset for static aerostructural analysis of transonic wings is introduced. The dataset includes 2471 samples, along with three out-of-distribution samples. The wing planform geometry is parameterized by span, sweep, dihedral, tip chord, and tip twist, while the airfoil cross-sections are parameterized using the class-shape transformation method. The analysis is further parameterized by Mach number, angle of attack, altitude, and structural load factor. The utility of the dataset is demonstrated by training state-of-the-art ML models to predict aerodynamic surface quantities, integrated aerodynamic performance quantities, and the failure distribution of the wingbox. ML models achieve at most 0.0309 normalized root mean squared error (NRMSE) in the prediction of aerodynamic quantities and at most 0.1189 NRMSE in the prediction of failure distribution of the wingbox. By providing coupled aerodynamic and structural responses for a diverse set of transonic wings, this dataset establishes a benchmark for developing, evaluating, and comparing multi-physics ML models for wing design. Broadly, it is intended to encourage creation of similar high-fidelity multi-physics benchmark datasets across engineering domains.

</details>


### [157] [Split Federated Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36465)
*Jia Chen,Yecheng Lv,Xinrong Hu,Mingdi Huang,Jun Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文首次形式化分割联邦遗忘（SFU）问题，理论刻画历史影响传播与模式依赖的可行性，并提出SURE方法实现有效的遗忘-效用平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分割联邦学习（SFL）结合模型分割执行与联邦聚合，但跨分区耦合与双侧聚合导致的历史影响传播使“移除已学习的贡献”十分困难，现有工作缺乏系统研究。

Method: 1）首次系统形式化SFU，覆盖客户端级与类别级移除、反事实重训练、分区特定更新约束；2）理论分析历史影响传播与模式依赖的遗忘可行性，揭示受限模型更新的结构性局限；3）提出SURE，融合双重重要性估计、选择性参数扰动与重要性引导恢复。

Result: 将现有遗忘方法适配到SFL的广泛实验显示，在不同模型架构、数据分布与更新模式下存在显著的遗忘-效用权衡；SURE以适度的额外开销达到与模式匹配重训练相当的吻合度；进一步分析揭示分割位置与更新约束对遗忘行为的影响。

Conclusion: SFU应被视为独立的研究问题，本文为分区式联邦系统中的遗忘提供了理论与实践基础。

Abstract: Split federated learning (SFL) combines partitioned model execution with federated aggregation to support collaborative learning across distributed Web and edge environments. However, removing learned contributions in SFL is challenging due to cross-partition coupling and historical influence propagation through dual-side aggregation. To the best of our knowledge, we provide the first systematic formulation of Split Federated Unlearning (SFU), encompassing client-wise and class-wise removal, counterfactual retraining, and partition-specific update constraints. We theoretically characterize historical influence propagation and mode-dependent unlearning feasibility, revealing structural limitations of restricted model updates. As an initial solution, we propose SURE, integrating dual-importance estimation, selective parameter perturbation, and importance-guided recovery. Extensive experiments with existing unlearning methods adapted to SFL reveal substantial forgetting-utility trade-offs across model architectures, data distributions, and update modes. SURE achieves competitive agreement with mode-matched retraining at modest additional cost, while further analyses highlight the impact of split position and update constraints on unlearning behavior. These findings establish SFU as a distinct research problem and provide a foundation for unlearning in partitioned federated systems.

</details>


### [158] [CoHARL: Co-Evolving Agent Policies and Executable Harnesses with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36469)
*Jianwen Chen,Peng Xia,Kaide Zeng,Siwei Han,Zhen-Yu Zhang,Charles Fleming,Xinyu Ye,Huaxiu Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CoHARL框架，在策略训练过程中协同进化共享策略与一组可执行harness（工具调用与反馈接口），通过群体rollout与proposer修订harness代码，在SWE-rebench上取得27.6%平均解决率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两处割裂：固定策略下优化harness忽略了策略在学习中行为的变化；固定harness训练策略则使其生成训练经验的接口保持不变。因此需要在策略训练全过程中自适应调整harness。

Method: 基于群体的强化学习框架：维护一个共享策略和一个可执行harness种群，策略由跨种群的rollout进行学习；proposer利用执行轨迹在训练中修订harness代码。

Result: 在SWE-rebench上达到27.6%平均解决率，较仅做harness搜索提升6.4个百分点；在SWE-bench Verified与Terminal-Bench 2.0上于软件工程与终端任务均取得增益；在同等训练预算下优于"先搜索harness再RL"与"策略更新与单一活跃harness修订交替"两种基线。

Conclusion: 策略与可执行harness的协同进化优于单向优化，harness应作为随策略共同演化的训练组件。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents interact with their environments through executable harnesses that govern tool use and feedback. Optimizing a harness for a fixed policy overlooks how its behavior changes during learning, while training a policy under a fixed harness leaves the interface that generates its training experience unchanged. This motivates adapting the harness throughout policy training. We introduce CoHARL, a population-based reinforcement learning framework that co-evolves a shared policy and a population of executable harnesses. The policy learns from rollouts across the population, and a proposer uses its execution traces to revise harness code during training. CoHARL achieves a 27.6% mean resolved rate on SWE-rebench, improving over harness-only search by 6.4 percentage points. Experiments on SWE-bench Verified and Terminal-Bench 2.0 show gains in software engineering and terminal-based tasks. Under matched training budgets, CoHARL also outperforms searching for a harness once before RL and alternating policy updates with revisions to a single active harness.

</details>


### [159] [Underdamped Anchored Langevin Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36470)
*Qi Feng,Hoang M. Nguyen,Lingjiong Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出欠阻尼锚定Langevin动力学（UALD）及其离散算法UALMC，首个针对不可微与重尾目标、无渐近偏差的动量型Langevin采样方法，并给出2-Wasserstein非渐近收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典ULA等过阻尼Langevin算法依赖可微对数密度、对重尾分布采样效率低；已有的不可微与重尾采样方法均局限于过阻尼框架。

Method: 构造动量型欠阻尼锚定Langevin动力学：用光滑参考势替代原势，在漂移项中引入乘性缩放修正欠阻尼Langevin动力学；基于该动力学的离散格式设计UALMC算法。

Result: 给出到目标分布2-Wasserstein距离的非渐近收敛保证，迭代复杂度在维度和精度上的依赖优于过阻尼锚定Langevin算法；数值实验验证理论与实际性能。

Conclusion: 首次将锚定思想扩展到欠阻尼（动量）框架，实现无渐近偏差的不可微/重尾采样，复杂度更优。

Abstract: Classical Langevin Monte Carlo algorithms, such as the unadjusted Langevin algorithm (ULA), are based on discretization schemes of the overdamped Langevin dynamics and are widely used for sampling in machine learning because they scale to high dimensions and large datasets. However, they require differentiable log-densities, and are less efficient for sampling heavy-tailed distributions. Although various approaches have been proposed in the literature for sampling non-differentiable and heavy-tailed targets, all of them are developed in the overdamped setting only. We propose a novel momentum-based underdamped anchored Langevin dynamics (UALD), and underdamped anchored Langevin Monte Carlo (UALMC) algorithm based on a discretization scheme of UALD. Our paper is the first to provide a kinetic (momentum-based) Langevin algorithm for non-differentiable and heavy-tailed targets that is free of asymptotic bias. The method replaces the original potential with a smooth reference potential and modifies the underdamped Langevin dynamics via multiplicative scaling in the drift term. We establish non-asymptotic guarantees in the 2-Wasserstein distance to the target distribution and show that the resulting iteration complexity has better dependence on dimension and accuracy level compared to overdamped-based anchored Langevin algorithms. We provide numerical experiments to illustrate the theory and practical performance of our proposed approach.

</details>


### [160] [Proposing Better Rollouts for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36477)
*Sirui Chen,Yunzhe Qi,Haobo Xu,Jiaru Zou,Mengting Ai,Zihao Li,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出on-policy distillation (OPD)中固定的rollout策略会导致师生不匹配累积，提出MPOPD，将rollout策略本身作为优化对象，构造学生与教师策略的几何混合提议分布，在16个基准上一致优于OPD及变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD中rollout策略通常直接从当前学生采样，这迫使教师对学生采样到的所有轨迹提供监督，包括教师自身难以到达的轨迹；在这些轨迹上教师监督噪声大甚至误导，导致重复等不良模式，且因后续轮次rollout由更新后学生决定，该问题会持续并放大。由此作者认为需把rollout策略本身作为优化目标，而非仅修改蒸馏目标。

Method: 提出Mixture Proposal On-Policy Distillation (MPOPD)：基于相对教师支持度的局部信赖域目标，导出以学生为锚、教师引导的rollout分布，其目标为student与teacher策略的几何混合；并引入近似该混合的proposal policy，使rollout可在单次前向中生成。

Result: 在16个数学、知识与医学推理基准上相较OPD及近期变体取得一致提升；在Qwen3-4B-Base的数学推理上，平均准确率较OPD提升6.91，同时平均生成长度降低41%。

Conclusion: rollout策略设计是改进OPD的有效途径：将提议分布从纯学生策略改为学生-教师几何混合，可缓解复合的师生不匹配，在提升精度的同时显著缩短生成长度。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) improves language-model post-training by letting the student learn from its own sampled trajectories under teacher supervision, aligning the training process with inference-time behavior. However, the rollout policy in OPD is usually treated as fixed: each round samples directly from the current student. We show that this choice can create a compounding teacher--student mismatch, because it requires the teacher to provide effective supervision on whichever trajectories the student samples, including ones less reachable by the teacher itself. On such trajectories, teacher supervision can become noisy or even misleading, leading to undesirable student patterns such as repetition. Moreover, since the updated student in turn defines the rollout policy of subsequent rounds, these patterns persist and potentially amplify over training. This failure mode suggests that improving OPD requires treating the rollout policy itself as an optimization target, rather than only modifying the distillation objective. We propose Mixture Proposal On-Policy Distillation (MPOPD), which derives a student-anchored, teacher-guided rollout distribution from a local trust-region objective based on relative teacher support. The resulting target is a geometric mixture of the student and teacher policies, and we introduce a proposal policy that approximates this mixture, enabling rollout generation in a single forward pass. Experiments on 16 math, knowledge, and medical reasoning benchmarks show consistent gains over OPD and recent variants. On math reasoning, our method improves average accuracy by 6.91 over OPD on Qwen3-4B-Base while reducing average generation length by 41%, suggesting that rollout policy design provides an effective way to improve OPD.

</details>


### [161] [NextEval: An Agent-Based Solver and Benchmark for Derivative-Free Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36553)
*Shuo Chen,Li Cui,Cunxin Huang,Ya-Feng Liu,Minghang Xie,Meng Xu,Zaikun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于LLM智能体的无导数优化求解器NextEval：LLM智能体在数值环境中选择工具、配置参数并分配评估预算，数值方法负责计算候选点；同时提出NextEval Bench基准协议，在低维DFO问题上函数评估效率优于最先进基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无导数优化（DFO）中导数不可得或代价高昂，且黑盒目标函数评估本身昂贵，因此在有限预算下有效利用累积观测、选择有信息量的评估点是核心挑战。

Method: 构建LLM智能体与数值环境耦合的系统：环境提供分析历史评估与执行数值方法的工具，并维护共享评估历史与方法特定状态以支持方法切换后的延续；LLM智能体依据观测与工具反馈选择工具、配置参数、分配评估预算，数值方法则计算并评估候选点。

Result: 提出NextEval Bench基准协议，在相同评估预算与信息约束下用同一数值工具比较不同智能体配置；低维DFO问题上的数值结果表明NextEval在函数评估效率上优于最先进的DFO基线。

Conclusion: 将LLM智能体作为DFO的顺序决策层、以数值方法为执行层，是提升评估预算利用效率的有效途径；配套的基准协议为公平比较智能体配置提供了可复现的评测框架。

Abstract: Derivative-free optimization (DFO) addresses problems where derivatives are unavailable or costly to obtain. This setting often arises in simulation-based and experimental applications, where evaluating the black-box objective can itself be costly. Thus, it remains a central challenge in DFO to effectively exploit accumulated observations to select informative evaluation points under a limited budget. In this light, we introduce the NextEval solver, an agent-based DFO solver, which integrates the capabilities of a large language model (LLM) to guide the sequential selection of evaluation points. Specifically, we establish a system that combines an LLM agent with a numerical environment. The environment provides tools for analyzing past evaluations and executing numerical methods, while maintaining a shared evaluation history and method-specific state to support continuation across method switches. Using observations and tool feedback, the LLM agent selects tools, configures their parameters, and allocates the evaluation budget, while numerical methods compute and evaluate candidate points. Building on this system, we also introduce NextEval Bench, a benchmark protocol for comparing agent configurations on shared DFO tasks using the same numerical tools under matched evaluation budgets and information constraints. Numerical results on low-dimensional DFO problems demonstrate that the NextEval solver outperforms the state-of-the-art DFO baselines in function-evaluation efficiency.

</details>


### [162] [Learning from Points and Patches: A Pre-trained Hybrid Attention Transformer for Time Series Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36572)
*Peiwang Tang,Cheng HE,Yupeng Ye,Weitai Zhang,Qinghua Sun,Defu Lian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出通用时间序列预训练框架TimeHAT，通过统一点-片段掩码策略（UPPMS）解决"学什么"，通过混合注意力编码器（HAE）解决"怎么学"，在四类下游任务及微调/少样本/零样本设置下表现有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web服务与互联系统产生的时间序列支撑用户活动分析、服务监控与资源管理等应用，但从异构时间序列中学习可迁移表示仍具挑战：时序模式多样、序列长度不一、高质量标注有限。NLP与CV中预训练虽成功，但时间序列预训练需针对时序数据定制策略。作者据此将问题拆解为"学什么（what to learn）"与"怎么学（how to learn）"两个互补问题。

Method: 1) UPPMS（Unified Point-Patch Masking Strategy）：在统一框架内融合点级与片段级监督，同时执行上下文重建与因果外推，使模型既捕捉细粒度时序变化又捕捉宏观时序模式。2) HAE（Hybrid Attention Encoder）：浅层采用前缀约束的双向注意力，深层采用方向性混合注意力，在保留有效上下文交互的同时缓解秩退化（rank degeneration）。

Result: 在真实世界数据集与四类下游任务（分类、长期预测、插补、异常检测）上进行评估，覆盖微调、少样本与零样本迁移设置，各项任务均取得有竞争力的性能。代码已开源。

Conclusion: TimeHAT作为通用时间序列预训练框架，验证了将预训练目标设计与注意力结构设计分别对应"学什么/怎么学"的有效性，展现出跨任务、跨监督预算的可迁移性与通用性。

Abstract: Time-series data generated by Web services and connected systems support applications such as user activity analysis, service monitoring, and resource management. However, learning transferable representations from heterogeneous time series remains challenging due to diverse temporal patterns, variable sequence lengths, and limited high-quality annotations. Despite the success of pre-training in natural language processing and computer vision, effective time-series pre-training requires strategies tailored to temporal data.
  We propose TimeHAT (Hybrid Attention Transformer), a general-purpose pre-training framework for time-series analysis that addresses two complementary questions: what to learn and how to learn. For what to learn, we introduce a Unified Point-Patch Masking Strategy (UPPMS) that integrates point-level and patch-level supervision within a unified framework for contextual reconstruction and causal extrapolation, enabling the model to capture both fine-grained temporal variations and broader temporal patterns. For how to learn, we develop a Hybrid Attention Encoder (HAE) that combines prefix-constrained bidirectional attention in initial layers with directional hybrid attention in deeper layers, aiming to preserve effective contextual interactions while mitigating rank degeneration.
  We evaluate TimeHAT across real-world datasets and four downstream tasks: classification, long-term forecasting, imputation, and anomaly detection. Experiments cover fine-tuning, few-shot, and zero-shot transfer settings, demonstrating competitive performance across diverse time-series analysis tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/TangPeiwang/TimeHAT.

</details>


### [163] [Less is more: learning what to profile for GPU kernel optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36576)
*Yihan Wang,Peiyu Liu,Runyu Chen,Wei Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AutoProfiler，从优化反馈中学习确定性的剖析决策树，为每个kernel自适应选择硬件剖析信息，在受限剖析预算下优于固定剖析配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM自动优化GPU kernel时，其效果高度依赖提供给模型的硬件信息；现有系统依赖预定义的剖析策略，将"剖析什么"排除在优化过程之外。作者观察到剖析指标越多并非单调提升效果，且最优剖析信息因kernel而异。

Method: 理论分析表明在剖析效用异质的情形下，以kernel为条件的自适应选择可优于任何单一固定剖析配置。据此构建反馈驱动的框架，用LLM生成候选修改，通过REWRITE与SPLIT操作迭代精炼一棵确定性剖析树，并由经验评估决定是否接受修改；树建成后对任意kernel快速收集所需剖析信息，使LLM重写器仅需调用一次。

Result: 在KernelBench上，在受限剖析预算下相比固定剖析配置提升了kernel优化效果。

Conclusion: 硬件剖析应从静态指标采集转向"剖析推理"，使剖析本身成为提升kernel优化的新维度。

Abstract: Large language models have recently shown strong potential for automatically optimizing GPU kernels, yet their effectiveness depends critically on the hardware information available to the kernel optimization model. Existing systems typically rely on predefined profiling strategies, leaving what to profile largely outside the optimization process. We show that effective profiling is not simply about collecting more information: increasing the number of profiling metrics does not monotonically improve kernel optimization, while the most useful profiling information varies across kernels. Our theoretical analysis further shows that, under such heterogeneous profiling utility, kernel-conditioned selection can outperform any single fixed profiling configuration. Motivated by this observation, we propose AutoProfiler, a feedback-driven framework for learning a deterministic profiling tree from optimization feedback. The tree is iteratively refined through REWRITE and SPLIT operations, with an LLM generating candidate modifications and empirical evaluation determining their acceptance. Once constructed, the tree quickly gathers the required profiling information for any kernel, allowing the LLM rewriter to be invoked only once. Experiments on KernelBench show that our approach improves kernel optimization over fixed profiling configurations under constrained profiling budgets. These results suggest that hardware profiling should evolve from static metric collection toward profiling reasoning, making profiling itself a new dimension for improving kernel optimization. The codes are available at https://github.com/Satissss/AutoProfiler.

</details>


### [164] [Balanced State Subspaces for Mamba-2 State Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36617)
*Shang Tingyu,Cao Wenfei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于平衡截断的模型状态压缩方法（写作 \method{}），将压缩目标从"逐坐标"转向"写-读子空间"，并配套 HARP 层间秩分配策略，实现单次前向校准即可将 Mamba-2 状态维度减半而几乎不损性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mamba-2 将所有上下文压缩进固定大小的循环状态，该状态主导解码时的内存与带宽开销。现有压缩器（如 GHOST）在原始基底上逐通道独立打分，或量化状态值、或剪枝坐标。作者观察到训练后的状态能量近似秩一，但输出并不读取能量集中的方向：能量集中在循环累积出的偏置式方向上，而输出真正读取的内容位于与状态坐标不对齐的低维子空间。由于即使"输出感知"的打分也是逐坐标计算，因而会错过决定输入-输出映射的关键方向，所以压缩应针对写-读子空间而非单个坐标。

Method: 提出 \method{}：一种训练后变换，用完整的可控性与可观性 Gramian 实例化平衡截断，并借助 Mamba-2 的标量恒等转移结构精确施加旋转。观察到有用秩在层间差异很大，进一步提出 HARP：依据 Hankel 谱为每层分配秩，并保护对检索敏感的层，从而在统一预算下同时改善困惑度与检索表现。校准仅需一次前向传播，比 GHOST 公开协议快一个数量级，峰值内存更低。

Result: 在 130M–2.7B 规模上，配合 HARP 的 \method{} 将状态维度减半，WikiText-2 困惑度平均仅上升 0.3 点（1.8%），而 GHOST 上升 1.6 点（9.3%）；在 25% 保留率下差距进一步拉大。

Conclusion: 状态压缩应以写-读子空间为目标，而非逐坐标指标；平衡截断结合谱驱动的层间秩分配是一种高效、低开销的训练后压缩方案，在大幅削减状态维度的同时保持语言建模与检索性能。

Abstract: Mamba-2 compresses all context into a fixed-size recurrent state that dominates decoding memory and bandwidth. Existing compressors quantize state values or prune state coordinates, scoring each channel independently in the native basis. We examine trained Mamba-2 states and find that energy is almost rank-one, but the output does not read the energetic directions: energy concentrates in a bias-like direction accumulated by the recurrence, while the content the output reads lives in a low-dimensional subspace that is not aligned with the state's coordinates. Even output-aware scores are computed coordinate by coordinate, so they miss the directions that determine the input--output map. Compression should therefore target the write--read subspace rather than individual coordinates. We propose \method{}, a post-training transform that instantiates balanced truncation with full controllability and observability Gramians and applies the rotation exactly via Mamba-2's scalar-identity transition. Because the useful rank varies widely across layers, we further introduce HARP, which allocates ranks per layer from the Hankel spectrum and protects retrieval-sensitive layers, improving both perplexity and retrieval over uniform budgets. Calibration takes a single forward pass, an order of magnitude faster than GHOST's released protocol, with lower peak memory. With HARP, \method{} halves the state dimension at an average WikiText-2 perplexity increase of $0.3$ points ($1.8\%$) across 130M--2.7B, versus $1.6$ ($9.3\%$) for GHOST, and the gap widens at 25\% retention.

</details>


### [165] [When Does Perturbation Consistency Add Information Beyond Softmax Confidence? Theory and Evidence for the Decision Consistency Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36640)
*Amit Kalitaa,Moirangthem Tiken Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 扰动一致性分数(LS/CD/CS/CAG)作为不确定性指标整体不如干净数据的最大softmax概率;理论证明翻转需对数边际≤√2倍对数位移后,经验上CS在AUROC 0.515接近随机;仅在置信被误导的噪声腐败和PGD攻击场景下有一致性增益,且噪声增益不外推。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作普遍把扰动一致性视为不确定性/鲁棒性的有用信号,但其相对干净softmax输出的增量信息缺乏系统检验。本文追问:一致性分数在何时携带softmax输出所没有的信息?

Method: 提出Decision Consistency Framework(DCF):对每张图像在六个轻度算子下计算标签稳定性LS与置信偏差CD并合成一致性分数CS,以准确率与平均一致性之差(CAG)刻画模型。实验覆盖201次评估、每配置三种子;与MSP、logit margin比较;给出标签翻转的logit边际不等式及无标签split-conformal免翻转证书;并在CIFAR-10-C、PGD攻击、OCTMNIST上做分场景验证。

Result: CS在所有模型族上检测干净数据误分类均劣于MSP;冻结CNN的AUROC差0.057(95% CI 0.043–0.073),计算量高8–15倍。固定边际下位移预测错误AUROC仅0.515。split-conformal证书在84次运行中零违反。噪声腐败下CS优于margin,但增益不外推到未见shift族;PGD(2/255)下CS的AUROC 0.83对MSP 0.37。OCTMNIST的大负CAG在全微调与域内训练下持续,先验校正可恢复约40%差距。

Conclusion: 一致性分数并非通用不确定性估计器:在干净数据上性价比不及MSP,且其指示性受logit边际约束;但当置信被分布偏移或对抗扰动误导时,一致性提供了互补信息。大负CAG部分是类先验效应而非稳定性失效。

Abstract: Perturbation consistency scores judge whether a prediction survives small input changes. The Decision Consistency Framework (DCF) scores each image by label stability (LS) and confidence deviation (CD) under six mild operators, combines them into a consistency score (CS), and summarises a model by the gap between accuracy and mean consistency (CAG). We ask when these scores carry information that the clean softmax output lacks. Across 201 evaluations with three seeds per configuration, CS detected misclassifications on clean data less well than the maximum softmax probability (MSP) in every model family. For frozen CNNs, the deficit was 0.057 in area under the ROC curve (AUROC; 95% CI 0.043 to 0.073), at 8 to 15 times the compute. A label flip requires the logit margin to be at most the square root of two times the logit displacement, and at a fixed margin, the displacement predicted errors at AUROC 0.515. The same inequality gives a label-free split-conformal no-flip certificate, never violated in 84 runs. Consistency did add information where confidence is misled: under noise corruptions of CIFAR-10-C, it beat the margin, and under projected gradient attacks, it separated clean from attacked inputs with AUROC 0.83 against 0.37 for MSP at a budget of 2/255, as pre-specified. The gain under noise did not transfer to unseen shift families. The large negative CAG on OCTMNIST persists under full fine-tuning and in-domain training and is partly a class-prior effect: correcting the prior recovers about 40% of the gap.

</details>


### [166] [When Is a Cheap Fairness Edit Safe? Screening Post-Hoc Edits in Two-Sided Matching Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36649)
*Moirangthem Tiken Singha,Rabinder Kumar Prasada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在推理阶段用闭式线性投影器降低受保护属性对双边匹配结果的影响，并在"垂直有序"市场条件下给出平均福利变化的可证明界，配套提出了部署前的三态筛查（PASS/KILL/OUT-OF-SCOPE）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双边匹配系统（如招生、招聘管道）联合分配机会，降低受保护属性的影响通常需要重新训练编码器，成本高且不可控。作者希望在不重训的前提下、于推理阶段完成编辑，并能事先判断该编辑是否可被理论保证所覆盖。

Method: 提出一个闭式线性投影器用于推理期编辑；对编辑前后保持垂直有序的市场，将平均福利变化界定为"编辑引起的吸引力偏移"加显式余项；进一步给出概率化改进，用数据依赖的偏差项替代最坏情况常数；设计部署前筛查，结合垂直性检验与福利扫描，输出PASS、KILL或OUT-OF-SCOPE；并区分精确层级（所有机会均按吸引力排序申请人）与分级层级（允许近垂直市场）。

Result: 30个留出合成市场上，筛查与所有预设regime判定一致，无假PASS；60个精确垂直市场上无任何界被违反，概率界在69%–87%的配置中有信息量；来自三个真实市场的2700个配置（未达精确层级）中无任何plug-in界表达式被违反；闭式分解在90%–100%实例中处于分级范围内，而two-tower与图神经网络编码器全部落在范围之外。

Conclusion: 架构选择影响事后编辑是否可被容许：闭式分解结构天然适配可证明的编辑范围，而two-tower与GNN编码器难以满足。所提筛查可在部署前给出可解释的可容许性判定，但真实市场多数未达精确层级，理论保证覆盖仍有限。

Abstract: Two-sided matching systems, such as admissions pipelines, allocate opportunities jointly. Reducing a protected at tribute's influence usually requires encoder retraining. We study a closed-form linear projector that does this at inference time. For markets that stay vertically ordered before and after the edit, we bound the mean welfare change by the edit-induced appeal shift and an explicit remainder. A probabilistic refinement replaces worst-case constants with a data-dependent deviation term. A pre-deployment screen combines a verticality test with a welfare sweep and returns PASS, KILL, or OUT-OF-SCOPE. An exact tier requires every opportunity to rank applicants by appeal, and a graded tier admits near-vertical markets. On 30 held-out synthetic markets, the screen agrees with every pre-specified regime verdict, with no observed false PASS. On 60 exactly vertical markets, no bound is violated, and the probabilistic bound is informative in 69% to 87% of configurations. On 2700 configurations from three realistic markets, which do not reach the exact tier, no plug-in bound expression is violated. A closed-form factorization stays within graded scope in 90% to 100% of instances, whereas two-tower and graph neural network encoders fall outside it in every instance. Architecture choice thus appears to affect whether a post-hoc edit is admissible.

</details>


### [167] [RankFormer: Rethinking Time Series Transformers through Representation Capacity Analysis and Progressive Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36703)
*Peiwang Tang,Cheng HE,Xijie Liang,Defu Lian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RankFormer编码器-解码器架构，并以秩(rank)分析框架解释时序Transformer的表征容量来源，通过秩增强编码器、Hourglass Attention与交互式渐进解码器提升多变量时序预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer类模型在多变量时序预测中表现优异，但其表征容量的原理缺乏理论理解；同时现有模型多采用通道独立建模与直接投影头等简化解码策略，难以刻画复杂的多变量动态关系。

Method: 建立基于秩的分析框架，考察tokenization、embedding与attention设计对表征容量的影响；据此设计秩增强编码器（Hourglass Attention，提升信息交互中的秩稳定性）与交互式渐进解码器IPD（全局引导的跨通道交互+渐进式精化）。

Result: 分析表明patchification通过将多个时间观测聚合到单个token中提升潜在表征容量，而后续编码操作决定该容量的实际兑现程度；在真实长期预测基准上的大量实验显示RankFormer与SOTA方法相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 以秩视角为时序Transformer的表征容量提供了解释性分析，并据此指导架构设计，验证了秩增强编码与跨通道渐进解码在多变量时序预测中的有效性。

Abstract: Multivariate time series forecasting plays a critical role in Web and cyber-physical systems, where large-scale services and IoT infrastructures generate complex temporal data. Transformer-based models have achieved strong empirical performance in this domain, yet the principles governing the expressive capacity of their learned representations remain insufficiently understood. Moreover, many existing models rely on simplified decoding strategies, including channel-independent modeling and direct projection heads, which limit their ability to capture complex multivariate dynamics. To address these challenges, we propose RankFormer, a novel encoder-decoder architecture for multivariate time series forecasting. We establish a rank-based analysis framework to investigate how tokenization, embedding, and attention designs affect the representation capacity of time series Transformers. Our analysis reveals that patchification increases the potential representation capacity by aggregating multiple temporal observations into each token, while subsequent encoding operations determine how much of this capacity is realized. Guided by these insights, we develop a rank-enhancing encoder with Hourglass Attention to improve rank stability during information interaction. Furthermore, we introduce the Interactive Progressive Decoder (IPD), which improves future trajectory modeling through global-guided cross-channel interaction and progressive refinement. Extensive experiments on real-world long-term forecasting benchmarks demonstrate that RankFormer achieves competitive performance against state-of-the-art methods. Code repository: https://github.com/TangPeiwang/Rankformer.

</details>


### [168] [Select-TTT: Query-Conditioned Selective Fast Weight Updates for In-Place Test-Time Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36794)
*Yue Wu,Yang Zhang,Weinan Gan,Chongming Gao,Xinsen Guo,Xiaoyuan Li,Fuli Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Select-TTT：用查询条件化的写强度调节 In-Place TTT 的快速权重更新，无需监督和新参数，在长上下文问答上稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: In-Place TTT 将 MLP 下投影复用为快速权重，使测试时训练可扩展到 LLM，但在真实网页长上下文问答中收益基本消失。作者归因于其均匀写入调度：每个 chunk 以同等强度写入，无关内容稀释了快速权重记忆中与查询相关的信息。

Method: 提出 Select-TTT，一种模型无关、查询条件化的 In-Place TTT 写入规则：按 chunk 与用户查询的相关性缩放其写入强度。相关性信号无需监督、不引入新参数，直接取自模型冻结的输出头——即 chunk 隐状态分配给查询 token 的概率质量；再用温度缩放的 softmax 将得分转为学习率乘子，保持原有 chunk 级、上下文并行的计算结构不变。

Result: 在两个长上下文问答基准和两个骨干模型上，Select-TTT 均取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 结果凸显查询条件化快速权重写入对实际长上下文问答中测试时训练的价值，并可能适用于涉及多次用户查询、上下文持续演化的长时程智能体任务。

Abstract: Test-time training (TTT) enables a deployed language model to internalize incoming context on-the-fly into a small set of fast-weight parameters, rather than merely treating it as transient context for attention. In-Place TTT recently made this idea practical for large language models by repurposing the down-projection of standard MLP blocks as fast weights. However, we find that its benefit largely disappears on realistic web long-context question answering. We attribute this to In-Place TTT's uniform write schedule: every chunk is written with equal strength, so irrelevant content dilutes query-relevant information in fast-weight memory. Building on this insight, we propose Select-TTT, a model-agnostic, query-conditioned rule for In-Place TTT that scales each chunk's write strength by its relevance to the user query. This relevance signal requires no supervision and no new parameters: it is read directly from the model's frozen output head, as the probability mass that a chunk's hidden states assign to the query tokens. A temperature-scaled softmax turns these scores into learning-rate multipliers, leaving the chunk-wise, context-parallel computation of In-Place TTT intact. Select-TTT consistently improves long-context question answering on two benchmarks and two backbones. These findings highlight the value of query-conditioned fast weight writes for practical test-time training on long-context question answering, which is also potentially promising for long-horizon agent tasks involving multiple user queries over evolving contexts.

</details>


### [169] [Does LLM Pre-Training Typically Occur at the Edge of Stability?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36778)
*Yuhang Cai,Haodong Wen,Haofeng Huang,Deyi Liu,Yiyuan Ma,Kaifeng Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视二次近似作为神经网络训练动力学模型的保真度，指出其在LLM训练中存在持续负曲率导致二次动力学无论学习率多大都发散、而真实动力学稳定的失效模式（Edge of Convexity, EoC），并提出面向自适应优化器随机训练的广义Edge of Stability（EoS）定义，在最大787M参数的LLM预训练中验证EoC普遍存在、EoS普遍但非必然。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二次近似是研究神经网络优化的常用视角，但近期证据（如全批量梯度下降中的Edge of Stability，最大Hessian特征值集中于2/η）挑战了其预测有效性；作者希望系统刻画二次近似在LLM训练中的失效模式与适用边界。

Method: 识别出二次近似的一种独立失效模式：持续负曲率使二次动力学在任何学习率下发散，而真实动力学保持稳定，命名为Edge of Convexity（EoC）；在剔除该与学习率无关的不稳定性后，基于"增大学习率是否使二次动力学显著发散而真实动力学基本不变"，提出适用于自适应优化器随机训练的广义EoS定义；在多种模型规模（至787M）的LLM预训练上实证检验。

Result: (1) EoC在所有LLM预训练中均可观察到；(2) EoS同样普遍但并非必然——当学习率足够小（如衰减后）或批量大小低于某临界阈值（该阈值与临界批量大小正相关）时，EoS消失。

Conclusion: 研究刻画了二次近似在LLM训练中何时以及如何失效，为后续理解训练动力学奠定基础。

Abstract: Quadratic approximations provide a common lens for studying neural network optimization, but recent evidence challenges their predictive validity. In full-batch gradient descent with learning rate (LR) $η$, Cohen et al. (2021) observed the Edge of Stability (EoS), where the largest Hessian eigenvalue concentrates near $2/η$, seemingly at odds with classical stability conditions predicted by quadratic approximations. In this work, we revisit the fidelity of quadratic approximations as a model of neural network training dynamics, with a particular focus on their failure modes in LLM training. We first highlight a distinct failure mode of the quadratic approximation, where persistent negative curvature causes the quadratic dynamics to diverge regardless of the LR, even though the real dynamics remain stable. We term this phenomenon the Edge of Convexity (EoC). After removing the effect of this LR-independent instability, we then propose a generalized definition of Edge of Stability (EoS) for stochastic training with adaptive optimizers, based on whether increasing the LR induces significant divergence in the quadratic dynamics while leaving the real dynamics largely unchanged. Across different LLM pretraining runs with various model sizes up to 787M, we find that (1) EoC is always observed across LLM pretraining, and (2) EoS is also prevalent but not universal: it disappears when the LR becomes sufficiently small (e.g., after decay) or when the batch size falls below a critical threshold that has a positive correlation with the critical batch size. Together, these findings characterize when and how quadratic approximations fail and provide a foundation for future work on understanding training dynamics.

</details>


### [170] [HiLo-RD: Cross-Budget Self-Distillation for Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36901)
*Xiang Li,Chengting Yu,Jungang Li,Yilun Zheng,Junzhuo Li,Xingyao Zhang,Wenbo Su,Xuming Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HiLo-RD：用大窗口 teacher 状态与小窗口裁剪视图在“已提交位置”做隐层表征蒸馏，无外部 teacher 地改进掩码扩散语言模型在有限预算下的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MDLM 需同时决定“生成什么”和“在哪里提交”，在有限生成预算下会出现 budget-induced premature commitment：即便正确解在窗口内可行，模型也会在窗口边界附近过早固定答案片段，使其成为后续预测的上下文并误导推理。现有后训练方法无法直接迁移同一模型在大窗口下的更优轨迹。

Method: 提出 HiLo-RD：将大窗口教师状态与裁剪的小窗口视图配对，并在已提交位置上匹配二者的隐层表征，从而同时对 token 预测与提交顺序施加监督，无需外部教师模型。

Result: 在数学基准上提升低预算准确率并缩短回答；GSM8K 准确率提升 9.4 个百分点，平均可读长度下降约 25%；在无代码域训练下增益可迁移至代码生成。注意力诊断与固定预算下的顺序干预支持所提机制。

Conclusion: 大窗口推理可改进小窗口解码，表征蒸馏是一种有效的无外部教师的预算受限改进手段。

Abstract: We propose HiLo-RD (High-to-Low Budget Representation Distillation) to improve masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) under a limited generation budget. Unlike autoregressive models, MDLMs choose both what to generate and where to commit it. This creates two coupled challenges: predicting useful content before reasoning is complete, and deciding when to fix answer tokens that shape later predictions. We identify budget-induced premature commitment: even when a correct solution fits, the model can fix answer fragments near the window boundary before resolving intermediate steps. These fragments become context for later predictions and redirect subsequent reasoning. Existing post-training methods do not directly transfer the stronger trajectories that the same model follows with a larger window. HiLo-RD addresses this gap by (1) pairing large-window teacher states with cropped small-window views and (2) matching their hidden representations at committed positions. This supervision guides both token prediction and commitment order without an external teacher. Across mathematics benchmarks, HiLo-RD improves low-budget accuracy and shortens responses; these gains extend to code generation without code-domain training. On GSM8K, accuracy rises by 9.4 percentage points as mean readable length falls by about 25%. Attention diagnostics and fixed-budget order interventions support the proposed mechanism. Together, these results show that large-window reasoning can improve small-window decoding.

</details>


### [171] [V-LUMEN: Visual Lookup Memory for Embedding Scaling in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37019)
*Miso Choi,Daekeun Kim,Eunji Kim,Jungbeom Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 V-LUMEN：面向视觉语言模型（VLM）的视觉查表记忆，通过冻结码本离散化视觉特征、空间感知哈希构建记忆键、文本条件调制检索，并以门控残差注入骨干，在不增加逐token计算的前提下扩展容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入级缩放通过查表式记忆扩展语言模型容量，而非加深/加宽或增加稠密计算。但将其迁移到VLM存在困难：视觉特征连续、具空间结构且依赖查询，不像离散文本token天然支持 n-gram 查表。因此作者希望为VLM构建可行的外部视觉记忆容量轴。

Method: V-LUMEN 使用冻结码本对视觉特征离散化；通过基于空间聚合的哈希构建具空间感知的记忆键；以文本条件相关性信号对键进行调制；检索寻址完全非参数化；检索到的嵌入经门控残差通路融合进骨干网络；查表成本与表规模无关，且仅在 prefill 阶段发生一次。

Result: 在五个推理密集型基准上，V-LUMEN 显著提升冻结的 Qwen3-VL-2B 骨干；在三个图像与训练数据不相交的基准上实现零样本迁移。在参数预算匹配条件下，将部分 LoRA 或 MoE 适配器容量转入 V-LUMEN，在两个骨干上均保持准确率，说明外部视觉记忆是独立的容量轴而非更强的适配方法。

Conclusion: V-LUMEN 表明视觉查表记忆可为VLM提供独立于参数与稠密计算的容量扩展途径：容量随存储增长而非逐token计算增长，且与现有适配方法（LoRA/MoE）互补而非替代。

Abstract: Embedding-level scaling expands the capacity of language models through lookup-based memory rather than by increasing model depth, width, or dense computation. Extending this idea to vision-language models (VLMs) is challenging because visual features are continuous, spatially structured, and query-dependent, unlike discrete text tokens that naturally support n-gram lookup.
