We introduce V-LUMEN, a visual lookup memory for VLMs. V-LUMEN discretizes visual features with a frozen codebook, builds spatially aware memory keys through spatial aggregation-based hashing, and modulates them with text-conditioned relevance signals; all addressing is non-parametric, and the retrieved embeddings are fused into the backbone through a gated residual pathway. On five reasoning-intensive benchmarks, V-LUMEN substantially improves a frozen Qwen3-VL-2B backbone and transfers zero-shot to three benchmarks whose images are disjoint from the training data. At a matched parameter budget, moving part of a LoRA or MoE adapter's capacity into V-LUMEN preserves accuracy on two backbones, indicating that external visual memory is an independent capacity axis rather than a stronger adaptation method. Because lookup cost is constant in table size and incurred only once at prefill, this capacity grows in storage rather than in per-token compute. Code is available at https://github.com/miso-choi/V-Lumen.

</details>


### [172] [Provenance Signatures for Low Rank Adaptations of Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36933)
*Tobias Cheung,Mohan Dantam,Marc Juarez,Rik Sarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向开放世界场景的扩散模型溯源协议，仅利用生成图像集合的内在维度作为黑盒签名，通过假设检验区分基础模型、LoRA 适配版本与完全不同的模型家族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA 等高效适配方法使扩散模型可被广泛定制，但也可能被在线服务用于生成有偏、有害或非法图像。现有模型识别工作多假设候选模型集合已知，无法应对适配版本与替代模型均未知的真实情况，因此需要一个开放世界下的溯源方法与威胁模型。

Method: 明确溯源问题的精确设置与威胁模型，指出多模型家族与多适配并存带来的两类问题；采用假设检验框架，构造仅基于所生成图像集合内在维度的黑盒模型签名，在无需知道候选适配与替代模型的情况下进行判别。

Result: 所提协议能够在开放世界设定下区分基础模型、其 LoRA 适配版本以及完全不同的模型家族，突破了以往局限于已知候选列表的模型识别做法。

Conclusion: 基于内在维度的黑盒签名是一种可行且实用的扩散模型溯源手段，为在线服务中适配模型的责任归属与合规审查提供了方法基础。

Abstract: Diffusion models have revolutionized the generation of realistic artificial images. Efficient techniques such as Low Rank Adaptation (LoRA) can modify these models for particular use cases, making them more diverse and widely usable. At the same time, open base models may be adapted to deliver biased, harmful or illegal images in an online service. In this paper, we develop a protocol to detect when a service may be using an adaptation of an otherwise standard model. We describe the precise setup and threat model for this provenance question and define two different problems arising due the presence of multiple model families and multiple adaptations. In contrast to previous works that have considered model identification within lists of known candidate models, we consider the realistic open world scenario where the set of possible adaptations and alternative models are unknown. Using a hypothesis testing approach, our protocols can distinguish between the base model, a LoRA adaptation and other completely different families of models. For this test, we develop a black box signature of the models based only on intrinsic dimension of sets of images generated by the models.

</details>


### [173] [EnergyCal: Taming Loss Spikes in Muon-Family Optimizers for Sparse MoE Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37062)
*Masato Fujitake*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EnergyCal：按路由器专家更新方向的归一化平方Frobenius范数衰减其更新，抑制Muon族优化器训练稀疏MoE时的loss spike，无需额外优化器状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏MoE语言模型在Muon族优化器下训练易出现大幅loss spike，需要低开销的稳定化手段。

Method: 提出EnergyCal规则：保持谱变换不变，仅依据有限步Newton-Schulz方向的归一化平方Frobenius范数对路由专家更新进行衰减；不引入持久优化器状态。在4B参数MoE模型上评估，与开发集选出的低学习率MuonH配方对比，并测试两种指数下的原生Muon（匹配名义学习率与权重衰减）。

Result: 相对低学习率MuonH，EnergyCal-MuonH将平均阈值化loss spike负担降低99.51%，最终几何平均困惑度降低4.06%，吞吐开销适中；原生Muon下平均spike负担降低95.98%，并有小幅终点改善。

Conclusion: 在所评估设定中，EnergyCal在有无Hyperball两种情况下均能有效稳定Muon族优化器下的MoE训练。

Abstract: Sparse mixture-of-experts (MoE) language-model training can exhibit large loss spikes with Muon-family optimizers. We introduce EnergyCal, a lightweight rule that attenuates routed-expert updates according to the normalized squared Frobenius norm of their finite-step Newton-Schulz directions. EnergyCal leaves the spectral transform unchanged and adds no persistent optimizer state. We evaluate it on a 4B-parameter MoE model. Against a development-selected, lower-learning-rate MuonH recipe, EnergyCal-MuonH reduces mean thresholded loss-spike burden by 99.51% and final geometric-mean perplexity by 4.06%, with a modest throughput cost. With plain Muon at matched nominal learning rate and weight decay, the reduction in mean spike burden averages 95.98% across the two tested exponents, accompanied by small endpoint improvements. These findings support stabilization both with and without Hyperball in the evaluated setting.

</details>


### [174] [Self-Regularized Flow Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37115)
*Theodoros Kouzelis,Spyros Gidaris,Nikos Komodakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SERF：以去噪轨迹为自监督信号，通过"噪声→干净"自蒸馏改进连续扩散语言模型，无需外部预训练模型、不改变推理流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续流式语言模型通常只用干净目标 token 做监督，去噪轨迹这一丰富的过程信号被浪费。

Method: EMA 教师接收同一插值路径上更干净的输入，对学生提供额外监督；两种变体：SERF-L 对齐中间层表示与更深教师表示，SERF-P 对齐输出 token 分布（可在学生输出层或早期层）。

Result: 在 one-hot FLM、隐空间 ELF、超球面 S-FLM 三类范式上均显著提升：OpenWebText 上最高 7.7 倍实际训练加速，MAUVE +24.3%，GSM8K 准确率最高 +28.8%，采样步数最多减少 64 倍即可匹配基线。

Conclusion: 噪声到干净自蒸馏是提升连续扩散语言模型训练效率与生成质量的有效通用手段。

Abstract: We introduce SERF (Self-Regularized Flow Language Models), a simple self-supervised framework for improving continuous diffusion language models by exploiting the denoising trajectory itself. While continuous flow-based language models offer a promising alternative to autoregressive generation, their training objectives typically rely on supervision from clean target tokens, leaving the denoising trajectory underexploited as a learning signal. SERF addresses this limitation through noisy-to-clean self-distillation: an exponential moving average (EMA) teacher receives a cleaner version of the student's noisy input along the same interpolation path and provides additional supervision. We investigate two variants: SERF-L, which aligns the student's intermediate representations with deeper teacher representations, and SERF-P, which aligns their output token distributions, either at the student's output or at an early student layer. All variants augment the standard training objective without requiring external pretrained models or modifying the inference procedure. Experiments across three continuous language-model paradigms (one-hot FLM, latent ELF, and hyper-spherical S-FLM) demonstrate substantial improvements in training efficiency, generation quality, and few-step generation. In particular, SERF achieves up to 7.7x wall-clock training acceleration on OpenWebText, improves MAUVE by 24.3% and GSM8K accuracy by up to 28.8%, and matches baseline performance with up to 64x fewer sampling steps. These results establish noisy-to-clean self-distillation as an effective approach to improving continuous diffusion language models.

</details>


### [175] [TDLM: Adaptive Temporal Integration for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36940)
*Bohang Sun,Max Zhu,Francesco Caso,Christopher Irwin,Jialin Yu,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TDLM，让掩码扩散语言模型中每个未解析位置通过因果时序注意力访问自身隐藏表示历史，在冻结的去噪器中复用已完成的计算，训练仅增约0.84%参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散语言模型在每个去噪步都要为未解析token重复计算表示，而传统去噪器不会显式保留这些跨步表示以供复用，导致重复去噪计算被浪费。

Method: TDLM在每个未解析位置引入对自身隐藏表示历史的因果时序注意力（temporal self-conditioning），使后续预测可利用已完成的计算；训练基于自生成轨迹，对相邻采样解码步之间被揭示的token采用简单交叉熵目标，因果时序注意力允许训练时并行评估；时序模块加在冻结去噪器上，仅新增约0.84%可训练参数。

Result: 在LLaDA与Dream上，生成速度最高提升至原扩散语言模型的7.15×，评测任务上的答案质量具有竞争力；相较LLaDA参数开销约0.84%。

Conclusion: 时序记忆是更有效利用重复去噪计算的一种实用途径，为扩散语言模型的高效推理提供了动机与实证支持。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models repeatedly compute representations for unresolved tokens, but conventional denoisers do not explicitly retain these representations for reuse across steps. We introduce Temporal Self-Conditioning Diffusion Language Model (TDLM), which lets each unresolved position attend to its own history of hidden representations within a frozen denoiser. This gives later predictions access to computation already performed. Training uses self-generated trajectories and a simple cross-entropy objective for tokens revealed between consecutively sampled decoding steps, while causal temporal attention permits parallel evaluation during training. On LLaDA, the temporal modules add only approximately 0.84% trainable parameters. Across LLaDA and Dream, the main comparison shows up to 7.15$\times$ faster generation than the corresponding original diffusion language model, with competitive answer quality on the evaluated tasks. These results motivate temporal memory as a practical way to make more effective use of repeated denoising computation.

</details>


### [176] [Algorithmic Monoculture in Machine Learning-based Site-Specific Fertilizer Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36941)
*Abdelghani Belgaid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出机器学习推荐模型通常逐一评估，但模型在多主体互联决策中部署时误差会聚合；作者用共享误差与农场特有误差模型把期望社会损失与模型采纳份额联系起来，以HHI刻画集中效应，并给出协方差推广形式、双模型阈值与凸组合配置规则，用摩洛哥五个谷类系统九个模型族做基准校准的算例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习推荐常以单模型为单位评估，但实际部署涉及相互关联的决策者。对连续NPK施肥处方而言，相关误差可能跨农场聚合，产生系统性成本，而现有评估框架缺乏刻画"采纳结构+误差依赖"的生态系统级评价变量。

Method: 构建共享误差与农场特定误差的推荐误差模型，将期望社会损失表达为模型采纳份额的函数；在共同误差独立且预测风险相等时用Herfindahl-Hirschman指数刻画集中效应；提出协方差广义化形式以刻画模型族间依赖；推导两模型阈值判定"私人最优单一模型"何时被社会福利支配；引入凸组合配置规则约束平均私人预测遗憾。以五个摩洛哥谷类系统、九个模型族进行基准校准。

Result: 在示意性参数与10%遗憾上限下，受精度约束的多样化在全部五个系统中降低模型化社会损失10.89%–41.53%（均值19.41%）；等权配置在该设定下亦降低损失，但在每种作物上均突破遗憾上限；相关性敏感性分析表明，模型误差高度依赖会削弱多样化收益。

Conclusion: 这些反事实结果并非农艺或货币福利的估计值；该框架的核心贡献在于将采纳架构（adoption architecture）与误差依赖识别为生态系统层面的评估变量，为跨决策者的机器学习部署评估提供结构性工具。

Abstract: Machine learning recommendations are often evaluated one model at a time, despite deployment across interconnected decision makers. For continuous NPK fertilizer prescriptions, correlated errors can aggregate across farms and generate systemic costs. A model of shared and farm-specific recommendation errors links expected social loss to model adoption shares; the Herfindahl-Hirschman Index characterizes concentration effects when common model errors are independent and predictive risks are equal. A covariance-generalized formulation captures the dependence between model families. A two-model threshold characterizes when privately optimal monoculture is socially dominated, and a convex portfolio rule limits average private predictive regret. Nine model families across five Moroccan cereal systems provide a benchmark-based calibration. Under illustrative parameters and a 10% regret cap, accuracy-constrained diversification lowers modeled social loss in all five systems by 10.89-41.53% (mean 19.41%). Uniform allocation reduces modeled loss in this setting, but breaches the regret cap in every crop. Correlation sensitivity shows that highly dependent model errors attenuate the benefits of diversification. These counterfactual outcomes are not estimates of agronomic or monetary welfare; the framework identifies adoption architecture and error dependence as ecosystem-level evaluation variables.

</details>


### [177] [Mechanistic Failure Modes of Weight Pruning in Transformer Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37126)
*Nabeelah Faumi,Deshan Sumanathilaka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过因子化剪枝实验表明，困惑度与准确率等输出指标会掩盖剪枝对模型内部机制（IOI电路、Greater-Than电路）的因果损伤，提出以电路级因果分析作为压缩模型评估的补充手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有剪枝语言模型几乎只以准确率和困惑度评判，隐含假设"输出行为保持即内部计算保持"。作者要检验这一假设是否成立，即输出指标与结构完整性检查是否足以反映机制层面的损伤。

Method: 在GPT-2上做因子化剪枝实验：2种重要性准则（幅度、随机）×2种组件类型（注意力头、MLP神经元）×3种稀疏度（3%、50%、70%）。以已充分刻画的间接宾语识别（IOI）电路作为真值，用激活修补（activation patching）、路径修补（path patching）和直接logit归因（direct logit attribution）度量电路完整性；并复现Greater-Than电路以检验结论的架构依赖性。

Result: 发现两类性质不同的失效模式：(1) 幅度准则剪除注意力头（≥某阈值）会使电路"归零"——面向输出的注意力头被移除，模型准确率跌至接近随机；(2) 幅度准则在约30%稀疏度剪除MLP神经元会使电路"反转"——26个电路头全部完好，但Name Mover贡献符号翻转，模型系统性偏向错误名字，准确率仅17.7%，而困惑度（74.6）却显示其损伤小于随机剪头（140.1）。Greater-Than电路的复现表明关键组件类型取决于电路架构。

Conclusion: 输出指标与结构检查都可能漏检机制性损伤，因此应将电路级因果分析作为压缩语言模型评估的互补手段。

Abstract: Pruned language models are judged almost entirely by accuracy and perplexity, which assumes that preserved output behaviour implies preserved internal computation. We test this assumption with a factorial pruning study on GPT-2, crossing two importance criteria (magnitude, random), two component types (attention heads, MLP neurons) and three sparsity levels (3%, 50%, 70%). Using the fully characterised indirect object identification (IOI) circuit as ground truth, we measure circuit integrity with activation patching, path patching and direct logit attribution. We find two qualitatively distinct failure modes. Magnitude head pruning >= zeroes the circuit: output-facing heads are removed, and the model falls to near-chance. Magnitude neuron pruning at 30% inverts it: all 26 circuit heads remain intact, but Name Mover contributions flip sign, and the model systematically prefers the wrong name (17.7% accuracy). Perplexity ranks it as less damaged than random head pruning (74.6 vs 140.1). A replication of the Greater-Than circuit shows that the critical component type depends on the circuit's architecture. These results show that output metrics and structural checks can both miss mechanistic damage, motivating circuit-level causal analysis as a complementary evaluation of compressed language models.

</details>


### [178] [Your Policy Is Secretly a Harness: The Decision Geometry of Agent Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37255)
*Jian Mu,Ruibo Shen,Yao Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出"harness控制"视角：策略从harness中习得其动作偏好，部署回报可分解为harness上限减去策略失配（ceiling − mismatch）；据此解释harness间比较的混淆，并在MiniWoB++与Terminal-Bench Lite上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体通过harness（提示、工具、控制逻辑）进行后训练与部署。现有做法常用"固定模型在新harness下的表现"来评价harness，隐含假设模型在旧harness下学到的东西可迁移到新harness。作者质疑该假设：模型究竟从harness中学到了什么？换harness后什么能保留？

Method: 在KL正则化控制框架下做理论分析：证明最优策略的对数几率（log odds）可恢复harness对各动作的相对偏好强度，即策略"携带"了harness的偏好；由此将部署回报分解为harness上限（ceiling，其可提供的最好回报）与策略失配（mismatch，因自身次优决策而损失的回报）。训练只改变mismatch，更换harness则同时改变两项。实验上：MiniWoB++中用字段顺序反转构造等价最优但不同偏好的harness；并以ceiling与mismatch作为事前预测指标；Terminal-Bench Lite上以更强模型替代最优策略来估计ceiling与mismatch。

Result: MiniWoB++中，后训练使模型偏好高度绑定训练harness：仅反转观测字段顺序（最优决策完全不变），成功率从85.0%降至19.2%。在固定模型比较中被否定的新harness，只要其ceiling更高，经在其上继续训练后即优于原始智能体；且事前测得的ceiling与mismatch可预测这些增益。Terminal-Bench Lite上，用更强模型估计的ceiling−mismatch能复现准确率排序，而单看任一term都无法复现。代码已开源。

Conclusion: harness不仅塑造能力，还塑造策略的偏好结构，故"固定模型比harness"混淆了harness自身优劣与模型对该harness的适配度。应以ceiling与mismatch两项分别评估harness，并认识到换harness需重新训练才能兑现其潜力；该分解为智能体harness设计与选型提供了可量化的理论工具。

Abstract: Language model agents are post-trained and deployed through a harness, the prompts, tools, and control logic that present a task to the model and execute its outputs. Harnesses are often revised and judged by how an already trained model performs under them, which assumes that what the model learned under one harness carries over to the next. We ask what a policy learns from its harness and what survives when the harness changes. Under KL-regularized control, we show that the log odds of an optimal policy recover how strongly its harness favors each action over the others, so a trained policy carries the preferences of its harness. Deployed return then equals the ceiling of the harness, the best return it offers, minus the mismatch of the policy, the return lost to its own suboptimal decisions. Training changes only the mismatch, while changing the harness moves both terms. Comparing harnesses with a fixed model therefore mixes what each harness offers with how well that model uses it. In MiniWoB++, post-training makes a model's preferences specific to its training harness. Reversing the order of the observation fields leaves every optimal decision unchanged yet lowers a trained model's success from 85.0% to 19.2%. Conversely, every new harness that a fixed-model comparison rejects but that raises the ceiling outperforms the original agent after further training under it, and ceiling and mismatch measured beforehand predict these gains. For coding-agent harnesses on Terminal-Bench Lite, ceiling minus mismatch estimated with a stronger model as a stand-in for the optimum reproduces the accuracy ranking, while either term alone does not. Code is available at https://github.com/2020-qqtcg/HRP.

</details>


### [179] [Advancement or Acceleration? How Data Curation Improves LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36983)
*Jiaxing Wang,Zicheng Zhang,Deping Xiang,Jin Xu,Yong Li,Chao Liu,Pengzhang Liu,Qixia Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文区分数据精选(curation)的两种作用——"提升"(advancement，提高可达最优性能)与"加速"(acceleration，以更少数据达到同样性能)，指出只有当基线训练目标与目标目标之间存在可纠正的差距(train-target gap)时，精选才能带来提升；并据此提出可在昂贵精选前计算的 Δ-test 作为判别条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据精选被普遍认为能改善LLM训练，但一个基本问题尚未解决：精选究竟是扩展了模型最终可学到的东西，还是只是更快到达同一解。作者希望形式化这一区分，并给出可操作的判据。

Method: 通过解析刻画加真实LLM实验，区分 advancement 与 acceleration。在"以差距为中心"(gap-centric)的数据混合与选择视角下，将条件操作化为 Δ-test：即未精选基线在训练损失与目标损失之间可观测的差异 Δ，且该值可在昂贵的精选之前计算。

Result: 当基线训练目标与目标目标一致时，任何精选策略都不能改善最终端点(仍可能加速)。大量实验中，Δ 显著非零可预测持续增益；而 Δ≈0 时的增益随训练预算增大而趋于消失。

Conclusion: "提升"是精选任务本身的属性而非精选方法的属性；研究应把数据精选重新聚焦于任务本身，并将目标集构建(target-set construction)视为塑造精选所能达成效果的有前景方向。

Abstract: While data curation is widely credited with improving large language model training, a basic question remains unresolved: does curation expand what a model can ultimately learn, or merely reach the same solution faster? In this paper, we formalize this distinction between advancement (improving the attainable performance) and acceleration (reaching the same performance with less data). Through an analytic characterization and real-world LLM experiments, we show that advancement requires a correctable train-target gap. When the baseline training and target objectives coincide, no curation policy improves the endpoint, although curation may still accelerate training. This reframes advancement as a property of the curation task rather than the curation method. Under a common gap-centric view of data mixture and selection, we operationalize this condition as the $Δ$-test, an observable discrepancy between the uncurated baseline's training and target losses that is computable prior to expensive curation. Across extensive experiments, an appreciably nonzero $Δ$ predicts persistent gains, whereas gains near $Δ=0$ tend to diminish as the training budget grows. Beyond individual methods, our findings recenter data curation on the task itself and point to target-set construction as a promising direction for shaping what curation can achieve.

</details>


### [180] [Meta Models: Models that Model Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37001)
*Peihang Li,Ziming Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将"元建模"（预测训练run的学习行为）视为一种独立的数值模态而非LLM的文本任务：用现成表格型上下文学习器TabPFN直接在数值化训练记录上拟合，即可在多个设置下匹敌或超越微调LLM元模型，且耗时仅秒级；叠加KAN可进一步提升精度；并预注册了对535B参数Marin Hero Run的滚动预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 每次训练run不仅产出模型，也产出关于"模型如何学习"的证据。作者希望把这些累积实验转化为可预测新训练run的元模型，并统一看待终局性能预测、学习曲线预测与超参选择——它们是同一"训练响应函数"的不同查询。作者质疑当前用文本化配置微调LLM的范式（成本数十至数百GPU小时）是否必要。

Method: 将真实语言模型训练记录当作原生数值数据（而非token化配置文本）；采用现成表格型上下文学习器TabPFN进行无需权重更新的fit-and-predict；与微调LLM元模型在多个评测设置下对比；进一步用Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks（KAN）增强TabPFN；并预注册对535B参数公开训练run（Marin Hero Run）的逐点预测，与后续逐步揭示的真实结果对照。

Result: TabPFN在多个评测设置下匹配或超越微调LLM元模型；前者仅需秒级拟合与预测时间，后者需数十至数百GPU小时的任务特定微调。TabPFN+KAN在部分预测任务上进一步提升精度。对Marin Hero Run的预注册预测给出了可检验的对照结果（随训练推进逐点揭示）。

Conclusion: 元建模应被视为独立的数值模态，而非交给LLM的文本任务；轻量表格型上下文学习器是高性价比且具竞争力的元模型基线，KAN增强可带来额外收益。预注册的实战预测为元模型预测能力提供了严格的检验方式。

Abstract: Every neural-network training run produces not only a model, but also evidence about how models learn. Can these accumulated experiments enable us to predict and compare new training runs before executing them? We study meta-models: models that predict the learning behavior of other models. From this perspective, final-performance prediction, learning-curve prediction, and hyperparameter selection are different queries to the same training response function. We take a first, humble empirical step toward this goal: treating real language-model training records as native numeric data, we find that an off-the-shelf tabular in-context learner, TabPFN, can match or outperform a fine-tuned LLM meta-model in multiple evaluated settings. Whereas recent work fine-tunes LLMs on tokenized configurations at a cost of tens to hundreds of GPU-hours, TabPFN requires seconds of fit-and-predict time without task-specific weight updates. We argue that meta-modeling is better treated as its own numeric modality rather than as a text task for LLMs. Going one step further, augmenting TabPFN with Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) further improves accuracy on selected prediction tasks. Finally, we pre-registered a forecast of the Marin Hero Run, a 535B-parameter public training run, whose ground truth is revealed point by point as training progresses, putting meta-model predictions to the test against training outcomes not yet observed at the time of forecasting.

</details>


### [181] [Crop Yield Prediction Using Regression Models: A Comparative Evaluation of Model Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37022)
*Saptak Das,Arijit Sarkar,Soumik Neogi,Suborno Naskar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究在100万条样本数据集上对比了多元线性回归、随机森林、决策树、岭回归与Lasso五种回归模型的作物产量预测性能，发现线性类模型（LR/岭/Lasso）在测试集上表现最佳且高度接近（R²≈0.913），随机森林存在明显过拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的作物产量预测可为农业规划与资源分配提供支持。作者希望通过实证比较不同回归模型，确定在该数据集上具有竞争力的预测基线模型。

Method: 数据初始包含1,000,000条观测、10个变量，预处理时删除231条目标值为负的样本；类别变量采用one-hot编码，布尔变量转为二值。对比模型为多元线性回归(LR)、随机森林(RF)、决策树(DT)、岭回归和Lasso回归。采用80:20与70:30两种训练-测试划分，以R²、MSE、RMSE作为评价指标。

Result: 80:20划分下，LR测试R²约0.9130、测试RMSE约0.5008；70:30划分下，Lasso获得最高测试R²（约0.9131）和最低测试RMSE（约0.5008），与LR、岭回归几乎一致。DT在70:30下测试性能有所提升，RF训练与测试性能差距显著，提示过拟合。

Conclusion: 在所评估数据集上，线性回归类模型提供了具有竞争力的基线性能；后续需引入验证集实验以更充分地判定各模型的性能与可预测性。

Abstract: Reliable crop-yield prediction can support agricultural planning and resource allocation. This study evaluates five regression models for predicting crop yield from environmental, crop-related, and agricultural management variables. The dataset initially contained 1,000,000 observations and ten variables, with 231 observations having negative target values removed during preprocessing. Categorical variables were represented using one-hot encoding, and Boolean variables were converted into binary values. The models considered were multiple linear regression (LR), random-forest regression (RF), decision-tree regression (DT), ridge regression, and lasso regression. Their performance was evaluated using 80:20 and 70:30 training-testing splits based on the coefficient of determination (R-squared), mean squared error (MSE), and root mean squared error (RMSE). Applying an 80:20 split, LR achieved a test R-squared of approximately 0.9130 and a test RMSE of 0.5008. Applying a 70:30 split, lasso regression yielded the highest test R-squared (approximately 0.9131) and the lowest test RMSE (approximately 0.5008), with performance almost identical to LR and ridge regression. DT also showed improved test performance under the 70:30 split, whereas RF exhibited a substantial gap between training and testing performance. These results indicate that the evaluated linear regression model provides competitive baselines for this dataset. Further experimentation, including validation testing, will help determine the models' performance and predictability.

</details>


### [182] [Generative Replay for Equilibrium Propagation: Toward Edge-Friendly Continual Learning in Hopfield Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37139)
*Victor Cook,Gilles Sassatelli,Thierry Gil,Mohamed Watfa,Marina Reyboz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出能量生成式回放（EB-Gen），利用Hopfield网络内在生成动力学缓解平衡传播训练网络的灾难性遗忘，无需外部数据缓冲区或架构开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平衡传播（EqProp）是训练Hopfield网络的一种具生物合理性与硬件友好性的反向传播替代方案，但其在持续学习中的行为尚未被充分探索。标准经验回放与生成回放需外部数据缓冲或额外架构开销，作者希望从网络内部机制出发消除这一需求。

Method: 提出EB-Gen策略：利用Hopfield网络内禀的生成动力学，使单一模型同时充当分类器与回放样本生成器。通过对网络冻结副本进行弛豫生成携带旧任务知识的伪样本，仅依靠内部生成数据进行排练。

Result: 在类增量设置下于Split MNIST与Split CIFAR10上与FineTune、经验回放、生成回放、SRC及联合训练对比；EB-Gen显著提升稳定性同时保持可塑性，平均准确率分别达75%与64%。

Conclusion: 平衡传播可在保留其原有优势的前提下被改造以缓解灾难性遗忘，为无缓冲、架构轻量的持续学习提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Equilibrium Propagation (EqProp) offers a biologically grounded and hardware-aligned alternative to backpropagation for training Hopfield Networks. However, its behavior in continual learning settings remains largely unexplored. This work confronts catastrophic forgetting in EqProp-trained Hopfield Networks from the inside out, eliminating the need for external data buffers or architectural overhead that standard Experience Replay \textbf{(ER)} and Generative Replay \textbf{(Gen-Replay)} inevitably require. We introduce an Energy-Based Generative Replay strategy \textbf{(EB-Gen)} which exploits the intrinsic generative dynamics of Hopfield Networks, allowing a single model to act as both classifier and replay samples generator. Samples capturing past task knowledge are generated by relaxing a frozen copy of the network, enabling rehearsal solely through internally generated pseudo-data. We evaluate the proposed method in the Class-Incremental setting, using the split versions of the MNIST and CIFAR10 benchmarks and compare it with FineTune, Experience Replay, Generative Replay, Sleep-Like Replay Consolidation (SRC) and Joint training. Our results show that EB-Gen significantly improves stability while preserving plasticity, achieving a 75\% and 64\% average accuracy on Split MNIST and Split CIFAR10 respectively. These findings show that Equilibrium Propagation can be adapted to alleviate catastrophic forgetting without forgoing the traits which made it compelling in the first place.

</details>


### [183] [MLE-World: Predicting Machine Learning Experiment Outcomes with a Learned World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37175)
*Brian Ko,Han Wang,Hejia Geng,Li Erran Li,Sherry Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MLE-World 是基于 Qwen3.5-4B 微调的世界模型，用单次推理预测程序终端输出与置信度，使 ML 智能体可低成本进行测试时扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 研究智能体每评估一个候选程序都需真实训练与评估 ML 模型，代价高昂，限制了每步可探索的候选数量与测试时扩展（TTS）的可行性。

Method: 从 Qwen3.5-4B 微调世界模型，输入代码与数据描述，输出预测的终端输出（报错栈、训练日志、验证分数）及置信度；基于该模型设计五种 TTS 策略：顺序修订、Best-of-N、成对评判、融合、以及引入置信度探索奖励的 UCB 式规则；置信度低时才真实执行候选。

Result: 在 17 个 MLE-bench Lite 任务上，对留出程序的终端输出预测一致率 77.9%，显著高于更大提示模型的 41.1%；引导式 TTS 相比无世界模型的智能体观测平均性能最高提升 22.1%，且实际执行更少。

Conclusion: 以推理替代执行的世界模型可有效支撑 ML 智能体的低成本测试时扩展，并通过置信度门控在预测不可靠时回退到真实执行，兼顾性能与算力开销。

Abstract: AI research agents can propose, execute, and improve programs that solve machine learning tasks. However, executing each candidate program requires training and evaluating ML models, limiting how many candidates an agent can explore at each step. We introduce MLE-World, a world model finetuned from Qwen3.5-4B that predicts a program's terminal output (error traces, training logs, and validation scores) from its code and data description without executing it, together with a confidence score estimating the prediction's correctness. Because a prediction costs one inference call rather than a training run, MLE-World makes test time scaling (TTS) affordable for these agents. We build five TTS policies, guided by MLE-World, that compare several candidates per step by their predicted outputs: Sequential revision, Best-of-N, Pairwise judging, Fusion, and a UCB style rule that also adds an exploration bonus from this confidence score. Since predictions can be wrong, we run a candidate for real only when its confidence score is low. On 17 MLE-bench Lite tasks, MLE-World matches the real terminal output on 77.9% of held out programs, versus 41.1% for much larger prompted models. MLE-World guided TTS achieves up to 22.1% higher observed mean performance than the agent without a world model, with less real execution. Project webpage: https://mle-world.github.io/.

</details>


### [184] [TRIDENT-Seq: Profile-Matched Prefix-Future Contrast for Label-Efficient Payment Risk Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37365)
*Weiyu Han,Haoyu Jiang,Jie Sheng,Ruobing Zuo,Huilin He,Ze Zhao,Xiao Tan,Nairong Zhang,Shuai Chen,Tianyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出交易基础模型 TRIDENT-Seq，用 Prefix-Future Contrast（PFC）预训练，解决交易日志中粗粒度活跃度差异导致对比学习过易的问题，提升欺诈与拒付风险识别，并在 Ant International 生产环境显著优于 XGBoost 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欺诈与拒付标签稀缺、延迟且各业务独立，难以支撑单业务建模；而跨业务共享编码器需预训练真正捕捉区分风险的行为。但交易量、金额、时间等粗粒度活跃度差异会让对比预训练任务过于简单——不需建模支付资源使用方式即可区分正负样本，从而学不到风险信号。

Method: TRIDENT-Seq 交易基础模型，核心为 Prefix-Future Contrast（PFC）：将每个前缀与其后续行为的摘要匹配，且候选仅在活跃度画像相似的样本中采样；同时对编码器输出施加活跃度预测任务与分散度正则（dispersion regularization）。

Result: 在三个生产支付业务、两个干预预算（含 0.5%）共六种设置下，预训练编码器相对同架构从零训练在 AP 与召回上全面提升；固定验证集下，仅用 10%（Wallet）、25%（Shopping、SME）训练标签即超过全标签从零训练的平均 AP 与召回；跨业务预训练亦有增益；将画像匹配候选替换为随机候选，召回由 24.62% 降至 5.64%；线上部署中，同干预预算下金额口径拒付率与整体损失率分别比原 XGBoost 系统低 37.7% 和 37.9%。

Conclusion: 通过消除粗粒度活跃度捷径，PFC 预训练可获得可迁移、标签高效的风险表征，支持跨业务复用与冷启动，并在实际部署中带来显著的拒付与损失下降。

Abstract: Ant International accumulates large volumes of transaction history across users and payment businesses. Confirmed fraud and chargeback outcomes, by contrast, are scarce, delayed, and specific to each business. A single encoder pretrained on unlabeled histories could serve all businesses, but only if pretraining captures the behavior that separates risk. Coarse activity differences can make contrastive pretraining on transaction logs too easy: transaction volume, amount, and timing can distinguish a history's continuation from arbitrary candidates without modeling how payment resources are used. We present TRIDENT-Seq, a transaction foundation model trained with Prefix-Future Contrast (PFC). PFC matches each prefix to a summary of its subsequent behavior among candidates with similar activity profiles; activity prediction and dispersion regularization act on the encoder output. Across three production payment businesses, the pretrained encoder improves average precision (AP) and recall at a 0.5% intervention budget over architecture-matched scratch training in all six business-budget settings. With a fixed validation set, it exceeds full-label scratch training in mean AP and recall using 10% of the training labels on Wallet and 25% on Shopping and SME. Pretraining only on another business's histories still helps. Replacing profile-matched candidates with random ones cuts recall from 24.62% to 5.64%. In Ant International's deployment evaluation, recorded amount-based chargeback and overall loss rates are 37.7% and 37.9% lower than those of the previous XGBoost-based system under the same intervention budget.

</details>


### [185] [Euclidean Crowding-Driven Adaptive Euclidean-Hyperbolic Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37373)
*Hao Chen,Jian Sun,Chuan Shi,Hongying Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文从几何与泛化理论角度解释了双曲空间在层级数据上的优势，并提出自适应欧氏-双曲融合框架EH-Fusion。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双曲图表示学习在层级数据上经验表现优异，但其理论机制（为何优于欧氏空间）理解不足；欧氏空间中"欧氏拥挤"缺乏定量刻画。

Method: (1) 通过分析节点嵌入的逐层最小间隔（layer-wise minimum separation），对欧氏拥挤作几何定量，证明固定维欧氏嵌入树状图必然出现逐层拥挤，从而得到更弱的基于margin的泛化界；(2) 推导双曲几何在层级数据上优越性的泛化界；(3) 提出EH-Fusion：用指数映射将欧氏GNN表示提升到双曲空间，并依据节点级邻域扩张模式自适应融合两种几何。

Result: 理论证明欧氏嵌入的逐层拥挤导致更弱的margin泛化界，而双曲几何可获得更优的泛化界；大量实验验证了理论洞见并显示EH-Fusion在基准数据集上的有效性。

Conclusion: 工作为双曲嵌入的优势提供了泛化理论解释，并以几何自适应融合框架将理论转化为可实践的模型，弥合了双曲图学习的理论与实践鸿沟。

Abstract: Hyperbolic graph representation learning has emerged as a powerful paradigm for modeling hierarchical data. Unlike Euclidean space, which exhibits polynomial volume growth, hyperbolic space grows exponentially with radius, naturally accommodating tree-like structures without the geometric distortion known as Euclidean crowding. Despite strong empirical performance, the theoretical mechanisms underlying hyperbolic embeddings remain poorly understood. In this work, we provide a geometric quantifcation research for Euclidean crowding, a generalization-theoretic explanation for the superiority of hyperbolic space on hierarchical data, and further develop an adaptive Euclidean-hyperbolic fusion learning framework toward bridging this gap. Specifically, we \textbf{first} establish a geometric quantification of Euclidean crowding by analyzing layer-wise minimum separation in node embeddings, proving that fixed-dimensional Euclidean embeddings of tree-like graphs inevitably exhibit layer-wise crowding that leads to weaker margin-based generalization bounds. \textbf{Second}, we provide a generalization-bound for the superiority of hyperbolic geometry on hierarchical data. \textbf{Third}, we propose EH-Fusion, a geometry-adaptive framework that lifts Euclidean GNN representations into hyperbolic space via exponential mapping and adaptively fuses both geometries based on node-level neighborhood expansion patterns. Extensive experiments validate our theoretical insights and demonstrate the effectiveness of EH-Fusion on benchmark datasets.

</details>


### [186] [Displacement Moments and Path-Terminal Separation in Brownian Interpolant Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37413)
*Zhong Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对布朗随机插值（Brownian stochastic interpolants）的Euler采样误差，在端点连一阶矩都不存在的一般Borel耦合下给出目标到输出的KL保证，揭示误差取决于位移矩并按路径分解刻画马尔可夫投影丢弃的源信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 布朗随机插值的采样误差常被假设依赖位移矩，但即便两个端点都无一阶矩，仍需严格的误差刻画；现有分析对任意耦合、无矩条件下的KL保证缺失，且不清楚桥路径、比较路径与终端误差之间的关系，因此需要给出统一的理论框架。

Method: 对任意Borel耦合，从真实源出发分析标准Euler采样，证明提前停止的目标到输出KL界：四阶位移矩给显式O(N^{-1})界，二阶矩足以在漂移误差消失时保证固定律收敛。对某一不存在高于二阶位移矩的固定耦合，分别刻画原始桥路径KL、比较路径KL与终端KL；通过路径分解识别马尔可夫投影所丢弃的源信息。当原始桥为马尔可夫时，改用当前状态Euler采样得到二阶矩O((M_2+d)/N)界，并纳入概率布朗参考下的有限熵Schrödinger桥。另构造基于iid端点对的当前状态学习器，证明其期望输出KL一致性。

Result: 四阶位移矩下得到显式O(N^{-1})误差界；二阶矩下实现固定律收敛。无高于二阶矩的固定耦合中，原始桥路径KL保持O(1)，比较路径KL为O(1/log N)，同一网格上的终端KL至多C/N^2。马尔可夫桥情形下当前状态Euler采样具有O((M_2+d)/N)界，并覆盖有限熵Schrödinger桥。数值诊断展示了各类误差的差异；条件扩散恒等式给出复原接口，而图像对照实验未发现相对廉价概率预测器的迭代优势。

Conclusion: 误差主要由位移矩控制，路径分解清晰区分了不同路径与终端的收敛率，马尔可夫结构可显著改善当前状态采样的界。一般二阶矩耦合下的一致终端速率仍为开放问题。

Abstract: Brownian stochastic interpolants connect paired endpoint samples, yet their sampling error can depend on displacement moments even when neither endpoint has a first moment. For arbitrary Borel couplings, we prove early-stopped, target-to-output KL guarantees for standard Euler sampling from the true source. A fourth displacement moment gives an explicit $O(N^{-1})$ bound; a second moment suffices for fixed-law convergence as drift error vanishes. For one fixed coupling with no displacement moment above two, original bridge-path KL stays of order one, comparison-path KL is of order $1/\log N$, and terminal KL is at most $C/N^2$ on the same grid. The path decomposition identifies the information about the source discarded by Markov projection. When the original bridge is Markov, current-state Euler sampling instead has a second-moment $O((M_2+d)/N)$ bound; finite-entropy Schroedinger bridges for the probability Brownian reference are included. We also construct a current-state learner with expected output KL consistency from iid endpoint pairs. Numerical diagnostics illustrate the distinct errors. Conditional diffusion identities specify a restoration interface, while image controls find no iterative advantage over cheap probabilistic predictors. Uniform terminal rates for general second-moment couplings remain open.

</details>


### [187] [Bayes-Adaptive MDPs for Rollout Allocation to Enhance GRPO Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37429)
*Faeze Moradi Kalarde,Armin Toroghi,Nam Ho Koh,Doo Hwan Hwang,Seungjin Kim,Kee-Eung Kim,Scott Sanner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 AdaRoll-GRPO，将 GRPO 的 rollout 预算分配建模为难度感知的顺序决策问题，用贝叶斯自适应 MDP 学习离线分配策略，在固定总预算下提升训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO 对每道训练题分配统一的 rollout 预算，忽略题目难度差异：高难题 rollout 过少、难以产生有信息的正奖励信号而贡献受限，中等难度题则常被过度分配。

Method: 将难度感知的 rollout 预算分配形式化为在总预算约束下最大化训练效用的优化问题；把分配过程重构为顺序决策问题，采用 Bayes-adaptive MDP 建模并学习自适应分配策略；策略离线求解，训练时直接应用，不引入额外在线优化开销。

Result: 在多个推理数据集与多种 LLM 架构上的广泛实验表明，在固定 rollout 总预算下，AdaRoll-GRPO 稳定优于标准 GRPO 的训练表现。

Conclusion: 自适应（难度感知）rollout 预算分配对 GRPO 及 LLM 后训练有效，可在不增加在线开销的前提下提升固定预算下的训练效率与效果。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has demonstrated strong performance in LLM post-training. However, it allocates a uniform rollout budget to every training question, ignoring variations in question difficulty. As a result, highly difficult questions may receive too few rollouts to generate informative positive reward signals, limiting their contribution to training, while moderately difficult questions often receive more rollouts than necessary. In this work, we formulate the problem of difficulty-aware rollout budget allocation to maximize training utility under the same total rollout budget as standard GRPO. We propose AdaRoll-GRPO, which reformulates rollout allocation as a sequential decision-making problem and leverages a Bayes-adaptive Markov decision process (MDP) to learn an adaptive allocation policy. The learned policy is computed offline and applied during GRPO training without introducing additional online optimization overhead. Extensive experiments on multiple reasoning datasets and LLM architectures demonstrate that AdaRoll-GRPO consistently improves GRPO training performance under a fixed rollout budget, highlighting the effectiveness of adaptive rollout allocation for GRPO in LLM post-training.

</details>


### [188] [CRISP: Co-designing Pruning and Scheduling for Efficient MoE Inference on Edge Servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37442)
*Congwei Zhang,Youming Tao,Yifei Zou,Yu Yang,Ruirui Zhang,Xiaoyu Zhang,Falko Dressler,Xiuzhen Cheng,Dongxiao Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CRISP：面向内存受限边缘平台的MoE推理组合感知框架，协同设计剪枝与运行时调度，将吞吐提升至2.1×。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘平台MoE推理瓶颈在于专家权重的搬移而非计算；每token仅激活少量专家，但路由输入相关，需保证全部专家可访问，故权重移动主导延迟。现有均匀剪枝与专家丢弃忽略了两个特性：专家组合出现频率高度偏斜，且组合内各专家贡献不对称。

Method: 1）按重要性重排神经元，使同一份存储的专家可支持多种执行宽度；2）依据组合频率与专家角色，将组合划分为hot/warm/cold并分配差异化执行策略；3）利用异构宽度实现专家加载与计算的重叠。

Result: 在Mixtral-8x7B与DeepSeek-MoE-16B上，相同内存预算下吞吐最高提升2.1×，相对全宽推理精度损失控制在3.6%。

Conclusion: 高效的边缘MoE服务需联合优化"保留哪些专家容量"与"如何调度专家执行"两方面。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) inference on memory-constrained edge platforms is often dominated by expert-weight movement rather than computation. Although each token activates only a small subset of experts, routing is input-dependent, so all experts must remain accessible and memory movement dominates latency. Existing approaches such as uniform pruning and expert dropping overlook two properties of MoE execution: expert combinations are highly skewed in frequency, and experts contribute asymmetrically within each combination. We present CRISP, a combination-aware framework that co-designs pruning and runtime scheduling for efficient MoE serving on memory-constrained edge platforms. CRISP reorders neurons by importance so one stored expert can support multiple execution widths, assigns differentiated execution to hot, warm, and cold combinations based on frequency and expert role, and exploits heterogeneous widths to overlap expert loading with computation. Across Mixtral-8x7B and DeepSeek-MoE-16B, CRISP improves throughput by up to $2.1\times$ at the same memory budget while limiting accuracy loss to 3.6\% relative to full-width inference. Our results highlight that efficient edge MoE serving requires jointly optimizing which expert capacity is preserved and how expert execution is scheduled.

</details>


### [189] [An Input Skip Can Reparameterize a VAE Without Improving the Generator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37456)
*Kevin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 输入跳跃(skip)可大幅降低VAE的重构误差，却保持新样本分布不变；因此重构误差不能作为生成质量的判据，评估生成必须超越重构指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清"输入跳跃降低重构误差"这一现象的本质：它是模型分布的真实改进，还是仅参数化坐标变化带来的度量假象，从而检验重构MSE作为模型选择/生成评估指标的可靠性。

Method: 对常数输入跳跃 0≤α<1 的高斯VAE，将无跳跃模型改写为跳跃形式（解码输出乘 b=1-α、噪声方差乘 b²），证明观测与隐变量的联合分布及精确生成分数不变而MSE乘 b²；并在已知密度的高斯实验与七个年度DART海啸记录上，用能量分数与重要性采样负对数密度估计进行比较；另考察固定编解码器下由训练重构误差估计观测方差的后果，并与PPCA对照。

Result: 联合分布不变但MSE按 b² 缩小；独立训练因参数坐标不同可得到不同生成器。高斯实验中同时调整跳跃与观测方差可降MSE，却使能量分数和重要性采样负对数密度估计变差。DART上同观测方差下能量分数差异在四个原始记录中正负兼有，在2018年三个记录上偏向跳跃。PPCA的平均能量分数低于所有神经DART拟合；固定编解码器时用重构误差估计观测方差同样会恶化能量分数。

Conclusion: 重构误差的降低可能仅是参数化重标定，而非生成能力提升；跳跃与噪声方差的耦合使重构误差与能量分数等评分脱钩，重构误差不足以评判生成模型，需采用能量分数等分布层面的评估。

Abstract: An input skip can reduce reconstruction error while leaving a model's distribution of new samples unchanged. For a Gaussian variational autoencoder (VAE) with constant input skip $0\leqα<1$, a model without a skip can be written in skip form by multiplying its decoder output by $b=1-α$ and its noise variance by $b^2$. The distribution of observations and latent variables and its exact generative scores stay the same, while reconstruction mean squared error (MSE) is multiplied by $b^2$. Separate training can produce different generators because optimization depends on the parameter coordinates. In a Gaussian experiment with known density, changing skip and observation variance together reduces MSE but worsens energy score and the estimate of negative log density from importance sampling. On seven annual Deep-ocean Assessment and Reporting of Tsunamis (DART) records, energy score differences at the same observation variance have both signs on four original records and favor the skip on three records from 2018. Probabilistic principal component analysis (PPCA) has lower mean energy scores than every neural DART fit. With the encoder and decoder kept the same, estimating observation variance from training reconstruction error can also worsen energy score. Assessing generation requires more than reconstruction error.

</details>


### [190] [The Structure Gap: When More Data Cannot Replace Structured Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37523)
*Kheer Sagar Patel,Mojtaba Soltanalian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"结构编码问题"（输入-输出映射由求解器、对称群、图、物理定律、算子、文法、搜索空间或优化目标等已知结构决定或强约束），界定"结构鸿沟"——接收结构与仅接收样本的方法在有限资源下的差距；主张而非绝对：结构无关学习随覆盖增长可逼近结构求解器，但任何有限数据集都留有结构天然可消解的残余不确定性。在已知算子高斯逆问题上精确证明：Wiener 求解器达 Bayes 风险，稠密经验学习器存在持续的超额期望风险；并给出单元格覆盖下界与不可观测轨道不可辨识性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反驳"足够大的模型+足够多的数据可使显式领域知识可选"这一流行信念，指出结构编码任务的核心困难不是表达力，而是有限数据下的辨识、外推与认证。

Method: 定义结构编码问题与结构鸿沟；对已知算子的高斯逆问题作精确理论分析（对比 Bayes 最优的 Wiener 求解器与同线性类中的稠密经验学习器）；证明单元格覆盖下界与不可观测轨道不可辨识性；在已知算子反演、稀疏恢复、图可达性（含规模外推）上开展受控实验验证机制。

Result: 理论上，已知算子 Wiener 求解器达到 Bayes 风险，而正确线性类中的稠密经验学习器存在持续期望超额风险；单元格覆盖下界与不可观测轨道不可辨识性成立；三类受控实验复现相应机制。

Conclusion: 结构并非可被数据规模替代；经验证的结构可在学习开始前约束假设空间，使学习仅处理结构未指定的部分。

Abstract: A persistent belief in modern AI is that sufficiently large learners trained on sufficiently much data can make explicit domain knowledge optional. This paper examines a limitation of that belief. We define structure-encoded problems: tasks whose input-output map is determined, or sharply constrained, by known structure such as a solver, symmetry group, graph, physical law, measurement operator, grammar, combinatorial search space, or optimization objective. For such tasks, the central question is not expressivity--a dense neural network may contain the desired map--but finite-data identification, extrapolation, and certification. We introduce the structure gap: the finite-resource gap between a method that receives the governing structure and a learner that receives only examples. The strongest useful form of the claim is asymptotic rather than absolute: structure-agnostic learning may approach a structured solver as coverage grows, but every finite dataset can leave residual uncertainty that the structure resolves by construction. We prove this exactly for a Gaussian inverse problem: the known-operator Wiener solver attains Bayes risk, while the dense empirical learner in the correct linear class has persistent expected excess risk. We also prove a cell-coverage lower bound and an unobserved-orbit non-identifiability result. Controlled experiments in known-operator inversion, sparse recovery, and graph reachability with size extrapolation instantiate the mechanisms. When validated structure is available, it can constrain the hypothesis space before learning begins, leaving learning to address components the structure does not specify.

</details>


### [191] [Collective Dynamics of Self-Reinforcement: How Cross-Task Coupling Drives Policy Collapse in Unsupervised RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37553)
*Peize Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将无标签RLVR（基于自一致性多数投票奖励）的推理坍缩解释为一种"集体不稳定性"：在共享参数的多问题模型中，问题间共享的扰动模式以与问题数N无关的速率放大，而逐问题锐化被1/N减慢，导致存在临界问题数N_c≈ln(1/δ)/(λ²G)，超出则奖励升而准确率崩、最终对所有问题输出同一答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无标签RLVR用模型自身多数投票作为可验证奖励，初期提升推理能力，但最终出现奖励趋于1而准确率下降的坍缩，且坍缩仅在训练问题数较多时出现。作者希望解释这一"数据集规模依赖"现象，并给出可预测的临界条件与稳定化机制。

Method: 构建一个可解的多问题模型：各问题共享参数、各自偏好不同答案。分析单问题自强化漂移F及其种群平均斜率⟨F'⟩；刻画逐问题锐化（速率受1/N抑制）与共享模式的竞争（增长率∝λ²，与N无关，λ为共享耦合强度）。推导临界问题数N_c，并进行数值模拟验证；进一步给出核版本理论，识别保护性耦合（锐化的推广）与边际熵奖励对均匀集体模式的稳定作用；最后在小Transformer上做无标签RL实验，通过移除/恢复共享方向验证机制。

Result: 模拟证实理论预测：存在尖锐相变；N_c∝λ^{-2}（含对数修正）；跨耦合、置信度分布与训练变体存在单一临界放大因子λ²NG；对批量大小呈对数依赖；初始高置信模型不坍缩。核理论给出保护性耦合与边际熵奖励可稳定均匀集体模式。Transformer实验中，移除共享方向完全阻止坍缩，恢复则导致坍缩，且坍缩随训练集规模与基座模型熵增大而加剧。

Conclusion: 无标签RLVR的坍缩是共享参数下多问题集体不稳定性的必然结果，其发生由N、λ、初始置信度与熵等共同决定，并可用临界标度N_c定量预测；抑制共享（坍缩）方向或引入边际熵奖励是有效的稳定化手段。

Abstract: Label-free reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) rewards a language model for agreeing with its own majority vote. It improves reasoning at first but eventually collapses: the self-assessed reward goes to one while accuracy falls, often until the model gives the same answer to every problem. The collapse appears only when training uses many problems. We explain this dataset-size dependence as a collective instability. In a solvable many-problem model in which problems share parameters, a population whose problems prefer different answers amplifies any shared perturbation for as long as the population-averaged slope $\langle F'\rangle$ of the single-problem self-reinforcement drift $F$ is positive. This holds while problems are still uncertain about their answers. Per-problem sharpening, slowed by $1/N$ for $N$ training problems, races against the shared mode, which grows at a rate $\proptoλ^2$ independent of $N$, where $λ$ is the strength of the shared coupling. This yields a critical number of problems $N_c\simeq\ln(1/δ)/(λ^2G)$, where $δ$ seeds the shared mode and $G$ decreases with the initial confidence. Simulations of the model confirm the predictions: a sharp transition, $N_c\proptoλ^{-2}$ up to a logarithmic correction, a single critical amplification $λ^2NG$ across couplings, confidence profiles and training variants, a logarithmic dependence on batch size, and no collapse for confident initial models. A kernel version of the theory identifies a protective coupling (generalization of sharpening) and a marginal-entropy bonus that stabilizes the uniform collective mode. In label-free RL of a small Transformer, removing the shared direction prevents collapse entirely, restoring it causes collapse, and collapse grows with training-set size and base-model entropy.

</details>


### [192] [Pneuma: A Large-Scale Real-World Time Series Benchmark for Commercial Aircraft Predictive Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37563)
*Jiafeng Lin,Yutian Zhang,Huan Zhang,Yuxuan Wang,Jinyan Feng,Yuhui Liu,Zhongyi Pei,Jianmin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Pneuma——首个基于在役商用飞机机载时间序列的公开大规模预测性维护基准，覆盖引气系统、4种机型、9类经维护日志核实的故障场景，含9.2亿+多变量时间步，并围绕预测、故障分类、异常检测三类任务统一评测25+模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商用航空预测性维护缺乏公开大规模真实数据集，现有方法多基于专有数据开发评估，导致可复现性差、难以公平比较。

Method: 构建面向引气系统的机载多变量时间序列基准：4种机型、9个经维护日志核实的故障场景、逾9.2亿时间步、9个精选子集；标签源自真实维护记录；设置多变量预测、有监督故障分类、无监督异常检测三类任务，在统一设置下评测来自四个方法族的25+模型。

Result: 数据呈现显著类别不平衡、故障演化信号微弱、机型配置差异大等真实挑战；即便是先进模型也难以识别细微故障特征；增加上下文示例与多变量推理可带来稳定增益，且预测质量与异常检测性能强相关。

Conclusion: Pneuma 为面向商用航空实际部署的数据驱动预测性维护提供严谨、可复现的评测基础，并给出复杂真实航空时间序列建模的实践性洞见。

Abstract: Predictive maintenance is vital for commercial aviation, yet progress remains hindered by the lack of public, large-scale real-world datasets. As a result, most methods are developed and evaluated on proprietary data, limiting reproducibility. We introduce Pneuma, the first publicly available large-scale benchmark for predictive maintenance using onboard time series from in-service commercial aircraft. Built around bleed air systems, Pneuma contains over 920 million multivariate time steps across four aircraft types and 9 verified fault scenarios, directly aligned with real maintenance logs. The dataset exhibits key real-world challenges, including substantial class imbalance, subtle fault evolution patterns, and significant variance across aircraft configurations. We structure the benchmark around three tasks: multivariate forecasting, supervised fault classification, and unsupervised anomaly detection, with labels derived from actual maintenance records. Under unified settings, we evaluate over 25 models from four methodological families on 9 carefully curated subsets. The results reveal that even strong contemporary models struggle with subtle fault signatures. Further analyses show consistent gains from additional in-context examples and multivariate inference, while forecasting quality is strongly associated with anomaly detection performance. Together, these findings provide practical insights into effectively modeling complex real-world aviation time series. We hope Pneuma will serve as a rigorous and reproducible foundation for advancing data-driven predictive maintenance toward real-world deployment in commercial aviation.

</details>


### [193] [LCM-Promo: An LLM-Based Uplift Model for Promotion Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37570)
*Nesta Midavaine,Floris Fok,Paulo Mendes,Joao Silva,Önder Akaçık,Dimas Lima,Zülküf Genç*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LCM-Promo——基于LLM的促销分配uplift模型，通过在交易数据上微调，将单token输出解释为转化阈值概率以学习单调促销响应曲线，并在生产A/B测试中使转化率提升1%、促销成本降低6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统uplift模型难以同时利用大规模交易数据与上下文信息来刻画用户意图和促销响应；作者希望借助微调LLM（Large Commerce Model）统一建模用户意图与促销响应，从而在促销分配中兼顾转化提升与成本控制。

Method: LCM-Promo是一个任务特定的大商业模型（LCM），在交易型电商数据上微调。它将LLM的单token输出解释为转化阈值概率，从而学习单调的促销-响应曲线，并以会话上下文（如日期、配送班次）为条件进行建模。

Result: 在流失用户召回的线上A/B测试中，相对Segment-EMA生产基线，LCM-Promo使转化率提升1%，促销成本降低6%。

Conclusion: 将微调LLM与阈值概率化输出结合，可有效实现单调促销响应建模，并在真实生产环境中同时提升转化、降低促销成本，为基于LLM的uplift建模提供了可行范式。

Abstract: We present LCM-Promo, an LLM-based uplift model for promotion allocation. LCM-Promo is a task-specific Large Commerce Model (LCM): a finetuned LLM trained on transactional commerce data to predict user intent and promotion response. It learns monotonic promotion-response curves from production logs by interpreting single-token outputs as conversion-threshold probabilities, while conditioning on session context such as day and delivery shift. In a production A/B test on churn reactivation, LCM-Promo increased conversion by 1% and reduced promotion cost by 6% relative to the Segment-EMA production baseline.

</details>


### [194] [GAS-PFN: Graph-Aware Spatiotemporal Prior-Data Fitted Networks for Zero-Shot Traffic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37598)
*Joseph Natter,Long Vu,Yifan Zhang,Bobin Deng,Kun Suo,Rui Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GAS-PFN 提出图感知的时空先验数据拟合网络，通过随机游走扩散在合成任务先验中注入路网拓扑，并设计双掩码条件 Transformer 推断架构，实现无需参数更新的零样本交通预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督式图神经网络交通预测虽性能强，但针对特定数据集与传感器图训练，部署到新网络或演化网络成本高；现有 PFN 类零样本预测方法未显式建模交通动态所依赖的图结构依赖关系。

Method: 将采样得到的道路拓扑通过随机游走扩散纳入合成任务先验，使模型在预训练中学习图耦合动态；推断架构在共享 Transformer 主干上进行两次掩码条件化前向，分别建模单传感器时序动态与邻域传感器间时空交互，再融合表征进行预测。

Result: 在六个基准、90 分钟预测跨度下，相较最接近的 PFN 前作 TimePFN 平均降低 MSE 34.0%；相较四个近期工业界通用零样本预测器平均降低 MSE 27.7%（所有模型均获得 90 分钟连续近期历史，额外上下文取自各数据集按时间划分的训练集）。

Conclusion: 将 PFN 先验与推断架构同图结构对齐，可显著提升零样本交通预测的上下文学习能力，并支持直接利用目标网络结构与上下文而无需参数更新。

Abstract: Traffic forecasting requires modeling complex temporal dynamics and the spatial dependencies imposed by road topology. Supervised graph-based models achieve strong performance, but are often trained for a particular dataset and sensor graph, making deployment to new or evolving networks costly. Prior-Data Fitted Networks (PFNs) offer a zero-shot alternative by learning in-context inference from synthetic task priors, which provide an explicit mechanism for encoding domain-specific assumptions. Existing forecasting PFNs, however, do not explicitly represent the graph-structured dependencies governing traffic dynamics. To combine graph-aware traffic modeling with zero-shot adaptation, we introduce the Graph-Aware Spatiotemporal Prior-Data Fitted Network (GAS-PFN). GAS-PFN incorporates sampled road topology into its synthetic task prior through random-walk diffusion, teaching the model to learn graph-coupled dynamics during pretraining. Its co-designed inference architecture uses two mask-conditioned passes through a shared Transformer backbone to separately model sensor-specific temporal dynamics and spatiotemporal interactions among neighboring sensors before integrating their representations for prediction. This design enables GAS-PFN to use target-network structure and context without parameter updates. Across six benchmarks at the 90-minute horizon, GAS-PFN reduces MSE by 34.0% on average relative to TimePFN, its closest PFN-based predecessor. It also reduces MSE by 27.7% on average relative to four recent industry-developed general-purpose zero-shot forecasters. In this comparison, all models receive 90 minutes of contiguous recent history, and any additional context is drawn from each dataset's chronological training split. These results indicate that aligning PFN priors and inference architectures with graph structure improves in-context learning for zero-shot traffic forecasting.

</details>


### [195] [Scaling Laws for Tool-Use Agents via Controllable Environment Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37648)
*Andrew Szot,Samira Abnar,Omar Attia,Abbas Kazerouni,Michael Kirchhof,Cheng-Yu Hsieh,Raviteja Vemulapalli,Alexander Toshev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SynthAPI 合成数据生成管线，通过显式控制工具、任务与数据库三维度的多样性，在 45.9 万任务、849 个模型上推导出预测工具使用智能体泛化的缩放定律，并据此指导数据组成，性能超越前人方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建能完成复杂多步任务的高性能 LLM 工具使用智能体是关键目标，但合成工具使用环境中"数据组成"（而非仅数据量）如何影响训练效果尚不清楚，缺乏可预测的指导原则。

Method: 提出 SynthAPI 管线，在工具、任务、数据库三个维度上显式控制合成环境多样性；系统性变化生成控制参数，覆盖 458,752 个任务、849 个模型（四种规模），拟合出基于合成环境规模与组成的下游泛化缩放定律。

Result: 得到可预测的缩放关系：数据预算增大时，扩展工具与数据库多样性比单纯增加任务数量更有效；提高数据复杂度带来更大收益。按此规律扩展的智能体在四个评测基准上均优于基于前人数据训练的模型。

Conclusion: 数据组成（多维多样性）是合成工具使用数据扩展的核心杠杆，缩放定律可为智能体数据构建提供可预测的定量指导。

Abstract: We explore how to build performant large language model (LLM) tool-use agents that complete complex, multi-step tasks. Specifically, we study how data composition in synthetically generated tool-use environments affects the training of such agents. To do so, we propose the SynthAPI pipeline, which explicitly controls the diversity of generated environments along the axes of tools, tasks, and the databases on which the tools operate. By systematically varying SynthAPI's generation controls in 458,752 tasks across 849 trained models of four different sizes, we derive a scaling law that predicts downstream tool-use agent generalization based on the scale and composition of the synthesized environments. These predictable relationships reveal several lessons for scaling tool-use agent data. As data budgets grow, scaling tool and database diversity becomes more effective than increasing the number of tasks, and increasing data complexity yields larger gains. Scaling according to these findings yields agents that outperform models trained on data from prior work in large-scale tool-use agent and synthetic environment generation across four evaluation benchmarks.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [196] [A State Augmented Approach to Paralle Modeling in High-Level Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36481)
*Nicholas Kovacs,Hao Zheng*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出一种基于同步顺序进程的显式架构建模方法，用C++表达持久状态、通信与反馈控制，使HLS只负责进程内部调度与绑定；以五级RV32I处理器验证，实现1.221 CPI@220 MHz，优于算法式HLS基线(4.000 CPI)与Ibex参考(1.575@242 MHz)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HLS只能调度其输入描述中已合法化的并发；对于反馈耦合设计，顺序行为模型会掩盖架构状态边界与冒险消解信息，导致难以实现高效的重叠执行。作者希望将"架构决策"与"工具调度"解耦，由设计者显式控制架构结构。

Method: 基于同步顺序进程（同步启动、完成同步后再循环）的显式架构建模方法：持久状态、通信与反馈控制在C++中显式表示，由HLS在给定结构内完成算子调度与绑定。以五级RV32I处理器为案例，讨论三项关键建模决策——消费者读取哪个版本的值、何时消费该值、架构访存动作如何映射到端口操作。

Result: Kintex UltraScale+ FPGA上：CPI优化配置达1.221 cycles/instruction @220 MHz（算法式HLS基线为4.000；Ibex参考为1.575 @242 MHz）；面积优化配置LUT减少65%、寄存器减少47%，基准周期增加9.7%，实现时钟250 MHz。

Conclusion: 结果体现了"设计者控制架构、工具控制调度"的有效分离：显式架构建模可在HLS流程中获得接近甚至优于手写RTL参考的CPI，并支持在性能与面积之间进行可预测的配置取舍。

Abstract: High-level synthesis (HLS) can schedule only the concurrency made legal by its input description. For feedback-coupled designs, a sequential behavioral model can obscure the architectural state boundaries and hazard resolution needed for efficient overlap. We develop an explicit architectural modeling methodology based on synchronous sequential processes that start in lockstep and synchronize completion before repeating. Persistent state, communication, and feedback control are represented in C++, while HLS schedules and binds operations within that structure. A five-stage RV32I processor illustrates three consequential modeling decisions: which version of a value a consumer reads, when it consumes that value, and how architectural memory actions map to port operations. On a Kintex UltraScale+ FPGA, the CPI-optimized configuration achieves 1.221 cycles per instruction at 220 MHz, compared with 4.000 for an algorithmic HLS baseline and 1.575 at 242 MHz for an Ibex reference. An area-optimized configuration reduces LUTs by 65\% and registers by 47\%, with 9.7\% more benchmark cycles and a 250 MHz achieved clock. These results demonstrate a separation between designer-controlled architecture and tool-controlled scheduling.

</details>


### [197] [p-DCIM: A 28nm Probabilistic SRAM DCIM based Ising Machine for Near-Memory Context Selection in LLM-RAG Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37033)
*Madhav Vadlamani,Rahul Singh,Yuyao Kong,Haomei Liu,Zheng Zhang,Shimeng Yu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出一款28 nm fabricated SRAM 数字存内计算（DCIM）Ising 机器宏，将 RAG 的证据选择重构为 QUBO 并在近存端求解，选择性能耗估计较 LLM 基线低6个数量级以上，单查询总能耗下降67%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG 中证据选择阶段日益依赖重复的语言模型重排序，给推理流水线带来显著额外能耗；需要以低能耗硬件替代该 LLM 选择通路。

Method: 将证据选择建模为 QUBO；在同一双模式 DCIM 宏的同一 MAC 数据通路上完成两项操作：计算用于填充 QUBO 系数的嵌入相似度分数，以及评估驱动退火的逐自旋哈密顿量差值。退火温度由数字 plane-count 控制配合 SRAM 伪读随机位翻转实现，全片内数字配置即可获得分级降温。28 nm 流片，0.16 mm²、0.41 mW；拟作为 hybrid HBM/HBF 堆叠逻辑 base-die 的组成部分，仅将选中的 passage 标识符回传主机。

Result: 宏归一化选择能耗估计比 LLM 基线低6个数量级以上；评估的单查询总能耗降低67%；在评估样例上 token 级 F1 相当。

Conclusion: 以近存 Ising 求解替代 LM 选择 pass 可在保持精度相当的前提下大幅降低 RAG 选择能耗，并借助 DCIM 双模式数据通路与全数字退火控制实现紧凑、低功耗的存内加速方案。

Abstract: We present a fabricated SRAM DCIM Ising machine that performs set-level context selection for large language model (LLM) with retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). In RAG, the evidence-selection stage is increasingly delegated to repeated language-model reranking, which adds substantial energy to the inference pipeline. We reformulate evidence selection as a quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) problem and solve it on a dual-mode digital compute-in-memory (DCIM) macro: the same multiply-and-accumulate datapath computes the embedding-similarity scores that populate the QUBO coefficients and evaluates the per-spin Hamiltonian differences that drive the anneal. The annealing temperature is set by a digital plane-count control along with the SRAM pseudo-read random bit flips, giving graded cooling entirely from on-chip digital configuration. The macro is taped-out in a 28 nm process [0.16 mm2, 0.41 mW] and is proposed to be integrated as part of the logic base-die in hybrid high-bandwidth memory (HBM) and high-bandwidth flash (HBF) stacks in a GPU package, where only the selected passage identifiers return to the host. Replacing the language-model selection pass with the near-memory Ising solver yields a macro normalized selection-energy estimate more than six orders of magnitude lower than the LLM-based baseline and reduces the evaluated total energy per query by 67%, while maintaining comparable token-level F1 on the evaluated examples.

</details>


### [198] [Kernel Performance Engineering under Physical Non-Idealities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37466)
*Supun Kuruppu,Pranav Dangi,Wei Li,Zhenyu Bai,Tulika Mitra*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 论文以H100 GPU的DSMEM为案例，揭示芯片"物理非理想性"（floorsweeping、功耗/TDP限制）如何制约kernel性能，并提出wave-aware聚类策略，使FlashAttention 4吞吐提升最高5.30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统硬件抽象隐藏了IC设计与制造中的物理非理想性，但这些限制会压低kernel性能并掩盖优化机会，需将其显式纳入性能分析与优化。

Method: 分析H100的floorsweeping如何限制cluster可用的硬件资源、抵消DSMEM降低数据搬移的收益；据此设计考虑资源约束的wave-aware聚类策略；并用global-load-and-broadcast微基准、GEMM和Hopper版FlashAttention 4评估；结合DVFS分析TDP约束下的频率效应；再在Tenstorrent Wormhole上验证功耗约束。

Result: 微基准、GEMM与FlashAttention 4实验体现了这些约束的性能影响，wave-aware聚类使FlashAttention 4吞吐相对基线最高提升5.30%；在TDP限制下，降低数据搬移通过DVFS带来更高SM频率，解释了原本异常的吞吐提升；Wormhole上同样观测到功耗限制吞吐扩展。

Conclusion: 物理非理想性（floorsweeping、功耗约束）是kernel性能分析与优化中不可忽视的因素，跨GPU与加速器均具有普遍意义。

Abstract: Physical non-idealities in integrated circuit (IC) design and fabrication are concealed by conventional hardware abstractions, yet they can limit kernel performance and obscure optimization opportunities. Using distributed shared memory (DSMEM) on NVIDIA H100 GPUs as a case study, we show how floorsweeping restricts hardware resources available for clustered execution, potentially offsetting the benefits of reduced data movement enabled by DSMEM. Guided by these insights, we develop a wave-aware clustering strategy that accounts for these resource constraints to improve kernel performance. Evaluations using a global-load-and-broadcast microbenchmark, GEMM, and the Hopper version of FlashAttention 4 demonstrate the performance implications of these constraints, with wave-aware clustering improving FlashAttention 4 throughput by up to 5.30% over its baseline. We further show that, under thermal design power (TDP) limits, reduced data movement enables higher Streaming Multiprocessor (SM) frequencies through Dynamic Voltage and Frequency Scaling (DVFS), explaining otherwise unexpected throughput improvements. Extending this analysis beyond GPUs, we show that power constraints also limit throughput scaling on Tenstorrent's Wormhole accelerator, highlighting the broader importance of accounting for physical non-idealities when analyzing and optimizing kernel performance.

</details>


### [199] [SLICE-CiM: Selective Layer-wise Inference Control Engine with Confidence-Based Early Exit for SRAM based Digital Compute-in-Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37601)
*Pratham Sharma,Mohd Faisal Khan,Santosh Kumar Vishvakarma*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出四个可综合的SRAM存内计算外围控制模块，解决精度缩放中的三类低效问题，在TSMC 65nm实现并在MobileNetV2上实现31.35→454.91 TOPS/W的能量效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字SRAM CIM宏可消除片外权重传输以实现高能效CNN推理，但外围控制逻辑引入三类低效：降精度时位切片过度激活（L1）、不必要的累加深度（L2）、精度切换时的空转停顿（L3）。

Method: 设计四个可综合外围模块协同优化开关活动、字线开销与漏电：Flush-Merge Enable（FME）控制器、基于置信度的早退（EE）模块、带解码器的精度寄存器（PR/PD）、位串行周期控制器（CC）。TSMC 65nm CMOS，335 MHz综合与PnR后评估，门级VCD功耗分析，并以MobileNetV2（CIFAR-100）做系统级评测。

Result: 外围框架面积1769.760 μm²（综合后）/1982.131 μm²（PnR后）；漏电6.0706 μW，动态功耗288.2902 μW（综合后）/322.8850 μW（PnR后），合计328.9556 μW，占系统功耗0.2402%。EE模块占动态功耗58.2%，WLG占面积58.1%。系统效率由31.35 TOPS/W（8b/8b, 1.2V）提升至454.91 TOPS/W（2b/8b, 0.8V），提升13.33倍。

Conclusion: 该外围框架在无需修改位单元或重训练的前提下实现显著能效提升，面向利用稀疏性的CNN负载；Transformer扩展为未来工作。

Abstract: Digital SRAM compute-in-memory (CIM) macros enable energy-efficient CNN inference by eliminating off-chip weight transfers; however, peripheral control logic introduces three inefficiencies: bit- slice over-activation at reduced precision (L1), unnecessary accumula- tion depth (L2), and idle precision-transition stalls (L3). To address these, we present four synthesizable peripheral blocks: a Flush-Merge Enable (FME) controller, a Confidence-Based Early-Exit (EE) module, a Precision Register with Decoder (PR/PD), and a Bit-Serial Cycle Controller (CC), co-designed to minimize switching activity, wordline overhead, and leakage. Implemented in TSMC 65-nm CMOS at 335 MHz, the peripheral framework occupies 1769.760 μm2 post-synthesis and 1982.131 μm2 post-PnR. Gate-level VCD analysis reports 6.0706 μW leakage and 288.2902 μW (post-synthesis) / 322.8850 μW (post-PnR) dynamic power, totaling 328.9556 μW (0.2402% of system power). The EE module dominates dynamic power (58.2%), while WLG dominates area (58.1%). System evaluation on MobileNetV2 (CIFAR-100) shows efficiency scaling from 31.35 TOPS/W (8b/8b, 1.2 V) to 454.91 TOPS/W (2b/8b, 0.8 V), achieving 13.33x improvement without bitcell modification or retraining. The framework targets CNN workloads exploiting sparsity, with Transformer extension as future work.

</details>


<div id='physics.optics'></div>

# physics.optics [[Back]](#toc)

### [200] [Theoretical study of high-order harmonic generation in solids based on deep-learning methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35991)
*Wen-Feng Huang,Jia-Qi Liu,Xue-Bin Bian*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 结合分子动力学与基于深度学习的紧束缚电子哈密顿量，构建了可处理大尺度非晶体系且接近第一性原理精度的固体高次谐波（HHG）计算框架，并揭示了大块Si中谐波阶数依赖的热响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固体HHG可提供紧凑相干的EUV辐射，但现有激光-晶体相互作用理论方法难以在从头算精度下处理大尺度非晶体系，计算成本过高。

Method: 将分子动力学与深度学习构建的紧束缚电子哈密顿量耦合，形成高效可扩展的激光-晶体相互作用求解方案；先用单层h-BN的HHG谱与现有方法对比验证精度，再用于研究大块Si的温度依赖HHG。

Result: 发现谐波阶数依赖的热响应：随温度升高低阶谐波增强、高阶谐波被抑制。前者源于体系位点间跃迁（hopping）的修正，后者主要受热效应引起的结构无序影响，且与实验观测一致。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了研究固体HHG的可扩展计算策略，并为晶格涨落与超快电子动力学之间的相互作用提供了新见解。

Abstract: High-order harmonic generation (HHG) in solids provides a compact and coherent EUV radiation, which has attracted considerable attention. Many theoretical methods for laser-crystal interactions are developed. However, an approach to treat large-scale amorphous systems with ab-initio accuracy is still lacking because of the enormous computational cost. Here, we incorporate molecular dynamics and tight-binding electron Hamiltonians based on deep-learning methods to solve the problem efficiently. Its accuracy is validated by comparing the HHG spectra of monolayer hexagonal boron nitride (h-BN) with those obtained using existing methods. Then we use it to study the temperature-dependent HHG in bulk Si. We uncover a distinct harmonic-order-dependent thermal response: low-order harmonics are enhanced with increasing temperature, whereas high-order harmonics are suppressed. The former is caused by the modification of the inter-site hopping in the system, while the latter is mainly influenced by the structural disorder induced by the thermal effects, in agreement with experimental observations. This work establishes a scalable computational strategy for investigating HHG in solids and provides new insights into the interplay between lattice fluctuations and ultrafast electron dynamics.

</details>


### [201] [Parameter-Decoupled Superresolution with Airy-Disk-Informed Learning: Application to NGC 4486B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36021)
*James Qu,Isabella L. Lee,Gang Zhang,Abdelali Sajia,Yashashree Jadhav,Xiao-Feng Qian*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 论文通过Hermite–Gaussian模式投影证明：在同纵向宇称模式下，模式概率比对亮度、相干度与相位等干扰参数精确消去，从而实现双源角距的无干扰反演；并据此构建Airy先验CNN，在合成与HST实测图像上获得高精度分离估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从强度图像中估计两个未分辨或近未分辨源的间距时，源亮度、相互相干性与相位通常未知，且实际点扩散函数（PSF）偏离高斯形（如圆孔Airy斑），导致传统高斯近似估计精度受限。

Method: 1) 对一维与二维Airy振幅做Hermite–Gaussian（HG）投影，证明在对称双源模型下，纵向宇称相同的模式概率之比可精确消去亮度、相干与相位干扰项；2) 对一维Airy切片给出显式的多比值分离反演；3) 对二维圆孔PSF推导有限光瞳重叠积分的解析表达，并证明分离响应局部可逆；4) 基于上述解析不变量，设计“Airy先验”卷积神经网络，在合成Airy图像上训练。

Result: 在15万张训练图像下，Airy先验CNN的平均绝对误差约为0.018（作图分离单位），比非Airy高斯PSF近似的0.18提升约10倍；将该网络用于NGC 4486B双核的HST档案图像，在10万–15万张训练集下给出约9.6–12.0 pc的分离估计，与历史约12 pc的结果大体一致。

Conclusion: 论文明确区分了“光学模式干扰项的精确消去”与“仅依赖图像的学习型估计器的经验鲁棒性”，并指出PSF失配与延展星系结构是该方法的主要误差来源与适用边界。

Abstract: Estimating the separation of two unresolved or nearly unresolved sources from recorded intensity images is difficult when source brightness, mutual coherence, and phase are unknown and the point-spread function (PSF) departs from a Gaussian. We formulate Hermite--Gaussian (HG) projections of one- and two-dimensional Airy amplitudes and prove that ratios of mode probabilities with equal longitudinal parity exactly cancel the brightness, coherence, and phase nuisance parameters under a symmetric two-source model. For a one-dimensional Airy slice we achieve an explicit multi-ratio separation inversion. For the physical two-dimensional circular-pupil PSF we obtain exact finite-pupil overlap integrals and locally invertible separation responses. Motivated by these analytical invariants, we examine Airy-informed convolutional neural networks (CNNs) on synthetic Airy images with known separations. At 150,000 training images, the approximate mean absolute errors are 0.018 in plotted separation units, 10 times better comparing to 0.18 for non-Airy Gaussian PSF approximation. Applied to an archival Hubble Space Telescope image of the double nucleus of NGC 4486B, the Airy-informed CNN gives estimates of approximately 9.6--12.0 pc for training sets of 100,000--150,000 images, broadly consistent with the historical approximately 12 pc value. We distinguish exact optical-mode nuisance cancellation from empirical robustness of an image-only learned estimator and identify the limits imposed by PSF mismatch and extended galaxy structure.

</details>


### [202] [High-Efficiency Graphene Oxide Moiré Lenses Enabled by Cyclic Self-Assembly and Laser Nanoprinting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36373)
*Guiyuan Cao,Xinyuan Du,Han Lin,Baohua Jia,Shibiao Wei*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出循环无掩模制备策略，将GO-PDDA层层自组装与飞秒激光纳米打印结合，制备六相位级GO莫尔透镜，实现约10–90 mm连续变焦及1X–9X全彩成像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑型连续变焦光学系统在机器视觉、生物医学成像与小型相机中需求强烈；旋转莫尔透镜提供平面化变焦途径，但超表面实现依赖复杂纳米加工，难以大面积生产。

Method: 循环式无掩模工艺：逐层自组装二维氧化石墨烯（GO）与聚二烯丙基二甲基氯化铵（PDDA），配合飞秒激光空间选择性烧蚀；重复组装-烧蚀实现原子级厚度控制与多级相位轮廓构建，降低相位量化误差以提升聚焦效率。

Result: 单个GO透镜聚焦效率由二级的22.4%提升至六级的54.8%；六级调制GO莫尔透镜实现约10–90 mm连续焦距调谐，并实验演示1X–9X放大倍率的全彩成像。

Conclusion: 该循环无掩模策略可实现高效多级GO平面光学元件，展示了其在紧凑、可重构成像中的潜力。

Abstract: Compact optical systems with continuously tunable focal lengths are highly desirable for machine vision, biomedical imaging, and miniaturized cameras. Rotational Moiré lenses offer a planar approach to focal-length tuning, but metasurface-based implementations typically require complex nanofabrication processes that complicate large-area production. Here, we present a cyclic, mask-free fabrication strategy that combines layer-by-layer self-assembly of two-dimensional graphene oxide (GO) and poly(diallyldimethylammonium chloride) (PDDA) with femtosecond laser nanoprinting. Repeated GO-PDDA assembly and spatially selective ablation enable atomic-scale thickness control and the construction of precise multilevel phase profiles, reducing phase-quantization errors and improving focusing efficiency. The measured focusing efficiencies of individual GO lenses increase from 22.4% for two phase levels to 54.8% for six levels. Using this six-level modulation strategy, the fabricated GO Moiré lens achieves continuous focal-length tuning from approximately 10 to 90 mm. Full-colour imaging with magnifications ranging from 1X to 9X is experimentally demonstrated. These results establish a cyclic, mask-free fabrication strategy for efficient multilevel GO flat optics and demonstrate its potential for compact, reconfigurable imaging.

</details>


### [203] [Optically gated transduction enables silicon electrical readout of mid-infrared light](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36475)
*Shutao Zhang,Jiaqing Shen,Chongwu Wang,Jiahui Xu,Wei Chen,Qing Yang Steve Wu,Yan Liu,Xie Yuen Gok,Febiana Tjiptoharsono,Xiaogang Liu,Qi Jie Wang,Joel K. W. Yang,Jinfeng Zhu,Zhaogang Dong*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出"光门控转导"探测策略：MIR光子不直接激发载流子，而是调控Nd³⁺预置激发态，经Si肖特基结读出电信号，在硅平台上实现6.8 μm室温零偏探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅带隙1.1 eV无法直接吸收中红外光子，现有方案依赖亚带隙吸收增强或引入外置红外吸收材料，性能与集成性受限，需要一种不依赖硅红外吸收的探测新范式。

Method: 将Nd³⁺掺杂镧系纳米颗粒与逆向设计的Al/p-Si超构表面集成，该超构表面支持双共振，同时增强近红外光门控并共振耦合入射MIR；MIR照明重排Nd³⁺发光通道，产生光学态变化，再由硅肖特基结转换为电信号。

Result: 实现6.8 μm室温零偏探测，调制响应度103 μA/W；门控与MIR组合输入可分辨为三种可电学区分的电流态。

Conclusion: 该工作不依赖扩展硅的红外吸收，而是以门控光学转导在硅平台上读取MIR信息，为集成红外传感、信号处理与可编程光子系统提供新路径。

Abstract: Silicon is the dominant platform for integrated photonics, yet its wide electronic bandgap of 1.1 eV fundamentally prevents efficient detection of mid-infrared (MIR) photons through direct valence band to conduction band excitation. Existing approaches therefore seek to enhance sub-bandgap absorption or introduce external infrared absorbers. Here we demonstrate a profoundly different detection strategy based on optically gated transduction, in which MIR photons modulate a pre-established excitation state rather than directly generating photocarriers. Our device integrates Nd3+-doped lanthanide nanoparticles with an inverse-designed Al/p-Si metasurface supporting dual resonances that simultaneously enhances optical gating in the near infrared and resonantly couples incident MIR radiation. The MIR illumination redistributes the Nd3+ emission pathways, producing an optical-state change that is converted into an electrical signal through a silicon Schottky junction. The device achieves room-temperature zero-bias detection at 6.8 um with a modulation responsivity of 103 uA/W and resolves combined gate and MIR inputs into three electrically distinguishable current states. Rather than extending silicon absorption into the infrared, this work establishes gated optical transduction as a route for reading MIR optical information on a silicon platform, opening opportunities for integrated infrared sensing, signal processing and programmable photonic systems.

</details>


### [204] [Wavefunction-templated metamaterials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37248)
*Yutao Chen,Tao Xiao,Jing Li,Aodong Li,Biye Xie*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出"波函数模板化超材料"（wavefunction-templated metamaterials），通过逆向设计稀疏哈密顿量并做厄米化与谱重塑，可在物理可实现的超材料中嵌入任意指定、谱隔离的本征态；所需非局域性仅由单接触边界格点决定，与态内部及晶格尺寸无关，并在离散晶格与声学超材料中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统超材料的本征模空间分布由其预先给定的结构（哈密顿量）决定，无法自由指定目标本征态的空间波形；要实现特定功能往往需反复试错。作者希望让"目标波函数"成为设计输入，构造能承载多样化指定本征态的超材料。

Method: 1) 以目标波函数为模板反推哈密顿量几何，得到稀疏（局域性受限）结构，并证明其服从"边界律"：所需非局域连接仅涉及单接触边界格点，与态内部及晶格尺寸无关；2) 对稀疏哈密顿量进行厄米化（Hermitianization）；3) 进行谱重塑（spectral reshaping），在目标本征态周围打开保护性带隙以隔离该态；4) 在离散晶格与声学超材料平台中数值/实验验证。

Result: 所构造的哈密顿量保持稀疏且物理可实现，且非局域性限定在单接触边界位点；经厄米化与谱重塑后，目标本征态获得保护性带隙、实现谱隔离；在离散晶格与声学超材料中均成功验证了指定本征态的嵌入。

Conclusion: 波函数可作为超材料设计的直接输入，且所需代价（非局域连接）仅局限于边界，具有良好的可扩展性。该方法虽在声学中演示，但其原理可推广至热学、电路与光子超材料，为按需定制本征模功能器件提供通用框架。

Abstract: Metamaterials, which store, guide and emit waves through their eigenmodes, have enabled the development of devices with unprecedented functionalities. Yet the spatial profiles of conventional metamaterials' eigenmodes are typically determined by their pre-determined structures (Hamiltonians). In this work, we introduce `wavefunction-templated metamaterials', a class of physically realizable metamaterials hosting diverse prescribed, spectrally isolated eigenstates. The resulting Hamiltonian geometries obey a boundary law, with the required nonlocality determined solely by single-contact boundary sites, independent of the state interior or lattice size. The sparse Hamiltonians are then Hermitianized, followed by spectral reshaping to open a protective gap around the target eigenstate. We validate our approach in discrete lattices and acoustic metamaterials. Although demonstrated in acoustics, the principle can be applied to thermal, electrical-circuit and photonic metamaterials.

</details>


### [205] [Catastrophes in Reconnections of Optical Vortex Rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37615)
*Zhamila Kulchukova,Albert Ferrando,Anton S. Desyatnikov*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文从突变论角度理论研究椭圆高斯光束中光学涡旋环的拓扑位形，识别出尖点突变与蝴蝶突变两类，指出在其附近椭圆形变的微小改变即可引发涡旋环的复杂拓扑变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数情况下参数微变仅引起结果微变（如双光束叠加形成的涡旋环随相位略变而轻微收缩），但动力系统也可能因微小参数扰动发生涡旋环重连、成核或坍缩为点等剧烈变化。此类现象正属突变论研究范畴，而椭圆高斯光束中涡旋环拓扑位形的突变机制尚缺乏理论刻画。

Method: 基于突变论与分支理论，对椭圆高斯光束中光学涡旋环的拓扑位形进行理论分析，考察势函数退化临界点，并定位对应特定光束参数的突变点类型。

Result: 识别出两类突变：尖点突变（cusp）与蝴蝶突变（butterfly）。在其附近，椭圆形变量（elliptic deformation）的极小改变即导致涡旋环发生复杂的拓扑变换。

Conclusion: 椭圆高斯光束中涡旋环的拓扑结构在特定参数处呈突变行为，可用突变论加以分类与预测；尖点与蝴蝶突变为微小形变触发涡旋环剧烈重排提供了理论框架。

Abstract: A small change in parameters leads to a small change in the outcomes, in most cases. For instance, an optical vortex ring, created in the superposition of two laser beams, can slightly shrink if the phase changes minutely. However, dynamical systems can also experience significant changes due to small perturbations in parameters; the vortex rings can reconnect, nucleate or collapse into a point. Parameters that cause such striking outcomes are at the focal point of catastrophe theory - a branch of bifurcation theory that studies degenerate critical points of a potential function. We present theoretical study of topological configurations of optical vortex rings in elliptic Gaussian beams, which change dramatically at specific beam parameters. We identify two types of catastrophes, cusp and butterfly, near which very small changes of elliptic deformations lead to complex topological transformations of vortex rings.

</details>


<div id='eess.SP'></div>

# eess.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [206] [Acceleration data-based robust on-board monitoring of hunting proximity in railway vehicles under uncertainty: A feasibility study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36122)
*Kyriakos Kritikakos,Spilios D. Fassois,John S. Sakellariou*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于灰色非线性车辆模型与最大Lyapunov指数(MLE)的列车蛇行失稳接近程度车载实时分类方法，将蛇行接近度划分为若干速度接近区间，蒙特卡洛仿真分类准确率达97.08%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在车辆/轨道及其他运行条件带来的不确定性下，评估车载实时蛇行失稳接近程度评估的可行性，并克服传统方法在该场景下性能不足的问题。

Method: 构建参数由车载加速度测量估计的灰色非线性车辆模型；以基于模型的MLE估计作为蛇行接近度量；将蛇行接近度分类为预设速度接近区间；用基于MATLAB的多体车辆模型和四种运行工况进行蒙特卡洛仿真验证。

Result: 分类准确率达97.08%，显著优于潜在替代方案；对故障悬挂和空心磨耗车轮具有鲁棒性；在磨耗轨道条件下出现一定性能折衷。

Conclusion: 车载实时蛇行接近程度分类方法可行且性能优异、鲁棒性良好，但在磨耗轨道工况下仍需权衡性能。

Abstract: The feasibility of on-board, real-time proximity assessment to railway hunting initiation under uncertainty arising from vehicle/track and other operational conditions is assessed. In this context, a novel method that classifies hunting proximity into one of several prespecified speed proximity intervals is introduced, based on a grey nonlinear vehicle model with parameters estimated from on-board acceleration measurements and model-based estimation of the Maximum Lyapunov Exponent (MLE) as a hunting proximity measure. The method's performance and robustness are assessed via Monte Carlo simulations using a MATLAB-based multi-body vehicle model and four operating scenarios. The method is shown to achieve excellent performance achieving 97.08 % classification accuracy, which marks a significant improvement over a potential alternative. Its robustness to faulty suspensions and hollow-worn wheels is also demonstrated, although certain performance compromise is observed under worn-track conditions.

</details>


### [207] [MtReNIRS: A Self-Supervised Foundation Model for Transferable Neurovascular Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36159)
*Tony Chae,Kleanthis Avramidis,Dimitra Emmanouilidou,Ivan J. Tashev*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出蒙太奇感知掩码自编码器基础模型 MtReNIRS，在12个公开fNIRS数据集上预训练，学到可跨被试、任务、设备与蒙太奇迁移的神经血管表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有fNIRS建模多局限于小规模、单session、低采样率数据，且采集设备与光极蒙太奇高度异质；fNIRS信号的复杂时空结构进一步限制模型的可扩展性与跨设置迁移能力。因此尚不清楚单一fNIRS编码器能否学到跨被试、任务、设备与蒙太奇的通用表征。

Method: 构建蒙太奇感知的掩码自编码器（montage-aware masked autoencoder），将异质蒙太奇映射到共享解剖参考空间，从而联合编码fNIRS缓慢的血流动力学特性、发色团身份与皮层位置；在12个公开数据集（268名被试、567小时记录）上预训练。

Result: 在三个外部数据集上实现迁移；跨被试性能与全监督基线相当或更优；在少样本被试内校准中优于此前SOTA fNIRS模型；对稀疏蒙太奇保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: 蒙太奇感知自监督能产生具生理依据、可迁移的fNIRS表征，为标签高效脑机接口提供可扩展基础。
