Abstract: Functional near-infrared spectroscopy (fNIRS) offers a promising approach for monitoring the hemodynamic correlates of neural activity in naturalistic settings. However, existing modeling methods for fNIRS are largely confined to small-scale, single-session data with low sampling rates, driven by high heterogeneity in acquisition devices and optode montages. In addition, the complex spatiotemporal structure of fNIRS signals further restricts model scalability and transferability across settings. As a result, it remains unclear whether a single fNIRS encoder can learn representations that are usable across subjects, tasks, devices, and montages. Here, we show that a montage-aware masked autoencoder, pretrained on 12 public fNIRS datasets spanning 268 subjects and 567 recording hours, learns generalizable neurovascular representations that transfer across three external datasets. Our fNIRS foundation model, MtReNIRS, jointly encodes the slow hemodynamic nature of fNIRS, chromophore identity and cortical location by mapping heterogeneous montages onto a shared anatomical reference. MtReNIRS achieves cross-subject performance on par or better than fully supervised baselines and excels in few-shot within-subject calibration, where it outperforms prior state-of-the-art fNIRS models, while remaining robust to sparse montages. Our results indicate that montage-aware self-supervision yields physiologically grounded, transferable fNIRS representations, providing a scalable foundation for label-efficient brain-computer interfaces.

</details>


### [208] [Soft Temporal Alignment Distillation for Camera-to-mmWave Radar Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36637)
*Jiuqi Yan,Chengbo Zhu,Chengdong Xu,Qisong Wu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出跨模态知识蒸馏框架，用基于相机2D人体关键点的教师模型监督纯雷达学生模型，通过软时间对齐蒸馏解决时间戳不对应的特征迁移问题，在MM-Fi与mmO-HAR上显著提升雷达HAR精度并保持隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波雷达HAR具隐私保护优势，但点云稀疏，难以表达人体结构与运动。现有跨模态蒸馏多假设教师与学生时间步一一对应，而学生将雷达序列压缩为少量片段，固定时间戳对应不再适用。

Method: 教师模型在相机2D关键点上训练，保留每个时间步的上下文化状态；学生模型仅用雷达，由PointNet点集编码、双向时序建模、片段级聚合和轻量共享潜在通道缩放构成。提出Soft Temporal Alignment Distillation：每个学生片段自适应检索并聚合教师时序证据作为蒸馏目标，对齐指以学生为条件的表征空间匹配，而非时间顺序或单调配准。训练后移除教师与对齐分支。

Result: MM-Fi上雷达单模态精度由87.04%提升至89.17%，在所评估的经典与近期蒸馏基线中平均精度最高；在多数点/帧丢弃设置下平均精度与macro-F1均优于雷达学生基线。迁移至自采mmO-HAR数据集，精度由80.33%提升至87.75%。

Conclusion: 软时间对齐蒸馏有效实现跨模态特征迁移，蒸馏后的雷达表征具可迁移性，且推理阶段仅需雷达、无需额外传感与计算分支，满足隐私保护要求。

Abstract: Millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar provides privacy?preserving human activity recognition (HAR), but sparse point clouds limit its representation of body structure and motion. We propose a cross-modal knowledge distillation framework in which a teacher trained on camera-derived two-dimensional (2D) human keypoints supervises a radar-only student. The stu?dent combines PointNet-based point-set encoding, bidirectional temporal modeling, segment-level aggregation, and lightweight shared latent-channel scaling. The teacher retains contextualized states at every time step, whereas the student summarizes the radar sequence into a small number of segments, making fixed timestamp correspondence unsuitable for feature transfer. Soft Temporal Alignment Distillation lets each student segment adaptively retrieve and aggregate teacher temporal evidence into its distillation target. Alignment denotes student-conditioned representation-space matching rather than chronological or monotonic registration. On the Multi-Modal Non-Intrusive 4D Human Dataset (MM-Fi), the method improves radar-only ac?curacy from 87.04% to 89.17%, achieving the highest mean accuracy among the evaluated established and recent distillation baselines. It also obtains higher mean accuracy and macro?averaged F1 score (macro-F1) than the radar student under most evaluated point- and frame-dropout settings. Adaptation to our independently collected millimeter-wave Office Human Ac?tivity Recognition Dataset (mmO-HAR) improves accuracy from 80.33% to 87.75%, supporting the transferability of the distilled radar representation. The teacher and alignment branch are removed after training, enabling privacy-preserving radar-only inference without additional sensing or computational branches.

</details>


### [209] [Static Multipath Suppression in Power Delay Profile of Time-Varying Channel at 28 GHz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36792)
*Jarosław Wojtuń,Bartosz Czaja,Tomasz Grasza,Jan M. Kelner,Cezary Ziółkowski,Radek Zavorka,Josef Vychodil,Tomas Mikulasek,Jiri Blumenstein,Ales Prokes,Aniruddha Chandra*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文基于28 GHz宽带测量，在动态道路环境中比较两种时域差分滤波器与三种多普勒域陷波配置对功率延迟谱（PDP）及RMS时延扩展的静态多径抑制效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时变信道中静态多径（如固定散射体回波）会掩盖车辆相关动态反射，需评估不同静态多径抑制方法的适用性与滤波选择的影响。

Method: 在28 GHz动态道路场景开展宽带测量；对比两种时间差分滤波器与三种多普勒域陷波滤波配置，分析其对PDP和RMS时延扩展分布的影响。

Result: 所有方法均能有效抑制静止多径成分，提升车辆相关反射的可辨识度；但滤波器频率响应的差异会改变动态多径成分的相对功率及RMS时延扩展分布。

Conclusion: 静态多径抑制及滤波器选择对时变传播信道表征具有重要影响，应根据目标动态成分特性谨慎选取滤波方案。

Abstract: This paper investigates static multipath suppression in a time-varying radio channel at 28 GHz using wideband measurements conducted in a dynamic road environment. Two temporal differencing filters and three Doppler-domain notch filtering configurations are compared in terms of their effects on power delay profiles (PDPs) and root mean square (RMS) delay spread. The results demonstrate that all investigated methods effectively suppress stationary multipath components, improving the visibility of vehicle-related reflections. However, differences in filter frequency responses influence the relative powers of dynamic multipath components and the resulting RMS delay spread distributions. The findings highlight the importance of static multipath suppression and filter selection in characterizing time-varying propagation channels.

</details>


### [210] [Spatial MIMO Channel Rendering: A 3D Gaussian Splatting Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36880)
*Juntaek Han,Geonha Kim,Chaozheng Wen,Ahmed Alkhateeb,Jun Zhang,Jeonghun Park*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文将视觉中的3D高斯泼溅引入MIMO信道，提出MIMO-GS：用收发3D高斯基元对表示信道场景，投影到波束域进行beam pair splatting，从稀疏观测渲染未观测用户位置的长时波束对功率图，渲染比无标定复刻的射线追踪快5.5倍，用于波束管理时净和速率达genie基准的98%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MIMO增益依赖CSI，而每个相干区间内每个用户都需消耗导频与反馈资源获取CSI；但塑造信道的传播环境是长期不变的，利用该先验可大幅降低CSI获取开销。为此需解决空间MIMO信道渲染问题：从稀疏观测学习潜在波束域信道场景，并渲染未观测用户位置的长时收发波束对功率图。

Method: 受视觉3D高斯泼溅启发，提出MIMO-GS：以成对的接收与发射3D高斯基元表示场景，将基元分别从用户侧和基站侧投影到对应波束域，并通过beam pair splatting累积波束关联，从而渲染波束对功率图。

Result: 在仿真城市场景与实测室内信道上，MIMO-GS渲染速度比未标定复刻的射线追踪快5.5倍；应用于多用户波束管理时，可在无需探测（sounding）的情况下调度用户与发送波束并计入用户间干扰，净和速率达genie-aided基准的98%，较基于位置的预测器高19%。

Conclusion: 信道场景的长期一致性可被显式建模并复用，MIMO-GS以低成本稀疏观测实现高效信道渲染，在波束管理中显著降低CSI获取开销并逼近理想基准性能。

Abstract: The achievable gains of multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) systems depend on channel state information (CSI), whose acquisition consumes pilot and feedback resources for every user in every coherence interval. Meanwhile, the propagation environment that shapes the channel persists. Exploiting such information will help to greatly reduce the overhead of CSI acquisition. This paper investigates the spatial MIMO channel rendering problem, which learns a latent beamspace channel scene from sparse observations and renders the long-term transmit and receive beam pair power map at unobserved user locations. Motivated by 3D Gaussian splatting in visual scene rendering, the proposed MIMO Gaussian splatting (MIMO-GS) represents the scene with pairs of receive and transmit 3D Gaussians, projects them from the user and base station onto the corresponding beam domains, and accumulates the beam associations through beam pair splatting. On a simulated urban scenario and measured indoor channels, MIMO-GS renders 5.5 times faster than ray tracing without a calibrated replica. Applied to multiuser beam management, the rendered maps allow users and transmit beams to be scheduled without sounding while accounting for the inter-user interference, and the net sum rate reaches 98% of the genie-aided benchmark, 19% above a position-based predictor.

</details>


### [211] [Closed-Form Subspace Loss and Snapshot-Limited Dimension Selection for Beamspace MVDR Radar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37089)
*Moris S. Mukwayi,Michael A. van Wyk,Andre M. McDonald*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该文给出DFT波束窗在点干扰下的子空间损失闭式表达，结合SMI的scaled-Beta律得到无加载SMI的最优窗长B*(K)选取规则，并在32×32阵列仿真中验证其精确性与有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波束域MVDR仅用B个波束替代N阵元，存在子空间损失（随B减小）与协方差估计损失（随B增大）的权衡；固定训练快拍数K时缺乏选取合适窗长B的定量依据。

Method: 推导DFT波束窗在点干扰下子空间损失的闭式解：对任意干扰功率精确成立，并借助阵列Dirichlet核给出高INR极限形式；再与固定变换下SMI的scaled-Beta律结合，导出无加载SMI的最优窗长B*(K)。

Result: 在32×32阵列、4–8 GHz、噪声干扰与Billingsley地杂波仿真中，精确式在25/49波束窗、10–50 dB干扰电平下与clairvoyant子空间损失相差0.04 dB以内；9波束窗（三列被三干扰占据）因忽略杂波损失可达5 dB；名义K下B*(K)在全部30个多窗可用情形中选出最低损失窗；干扰方向0.25 bin rms误差仅带来平均0.04 dB损失；对角加载时规则选较大窗处最优窗更大。

Conclusion: 该窗长选择规则是一个oracle（需干扰参数或真实协方差），但精度高、对方向误差稳健，可作为波束域MVDR窗长设计的解析基准。

Abstract: A beamspace minimum variance distortionless response (MVDR) processor adapts a window of B beams instead of all N elements of a large array, trading a subspace loss that falls with B against a covariance estimation loss that rises with B for a fixed number K of training snapshots. We give the subspace loss of a DFT beam window in point jamming in closed form: exactly for any jammer power, and in the high interference-to-noise ratio (INR) limit through the Dirichlet kernel of the array. Combined with the known scaled-Beta law of sample matrix inversion (SMI) with a fixed transform, it gives a window size B*(K) for unloaded SMI. On a simulated 32x32 array with carriers from 4 to 8 GHz, noise jammers and Billingsley land clutter, the exact form is within 0.04 dB of the clairvoyant subspace loss for 25- and 49-beam windows at jammer levels from 10 to 50 dB. For a 9-beam window whose three columns are taken by three jammers near the target's elevation, the clutter that the form leaves out costs up to 5 dB. With the nominal K, B*(K) picks the lowest-loss window in all 30 cases with more than one runnable window. The rule is an oracle: it needs the jammer parameters or the true covariance, and errors of 0.25 bin rms in the jammer directions add a mean loss of 0.04 dB. With diagonal loading the best window was larger still wherever the rule chose more than the smallest window.

</details>


### [212] [Robust PPG-Exclusive Heart Rate Estimation During Maximal Exercise Using Population-Based Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37132)
*Elisabetta De Giovanni,Iñigo Urteaga,Matteo Croci,Tomas Teijeiro*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该研究提出仅依赖PPG信号、利用心率动态先验与DTW对齐的贝叶斯式框架，在高强度运动中显著提升心率估计可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPG是可穿戴心率监测的主流技术，但剧烈运动时的运动伪影与心电信号频谱重叠，导致可靠性下降；作者希望在不使用辅助传感器（如加速度计）的前提下，仅凭PPG信号与心率动态先验获得可靠HR估计。

Method: 采集21名受试者最大功率自行车测试中的同步PPG与ECG信号；采用留一受试者交叉验证（LOSO），以ECG真值构建HR演化先验；用动态时间规整（DTW）对齐归一化HR曲线并估计barycenter平均；将PPG功率谱密度（PSD）与先验分布结合生成"后验PSD"，并在其中以启发式搜索识别、排序候选HR轨迹，选取最可能路径。

Result: DTW对齐在各受试者上均以最小误差对齐归一化HR曲线，验证了基于人群的先验具有稳健性；相比PPG+加速度计基准，该方法相对ECG真值的BPM平均绝对误差（MAE）中位数降低63.3%（IQR：47.3%–74.6%）。

Conclusion: 生理先验与基于DTW的对齐可使仅用PPG的心率监测在高强度运动中保持稳健，无需辅助传感器。

Abstract: PPG is the main technology for wearable heart rate (HR) monitoring. However, motion artifacts during intense physical activity reduce its reliability due to spectral overlap with the cardiac signal. This study investigates whether severely contaminated PPG signals can yield reliable HR estimates without auxiliary sensors by leveraging prior knowledge of HR dynamics. We acquired simultaneous PPG and ECG signals from 21 subjects during a maximal cycloergometer test. Using Leave-One-Subject-Out cross-validation, we built HR evolution priors from the ground-truth ECG and applied Dynamic Time Warping (DTW) to align normalized HR curves and estimate a barycenter average. DTW aligns normalized HR curves with minimal error across all subjects, validating robust population-based priors. For each test subject, we combined the PPG Power Spectral Density (PSD) with the prior distribution to generate a "posterior PSD". We identified and ranked candidate HR trajectories using heuristic search within the "posterior PSD" and selected the most likely path. Our approach reduced mean absolute error (MAE) in BPM relative to the ground-truth ECG by a median of 63.3% (IQR: 47.3%-74.6%) compared to a PPG+accelerometer benchmark. These results show that physiological priors and DTW-based alignment enable robust, PPG-exclusive HR monitoring during high-intensity exercise.

</details>


### [213] [Negative Results for Reciprocity-Based Over-the-Air Split Learning Under Channel Aging: Stability, Alignment, and Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37188)
*Tianhao Guo,Xianzhong Li,Hao Luo*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 通过七个案例研究，结合数值预测、机制检验与竞争性预测，系统检验了基于互易性的无线分体学习（over-the-air split learning）中信道老化引发的相位旋转/幅度衰减对梯度回传的影响：若干物理层面的局部关系成立，但多数面向训练效果的定量预测未获支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于互易性的 over-the-air 分体学习中，信道老化会使回传到发射端的导数信号发生相位旋转与幅度衰减，实践中常据此提出一系列"精度提升"预测（如截断反演优于极坐标校正、按最大接收功率选端口、逐子载波校正、球面波前补偿等）。这些预测是否真正成立缺乏系统检验，本文旨在区分哪些是可验证的局部物理关系、哪些是缺乏任务支撑的训练效果预报。

Method: 七个案例研究，方法上综合三类证据：数值预测（numerical forecasts）、机制检验（mechanism checks）与竞争性预测（competing predictions）。具体检验包括：相关性零点附近的 SGD 对比（截断反演 vs 极坐标校正，含配对 95% 置信区间与保留模态二次代理）、最大接收功率端口选择 vs 随机选择的五点对齐优势、幅度—相位梯度局部反比关系、七项干扰致精度崩溃预测、零均值干扰在固定线性链下条件均值不变性、逐子载波校正与球面波前补偿的增益、聚焦在单模模型与含 LoS+散射模型下的表现、非对称运动下的端口分配、定位误差预测，以及高斯抖动（σ=0.383 波长）与等幅固定偏置的对比。

Result: 相关性零点附近，截断反演的交叉熵比极坐标校正低 0.000813，差值配对 95% 区间为 [−0.001853, 0.000060]，且保留模态二次代理略偏向截断；最大功率端口选择未达预测的五点优势，两个测试间距下最多仅提升 0.97 点，但幅度—相位梯度的局部反比关系成立；七项干扰致崩溃预测全部被拒绝，零均值干扰在固定线性链下保持条件均值；逐子载波校正与球面波前补偿均未带来预测增益，聚焦在单模模型下两法接近随机水平、在 LoS+散射模型下无观测增益；非对称运动下的成功端口分配否证了严格对称性要求；竞争性定位误差预测获支持；σ=0.383 波长的高斯抖动获得高终端测试精度，而等幅固定偏置接近随机精度。

Conclusion: 多数基于局部物理直觉推出的训练效果定量预测不成立，仅局部物理关系（如幅度—相位梯度反比）与定位误差预测得到支持；抖动与偏置的巨大差异表明误差的统计结构而非幅值大小决定训练成败。借助任务支撑（task support）、噪声注入（noise injection）与更新矩（update moments），可将有效的局部物理关系与不受支持的训练预报清晰区分。

Abstract: In reciprocity-based over-the-air split learning, channel aging rotates and attenuates the derivative signal returned to the transmitter. Seven case studies combine numerical forecasts, mechanism checks, and competing predictions. Near the correlation zero, an archived SGD comparison finds truncated inversion 0.000813 lower in cross-entropy than polar correction, with a paired 95% interval for the difference from -0.001853 to 0.000060, and a retained-mode quadratic proxy slightly favors truncation. Selecting the return port with the largest received power falls short of the predicted five-point alignment advantage over random selection at both tested spacings, gaining at most 0.97 points, while the local inverse relation between amplitude and phase gradient is supported. Seven specified interference-induced accuracy-collapse predictions are rejected, and independent zero-mean interference preserves the conditional mean under a fixed linear chain. Per-subcarrier correction and spherical-wavefront compensation do not deliver their predicted accuracy gains. Focusing leaves both methods near chance in a single-mode model and adds no observed gain in a model with a line-of-sight component plus scattering. A successful port assignment under nonsymmetric motion refutes an exact-symmetry requirement. The competing positioning-error prediction is supported. Gaussian rail jitter with a standard deviation of 0.383 wavelengths yields high terminal test accuracy, whereas a fixed bias of the same magnitude yields near-chance accuracy. These cases distinguish valid local physical relations from unsupported training forecasts through task support, noise injection, and update moments.

</details>


### [214] [JEPA-Inspired Latent Field Prediction for Sparse-Pilot MIMO-OFDM Channel Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37391)
*Yuzhi Yang,Brahim Mefgouda,Hang Zou,Lina Bariah,Anis Bara,Yuhuan Lu,Hao Zhang,Merouane Debbah*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出受JEPA启发的"场世界模型"——在潜空间对无线传播场建模与预测，以潜先验融合稀疏导频重建MIMO-OFDM完整CSI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全CSI获取对MIMO-OFDM至关重要，但密集导频开销大；直接对原始CSI系数最小化预测误差对相位敏感、易受小尺度衰落、移动性、局部散射和空间微扰影响而脆弱。而信道在空间、时间、频率维存在强相关，可在潜传播场域中获得更可预测的表示。

Method: 设计联合嵌入预测架构(JEPA)启发的潜空间场世界模型：将CSI观测映射为潜时频token；由历史多频段上下文预测被掩码的未来/缺失潜场状态；再将预测的潜先验与当前稀疏含噪导频融合，重建完整MIMO-OFDM信道。

Result: 射线追踪仿真表明，相比仅用导频及直接重建的基线，所提方法在NMSE上取得改进。

Conclusion: 潜场预测可作为稀疏导频信道获取的有效先验，验证了在潜传播场域建模优于直接对原始CSI做预测的思路。

Abstract: Accurate acquisition of full channel state information (CSI) is essential for multiple-input multiple-output orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (MIMO-OFDM) wireless systems, while dense pilot-based estimation incurs substantial overhead. Directly minimizing the prediction error over raw CSI coefficients is fragile in realistic propagation environments, since small-scale fading, mobility, local scattering, and small spatial perturbations can induce large phase-sensitive variations. Nevertheless, wireless channels contain strong spatial, temporal, and frequency-domain correlations that can be more predictably represented in a latent propagation-field domain. Motivated by this observation, we propose a joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA)-inspired field world model, defined as a latent predictive model of the wireless propagation field, for sparse-pilot channel reconstruction. The model maps CSI observations into latent time-frequency tokens, predicts masked or future latent field states from historical multi-band context, and fuses the predicted latent prior with sparse noisy current pilots to reconstruct the full MIMO-OFDM channel. Ray-tracing experiments show that the proposed method improves normalized mean square error (NMSE) over pilot-only and direct reconstruction baselines, supporting latent field prediction as an effective prior for sparse-pilot channel acquisition.

</details>


### [215] [Assessing BIOMASS Radiometric Stability using Permanent Scatterers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37400)
*Marco Manzoni,Francesco Salvaterra,Claudio Maria Prati,Stefano Tebaldini*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出基于永久散射体（PS）的机会参考标定框架 PSCal，用于监测 BIOMASS P 波段 SAR 的相对辐射稳定性，并在内罗毕与科尔多瓦数据上验证其可识别约 1 dB 的辐射异常及 0.1 dB 量级的残余增益变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BIOMASS 的相对辐射稳定性监测需要将仪器与处理效应同场景后向散射的时变变化分离；而依赖专用定标目标的方案成本高、布设受限，因此寻求无需专用定标器的机会式参考手段。

Method: PSCal 框架：基于幅度的散射体筛选，结合迭代最大似然增益估计；通过仿真评估在 BIOMASS 栈有限采集次数条件下的检测与估计性能，分析筛选阈值、散射体丰度与信噪比的影响；并应用于肯尼亚内罗毕与阿根廷科尔多瓦数据集。

Result: 仿真表明严格筛选阈值至关重要，估计精度依赖散射体数量与 SNR。内罗毕数据识别出约 1 dB 辐射异常，溯源至射频干扰抑制处理缺陷，其余采集增益变化多在 0.1 dB 内；科尔多瓦 12 次采集跨两个任务阶段（间隔约 8 个月），尽管场景后向散射与几何显著变化，残余增益变化仍约 0.1 dB；结果与澳大利亚 New Norcia 的 BIOMASS 定标转发器（BCT）测量一致。

Conclusion: 经严格筛选的 P 波段永久散射体可作为专用定标基础设施的有效补充，用于相对辐射监测与处理异常检测。

Abstract: Monitoring the relative radiometric stability of the European Space Agency's BIOMASS P-band synthetic aperture radar (SAR) requires separating instrumental and processing effects from temporal changes in the scene backscatter. This study investigates the use of permanent scatterers (PSs) as opportunistic references for estimating residual radiometric gains without dedicated calibration targets. The PS-based calibration (PSCal) framework combines amplitude-based scatterer selection with iterative maximum-likelihood gain estimation. Simulations assess detection and estimation performance under the limited number of acquisitions available in BIOMASS stacks, highlighting the importance of stringent selection thresholds and the dependence of estimation accuracy on scatterer abundance and signal-to-noise ratio. The approach is applied to datasets over Nairobi, Kenya, and Cordoba, Argentina. Over Nairobi, PSCal identifies an approximately 1 dB radiometric anomaly subsequently traced to a defect in radio-frequency interference mitigation, while the remaining acquisitions exhibit estimated gain variations generally within 0.1 dB. Over Cordoba, twelve acquisitions spanning two mission phases separated by approximately eight months yield residual gain variations on the order of 0.1 dB despite pronounced changes in the scene backscatter and acquisition geometry. Results were observed to be consistent with measurements from the BIOMASS Calibration Transponder (BCT) installed in New Norcia (Australia). These results demonstrate the potential of carefully selected P-band PSs to complement dedicated calibration infrastructure for relative radiometric monitoring and the detection of processing anomalies.

</details>


### [216] [5G Sensing for Human Pose Estimation with PRS-based CSI: An Experimental Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37435)
*Zhaorui Yin,Pietro Morri,Mattia Brambilla,Monica Nicoli*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 基于5G下行PRS提取的CSI，验证了无设备2D人体姿态估计的可行性；联合幅相输入优于仅幅度输入，PCK在10%/30%/50%阈值下分别为32.2%、72.9%、84.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索标准化的5G定位参考信号（PRS）所测信道状态信息能否用于无设备人体姿态估计，以摆脱对专用雷达或摄像头的依赖。

Method: gNB向配置双接收天线的UE发送下行5G PRS；用最小二乘（LS）估计CSI，并做幅度平滑与相位校准；以幅度、相位表征作为深度学习模型输入，在20 MHz带宽下对比仅幅度与联合幅度-相位训练。

Result: 联合幅相训练优于仅幅度方案，PCK在归一化距离阈值10%、30%、50%（以左右肩-髋距离归一化）下分别达到32.2%、72.9%、84.2%。

Conclusion: 即使采用简单的射频配置和较小的数据集，标准化5G下行PRS所获CSI仍包含可用于人体姿态估计的有效信息。

Abstract: This paper presents an experimental study of device- free 2D human pose estimation using Channel State Information (CSI) extracted from downlink Fifth Generation (5G) Positioning Reference Signal (PRS). The proposed system employs a Next Generation Node Base (gNB) transmitting PRS to a User Equipment (UE) equipped with two receive antennas to measure the radio channel. The CSI is estimated using a least-squares (LS) method, and processed through amplitude smoothing and phase calibration. The resulting amplitude and phase representations are used as inputs to a deep learning model for pose estimation. We compare amplitude-only input with joint amplitude-phase training at a bandwidth of 20 MHz. The joint training outperforms the amplitude-only solution and achieves Percentage of Correct Keypoints (PCK) equal to 32.2%, 72.9%, and 84.2% below 10%, 30%, and 50% of the distance between left shoulder and right hip, respectively. These results demonstrate that standardized downlink 5G PRS-based CSI contains useful information for human pose estimation, even with a simple radio configuration and a relatively small dataset.

</details>


### [217] [Coordinated and Uncoordinated Channel Tracking for High-Rate IoT in Distributed Massive MIMO With Low-Resolution ADCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37508)
*Hediyeh Soltanizadeh,Ardavan Rahimian*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对低分辨率ADC的分布式大规模MIMO IoT，提出量化感知的联合协同卡尔曼滤波（Q-JCKF）与分布式概率数据关联滤波（Q-DPDAF）用于动态信道跟踪，并给出闭式UatF性能表达式、复杂度与仿真验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速IoT场景中，分布式mMIMO需低分辨率ADC降功耗，但导频与数据活动解耦、导频复用及跨接入时隙的移动信道演化带来量化噪声与干扰耦合下的动态信道跟踪难题，现有JC-KF/PDAF未考虑量化效应。

Method: 对已知导频活动构建量化感知联合协同卡尔曼滤波Q-JCKF；对非协调接入构建量化感知分布式概率数据关联滤波Q-DPDAF；推导UatF闭式统计表达式、大规模N导频复用上限及跨AP关联项与O(epsilon)后验集中界；采用Kronecker结构降复杂度、分组化假设枚举由2^K降至2^{K_r}并支持AP本地并行处理。

Result: 仿真验证分析结论，量化ADC分辨率与导频接入效应；改善低尾谱效、移动跟踪鲁棒性；4比特接近理想性能；Q-DPDAF活动推断可靠；相比传统实现显著降低运行时间。

Conclusion: 该框架在理想ADC、共址极限下退化为JC-KF/PDAF，明确了有限比特量化导致的卡尔曼测量信息条件损失，并给出计算、存储与前传的扩展规律。

Abstract: This paper investigates dynamic channel tracking for high-rate Internet of things (IoT) in distributed massive multiple-input multiple-output (mMIMO) with low-resolution analog-to-digital converters (ADCs), where pilot and data activities are decoupled, pilots are reused, and mobile channels evolve across access slots. We hereby develop a quantization-aware joint coordinated Kalman filter (Q-JCKF) for known pilot activity and a quantization-aware distributed probabilistic data association filter (Q-DPDAF) for uncoordinated access. For uplink analysis, we derive a closed-form statistical use-and-then-forget (UatF) expression for Q-JCKF and mixture-aware, pilot-conditioned, and long-term statistical UatF formulations for Q-DPDAF, together with the large-N pilot-reuse ceiling and a cross-access point (AP) association term with an O(epsilon) posterior-concentration bound. The proposed framework recovers JC-KF/PDAF in the ideal-ADC co-located limit, and quantifies the conditional loss of Kalman measurement information caused by finite-resolution quantization. We further characterize computational, memory, and fronthaul scaling: Kronecker-structured Q-JCKF reduces covariance-processing complexity, while groupwise Q-DPDAF limits hypothesis enumeration from 2^K to 2^{K_r} and enables parallel AP-local processing. Simulations corroborate the analytical and complexity outcomes, quantify ADC-resolution and pilot-access effects, and demonstrate improved lower-tail spectral efficiency, robust mobility tracking, near-ideal 4-bit performance, reliable Q-DPDAF activity inference, and substantial runtime reductions over legacy implementations and processing frameworks.

</details>


### [218] [A Theory of Transmit-Receive-Manifold of Single-Photon Quantum-Dot Antenna Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37542)
*Angeru Pavan Kumar,Sandip Ghosal*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出面向量子点阵列的完整"发射信道—接收流形"模型，指出接收单光子的量子点阵列是集体耦合天线，其接收流形不必是平面波流形，并以经典阵列激励所得的参考目标方向图进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究通常默认量子点阵列的接收流形为平面波流形，但接收单光子的量子点阵列实为集体耦合天线，其接收流形不必然等同于平面波流形，因此需要建立完整的发射—信道—接收流形模型。

Method: 构建量子点阵列的完整发射信道—接收流形模型；引入可由经典阵列激励获得的参考目标方向图作为验证基准。

Result: 摘要未给出定量结果，仅说明以经典阵列激励得到的参考目标方向图完成模型验证。

Conclusion: 量子点阵列的接收流形不应简单等同于平面波流形，需在发射—信道—接收一体化框架下建模，并以经典阵列方向图为参照加以校验。

Abstract: A quantum-dot array that receives a single photon is a collectively coupled antenna, and its receive manifold is not always the plane-wave manifold. So, this work aims to develop a complete transmit-channel-receive manifold model for quantum dot (QD) arrays. For the sake of validation, it considers a reference target pattern which can be obtained using classical array excitation.

</details>
